However, reliable cooperation critically depends on inferring trust over both information sources and their reported hypotheses, particularly under partial observability and potential adversarial interference. Existing approaches frequently depend on pre-established trust relationships or side-channel information, which may not be available or reliable in realistic MRS deployments. This work introduces NeST-Bayes, a neural-symbolic framework that jointly infers robot and hypothesis trust using only inter-robot observation consistency, without requiring preassigned robot trust, reputation information, or side-channel identity signals. NeST-Bayes infers instantaneous trust evidence scores for robots and object hypotheses by reasoning over ego-centric relational graphs constructed from cross-robot observation consistency. These evidence scores are accumulated over time through Bayesian updates of Beta-distributed trust beliefs to maintain temporally consistent trust estimates. Extensive evaluations show that NeST-Bayes achieves the highest mean object precision and robot recall across all benchmark settings, and the highest robot precision and object recall in most settings, compared to trust-based methods. Under aggressive optimized attacks, it achieves 98.8\% robot precision with 93.8\% recall and 98.3\% object precision with 95.4\% recall. In an occlusion-rich Webots warehouse, it maintains 92.5\% robot precision with 92.0\% recall and 97.3\% object precision with 85.0\% recall. These results demonstrate joint improvements in the reliability and coverage of retained information.

</details>


### [38] [GG-Nav: Goal-oriented Grounding Chain-of-Thought Elicits Reasoning in Navigation Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14541)
*Shaohao Zhu,Wensi Huang,Siqi Zhang,Yuqiang Yang,Meng Wei,Chenyang Wan,Tianle Liu,Tai Wang,Jinming Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GG-Nav导航框架，通过监督微调学习结构化思维链（CoT），显式定位目标、积累证据并支持可靠决策，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉语言模型（VLM）的导航基础模型在目标导向导航任务上表现不佳，关键问题在于流式观测利用不足和目标定位不精确。

Method: GG-Nav通过监督微调学习结构化思维链，包含两个阶段：（i）Observation-to-Evidence，对相邻决策步之间的所有中间帧进行推理以保留目标相关证据；（ii）Evidence-to-Decision，整合当前观测与累积证据来定位目标，并生成导航动作或澄清问题。

Result: 在HM3D ObjectNav、VL-LN和CoIN基准上成功率分别超越最先进方法3.1%、16.6%和5.3%。消融显示，从中间观测积累证据使证据缺失失败减少11.6%，显式定位使停止错误减少49.4%。在Unitree Go2机器人上部署，展示了在未见真实环境中的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: GG-Nav是一个简单有效的导航框架，通过结构化CoT显式定位与证据积累，显著提升目标导向导航的成功率，并具备真实世界的零样本泛化能力。

Abstract: Emerging strong Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have advanced navigation foundation models. However, these models still have unsatisfactory performance on goal-oriented navigation settings. In this paper, we first conduct a motivating study revealing that the key issue lies in the insufficient utilization of streaming observations and imprecise target localization. To tackle these challenges, we propose GG-Nav, a simple yet effective navigation framework that learns a structured chain-of-thought (CoT) mechanism via supervised fine-tuning to explicitly localize goal-relevant instances, accumulate grounding evidence, and support more reliable decision-making. Specifically, the structured CoT is designed with two key stages: (i) Observation-to-Evidence, which reasons over all intermediate frames between adjacent decision steps to retain useful goal-related evidence; and (ii) Evidence-to-Decision, which integrates the current observation with accumulated evidence to ground the target and support the generation of navigation actions or clarification questions. Extensive experiments show that GG-Nav outperforms the state-of-the-art methods in terms of success rates on the HM3D ObjectNav, VL-LN, and CoIN benchmarks by 3.1%, 16.6%, and 5.3%, respectively. Ablation studies further show that accumulating evidence from intermediate observations reduces evidence-missing failures by 11.6%, while explicit grounding reduces stopping errors by 49.4%. We also deploy GG-Nav on a Unitree Go2 robot, demonstrating its zero-shot generalization capability in unseen real-world environments.

</details>


### [39] [Before We Act: Learning Action-Conditioned Team-Response Beliefs for Decentralized Multi-Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14551)
*Zihao Li,Yanan Zhou,Ranpeng Qiu,Guoqiang Ren,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CARE框架，通过从局部历史学习"队友响应信念"，将队友适应性的预测转化为有效的去中心化动作选择，在多个多机器人任务中取得最高平均成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人操作中，一个动作的价值取决于队友如何响应，因此需要显式建模队友的适应性响应，而非仅通过分支效用隐式学习这一信息。

Method: CARE包含：预测性信念编码器表征交互并调整名义动作；匹配的反应式与重放延续分离候选动作的直接优势与队友适应带来的效用；候选条件读出学习响应分布与总优势；校准选择决定采用替代动作或保留名义动作。

Result: 在表示、提案与数据固定的条件下，显式响应监督相比通过分支效用学习相同信息，能改善候选排序并降低选择后悔，收益集中在队友适应会改变偏好动作的决策上。在RoboFactory、MARS Control Track及物理协作任务中，CARE取得所评估策略中最高的平均成功率。

Conclusion: CARE能将对队友适应性的预测转化为有效的去中心化动作选择，结合有能力的名义策略与选择性协调调整，证明显式建模队友响应信念的价值。

Abstract: In multi-robot manipulation, an action's value depends on how teammates respond. We present \method{} (Cooperative Action--Response Belief Estimation), a framework for learning \emph{team-response beliefs} from local history. A predictive belief encoder represents the interaction and conditions a nominal action. Matched reactive and replay continuations separate each candidate's direct advantage from the utility contributed by teammate adaptation. A candidate-conditioned readout learns this response distribution alongside total advantage, and calibrated selection chooses an alternative or retains the nominal action. Our analysis characterizes when response changes the preferred action and how prediction and source errors limit the recoverable gain. With the representation, proposals, and data fixed, explicit response supervision improves candidate ranking and reduces selection regret relative to learning the same information through branch utilities. The benefit is concentrated in decisions where teammate adaptation changes the preferred action. Across RoboFactory, the MARS Control Track, and physical collaboration tasks, CARE achieves the highest average success among the evaluated policies, combining a capable nominal policy with selective coordination adjustments. Together, these results show that CARE turns predictions of teammate adaptation into effective decentralized action selection. Project page: https://jeong-zju.github.io/care/.

</details>


### [40] [From Video to Vision: Learning Generalizable Humanoid Carrying from Human Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14561)
*Ziluo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MOVER——多阶段Teacher-Assistant-Student强化学习框架，实现人形机器人无需动捕系统即可从机载RGB-D相机感知物体位姿并完成搬运任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视频或动捕数据的RL方法需上层模块实时生成参考轨迹，物体搬运只能从指定位置按参考轨迹抬起，空间泛化差；且策略仅在动捕棚内可评估部署。

Method: 采集视频数据，用GENMO与GMR重定向并结合数据增强泛化不同形状尺寸物体。第一阶段基于DeepMimic训练人形从指定位置沿指定轨迹搬运物体；第二阶段放松参考动作跟踪并调整奖励，学习从不同位置搬运；第三阶段用Dagger-PPO蒸馏去除本体位置观测与参考关节位置，使部署策略不依赖动捕全局位置，直接从机载RGB-D相机感知物体位姿。

Result: 仿真实验对比了第一阶段与第二阶段、以及蒸馏前后的成功率；并将策略迁移到真实人形机器人，在无动捕环境下成功搬运不同位置的物体。

Conclusion: MOVER通过分阶段训练与蒸馏，提升了人形机器人物体搬运的空间泛化能力，并实现了脱离动捕系统的真实环境部署。

Abstract: Most existing approaches rely on video or motion-capture data for reinforcement learning (RL) training; however, all of these methods require an upper-level module to generate reference trajectories in real time. In object carrying tasks, the humanoid can only lift objects from designated locations along the reference trajectory, resulting in poor spatial generalization. Moreover, existing policies can only be evaluated inside a motion-capture studio. To address these limitations, we propose \textbf{MOVER}, a multi-stage, Teacher-Assistant-Student, RL training framework for humanoid object carrying. We first collect data from videos and employ GENMO and GMR to retarget the video data, combining this with data augmentation techniques to generalize the data across objects of varying shapes and sizes. In the \textbf{first stage}, building upon the DeepMimic \cite{peng2018deepmimic} motion-training framework, we train the humanoid to transport objects from specified locations along designated trajectories. In the \textbf{second stage}, we relax the tracking of the reference motion and adjust the reward so that the humanoid learns to transport objects from different positions. In the \textbf{third stage}, we apply a Dagger-PPO distillation method to distill away the proprioceptive position observations and reference joint position of the humanoid. This allows the deployed policy to operate without relying on global position information from a motion-capture system; instead, the humanoid can directly perceive the object's pose from its onboard RGB-D camera to complete the carrying task. We design a series of simulation experiments to compare the success rates across stages (Stages 1 vs. 2) and before versus after distillation. We further transfer the policy to a real humanoid, successfully transporting objects at varying positions in a motion-capture-free setting.

</details>


### [41] [Chunk-wise Model-based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14564)
*Weixi Song,Donglin Wang,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 chunk 世界模型作为可微训练期评估器，与 chunk actor 协作单次前向生成 H 步滚动并直接优化价值展开，规避 BPTT，在 HumanoidBench 19 任务及真实四足机器人上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习虽样本效率高，但现有方法（无导数规划/想象滚动训练或动力学梯度策略更新）存在不同程度的循环复合误差、推理时延迟高、BPTT 反向传播不稳定等问题。

Method: 以 chunk 世界模型作为可微的训练期评估器，优化 chunk actor，无需循环调用模型；通过世界模型与策略的 chunk 级协同预测，在单次前向传播中生成 H 步假设滚动，直接优化价值展开，避免昂贵且不稳定的 BPTT。

Result: 在 HumanoidBench 的 19 个高维人形任务上进行了评估，并将方法部署到真实世界的四足/双足行走场景中。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种无需 BPTT 的高效模型 RL 训练范式，兼顾快学习与稳定性，并具备仿真到真实部署的能力。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning has shown superior sample efficiency by leveraging the learned latent dynamics either in the form of derivative-free planning/imagined rollout training or dynamics gradient-based policy updates. While these approaches achieve comparatively fast learning, they all suffer, to varying degrees, from issues of recurrent compounding errors, high inference-time latency, and instability in backward propagation through time (BPTT). In this work, we propose a model-based RL approach that uses the chunk world model as a differentiable training-time evaluator to optimize the chunk actor without recurrent model invocation. Through the collaborative chunk-wise prediction of the world model and policy, we can generate H-step hypothetical rollouts in a single forward pass and directly optimize value expansion, avoiding the costly and unstable BPTT. We evaluate our method on 19 challenging high-dimensional humanoid tasks from HumanoidBench and deploy our method to the real-world for quadruped bipedal walking. Project Website: https://song-wx.github.io/cmpo.github.io/

</details>


### [42] [In View or Out of View? A Vision-Aware Filter that Infers and Reacts to the Human's Field of View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14577)
*Mengxue Fu,Ya-Chuan Hsu,Yinlong Dai,Erdem Bıyık,Dylan P. Losey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种"视野感知滤波器"，让机器人推断人类在交互中看到与未看到的内容，并据此调整自身动作，从而在协作任务中提升性能与人类偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人机交互中视觉至关重要：人类看到机器人时能更好预测其行为，看不到时则对其意图与计划感到不确定。现有的人类感知规划器要么忽略人类视野、假设人类能观察整个场景，要么刻意设计交互让机器人始终可见，缺乏对"人类实际看到什么"的推断与利用。

Method: 机器人通过在线估计过程持续推断人类的视野范围。给定名义机器人策略与交互模型，在短时域内展开策略以观察机器人状态更新，然后依据动作对机器人可预测可见性的影响对动作重新加权：使机器人进入（或离开）人类视线范围的动作用更高权重。实践中该框架提供在线估计与滤波过程，反复更新对人类视野的理解，并基于人类视觉对机器人动作进行局部扰动。

Result: 在协作烹饪与驾驶协调两类机器人任务上进行评估。仿真与一项12人用户研究表明，视野感知滤波器同时改变了性能与偏好：烹饪任务中使用"视野内辅助"时参与者完成的子任务数量超过三倍，驾驶任务中与故意避开其视野的车辆发生的碰撞更少。

Conclusion: 通过让机器人推断并利用人类视野进行动作滤波，能够有效提升人机协作的任务表现与人类偏好，证明视觉感知信息在人机交互规划中具有重要价值。

Abstract: During human-robot interaction, sight plays an important role. When the human sees a robot, they can observe the robot's actions and better predict the robot's future behaviors. Conversely, when the human cannot see the robot, they become uncertain about the robot's intent and plans. In this paper, we focus on the human's field of view. Existing human-aware planners either ignore this field of view and assume the human can observe the entire scene, or intentionally design their interactions so the robot is always visible to the human. By contrast, we enable robots to infer what the human has and has not seen during interaction, i.e., the human's field of view. We then apply this estimate as a vision-aware filter on the robot's actions. Given a nominal robot policy and a model of the interaction, we roll out the policy over a short horizon to see how the robot's state updates. We then reweight the actions based on how those actions impact the robot's predicted visibility: higher weights are assigned to actions that bring the robot into (or away from) the human's line of sight. In practice, our framework provides an online estimation and filtering process that repeatedly updates its understanding of the human's field of view and then locally perturbs robot actions based on the human's vision. We evaluate our vision-aware filter on robots that assist humans in collaborative cooking and coordinate with them in driving environments. Across simulations and a 12-participant user study, we find that our vision-aware filter changes both performance and preference: participants completed over three times as many subtasks with in-view assistance while cooking, and had fewer collisions with cars that purposely stayed out of their view while driving.

</details>


### [43] [Remembering What Matters for Long-Horizon Robotic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14583)
*Hao Wu,Tianyu Shao,Shengju Qian,Yizhou Zhao,Fan Zhang,Penghao Zhao,Weiyan Wang,Yufei Huang,Fan Xu,Kun Wang,Xin Wang,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练上下文调度方法 ReCAP，通过保留初始场景锚点与近期视觉-动作块、剔除陈旧历史，缓解长时程机器人视频世界模型中的记忆角色冲突，提升保真度与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于离散视觉 token 的自回归机器人视频世界模型在长时程推演中不可靠，保留全部生成历史并不必然提升预测质量。作者推测存在"记忆角色冲突"：持久场景信息、近期视觉-动作状态与陈旧生成历史在注意力中相互竞争，而它们的预测作用不同。

Method: ReCAP（Receding Context with Anchor Preservation）是一种免训练的上下文调度方法。冻结预训练 tokenizer、世界模型与视觉解码器，保留初始场景锚点与一段连续的近期完整视觉-动作块，剔除更早的生成历史；保持块内结构与时间顺序，无需额外模块或参数更新，并使活跃上下文增长与推演长度无关。

Result: 在 RT-1、BridgeV2、LIBERO-90、CALVIN 上以冻结自回归世界模型评测。相较 Full Context，ReCAP 在视觉保真度、动作条件一致性与机器人结构保持上均有提升；BridgeV2 上 LPIPS 降低 63.3%；H=64 基准中逻辑 KV 占用降低 73.3%，p50 延迟降低 6.51%。注意力分析显示对保留的近期状态注意力上升，历史干预表明陈旧上下文越多预测误差越高。

Conclusion: 结果与"记忆角色冲突"假设一致，但未能将其与上下文长度和位置处理效应完全区分。推理时上下文调度是无需重训练即可提升长时程机器人视频预测与计算效率的实用途径。

Abstract: Autoregressive robot video world models based on discrete visual tokens remain unreliable over long rollout horizons. Retaining the entire generated history does not necessarily improve prediction quality. We investigate a possible memory-role conflict in Full Context decoding: persistent scene information, recent visual-action states, and stale generated history compete for attention despite their different predictive roles. This perspective motivates ReCAP (Receding Context with Anchor Preservation), a training-free context-scheduling method for long-horizon robot video world models. ReCAP keeps the pretrained tokenizer, world model, and visual decoder frozen. It preserves the initial scene anchor and a contiguous window of recent complete visual-action blocks while evicting older generated history. The schedule preserves within-block structure and temporal order, requires no additional modules or parameter updates, and bounds active-context growth independently of rollout length. We evaluate ReCAP on RT-1, BridgeV2, LIBERO-90, and CALVIN using frozen autoregressive world models. Across evaluated environments and horizons, ReCAP improves visual fidelity, action-conditioned consistency, and robot structure preservation relative to Full Context. On BridgeV2, it reduces LPIPS by 63.3%. In the system benchmark at H=64, it reduces the logical KV footprint by 73.3% and p50 latency by 6.51%. Attention analyses show increased attention to retained recent states, while history interventions associate larger amounts of stale context with higher prediction error. These observations are consistent with the memory-role-conflict hypothesis, although they do not isolate it from context-length and position-handling effects. Our results support inference-time context scheduling as a practical approach to improving long-horizon robot video prediction and computational efficiency without retraining.

</details>


### [44] [PC7 Model: Dynamics of Expanding and Contracting Soft Robots using Piece-wise Constant 7D Deformation Based on Similarity Transformation Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14586)
*Yamato Kudo,Taiki Ishigaki,Ko Ayusawa,Ko Yamamoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为分段常数7D变形（PC7）的新型软体机器人模型，用于表示人工肌肉执行器的伸缩变形。基于SIM(3)定义7D变形向量，将PCS模型扩展至PC7模型并推导动力学方程，通过仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的分段常数应变（PCS）模型基于SE(3)定义了6D变形向量，仅能描述Cosserat杆的变形，无法表示人工肌肉等软体执行器的伸缩（扩展与收缩）变形。因此需要一种能同时刻画伸缩变形的新型软体机器人模型。

Method: 提出PC7模型，基于SIM(3)（3D相似变换群）重新定义7D变形向量，比基于SE(3)的6D向量多出一个维度，用于表示来自SIM(3)缩放参数的伸缩变形。利用SE(3)与SIM(3)之间的共同数学性质，将PCS模型的运动学与动力学直接扩展至PC7模型，并基于Euler-Poincare方程严格推导其动力学方程。

Result: 通过对薄圆柱结构和McKibben型人工肌肉执行器的动力学仿真，验证了所提出的PC7模型的有效性。

Conclusion: PC7模型成功扩展了PCS模型，能够刻画软体执行器的伸缩变形，并具备严谨的数学推导与仿真验证，适用于人工肌肉执行器等软体机器人系统的建模。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel soft robot model named piece-wise constant 7D deformation (PC7) model that can represent expanding and contracting deformation of, for example, artificial muscle actuator. In the proposed model, we newly define the 7D deformation vector based on SIM(3) - 3D similarity transformation group - whereas a 6D deformation vector was defined for Cosserat rod based on SE(3) - the special Euclid group - in the piece-wise constant strain (PCS) model proposed in the previous study. The additional dimension of the 7D deformation vector represents expanding and contracting deformation, which comes from the scaling parameter in SIM(3). Common mathematical properties between SE(3) and SIM(3) allow us to directly extend the kinematics and dynamics of the PCS model to those of the PC7 model. In particular, we derive the dynamical equation of the PC7 model based on Euler-Poincare equation in a mathematically precise manner. We validate the proposed model through dynamical simulations assuming a thin cylinder structure or McKibben-type artificial muscle actuator.

</details>


### [45] [Flow-Support-Weighted Proposals for Test-Time Verified VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14588)
*Chen Zhao,Zipeng Ye,Haitao Shen,Guanlin Li,Yuchen Xie,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出"提议收缩"现象：微调后的VLA模型其流匹配解码器集中于主导动作，导致测试时重复采样冗余；作者提出Action Dynamic Sampling (ADS) 在训练阶段缓解该问题，提升候选多样性与验证器效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时采样本应把单次动作预测变为一组可供验证器选择的候选动作，但下游微调使模型流匹配解码器集中于单一主导动作，多次采样差异极小，验证器收到大量相同决策（即"提议收缩"），成为测试时扩展的关键瓶颈，需从训练侧缓解。

Method: Action Dynamic Sampling (ADS) 在训练阶段引入：每步训练时从当前策略在新基础噪声下积分K条额外轨迹，用Hutchinson迹估计器按路径上的流支撑（flow support）打分，并通过detached有限集KL项将策略局部动作分布向该加权集合修正。训练开销上升，部署开销不变。

Result: 在仿真与四个真实机器人任务中，ADS提升了提议分布散度，且无高斯扰动带来的成功率损失；RoboCasa上oracle best-of-8成功率从57.6%提升至63.0%；仿真中固定验证器提升2.4–4.9个百分点；真实世界操作中TokenPB最多提升13.0个百分点。

Conclusion: ADS从提议端缓解提议收缩，在不增加部署成本的前提下提升候选多样性与测试时扩展效果，验证了训练侧干预对测试时采样的价值。

Abstract: Test-time sampling promises to turn one action prediction into a set of distinct executable candidates that a verifier can choose among. Downstream fine-tuning works against that promise. A vision-language-action model trained to solve one task distribution concentrates its flow-matching decoder around the dominant demonstrated action, so repeated samples differ only marginally and the verifier receives many copies of one decision. We call this proposal contraction and identify it as a key constraint on test-time scaling: repeated samples can remain highly redundant as the candidate budget grows. Action Dynamic Sampling (ADS) alleviates the constraint on the proposal side, during training. At each training step, ADS integrates $K$ additional trajectories from the current policy under fresh base noise, scores each by the flow support along its path with a Hutchinson trace estimator, and corrects the policy's local action distribution toward this weighted set through a detached finite-set KL term. Training cost rises; deployment cost is unchanged. Across simulation and four physical-robot tasks, ADS raises proposal spread without the success penalty that Gaussian perturbation incurs, lifts oracle best-of-8 success on RoboCasa from 57.6% to 63.0%, improves fixed verifiers by 2.4 to 4.9 points in simulation, and raises TokenPB by up to 13.0 points on real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [46] [ToF-Wheel Odometry: Navigating Degeneration of Localization With Limited Sensing Capabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14590)
*Xu Yang,Wenhao Li,Zhengyu Wei,Zening Li,Longxiang Huang,Bo Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TOF-轮式里程计（TWO），一个紧耦合滤波框架，将轮式运动学与ToF相机的几何约束融合，核心是退化感知的方向性协方差塑形策略，替代启发式在线协方差调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮式里程计为地面机器人提供高效运动先验，但易受轮滑或地形倾斜等瞬态破坏影响；在滤波式里程计中，这些问题与受限几何可观测性（如无纹理走廊）同时发生时，常引发发散。现有方法多依赖启发式在线协方差调优，缺乏可靠性与自适应性的平衡。

Method: 提出TWO：紧耦合滤波框架融合轮式运动学与ToF相机几何约束。核心为退化感知、方向性的协方差塑形策略，以几何条件化膨胀替代启发式在线调优。将后验到先验的修正分解为轮式可解释子空间与正交余项，根据ToF更新的可观测性对各向异性调整过程协方差：几何退化时抑制余项为传感器噪声，几何清晰时将其视为有效运动学破坏。另引入帧内反馈回路，在当前帧内回溯修复过程协方差，减轻突发扰动下的结构滞后。

Result: 在挑战性场景的大量实验中，TWO相比静态与自适应高斯滤波提升了轨迹精度与鲁棒性，稳健地平衡了运动学可靠性与有限感知能力之间的权衡。

Conclusion: TWO通过几何条件化的协方差塑形与帧内反馈回路，有效缓解轮式里程计在退化环境中的发散问题，提升地面机器人里程计的精度与鲁棒性，为紧耦合ToF-轮式融合提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Wheel odometry provides an efficient motion prior for ground robots but is prone to transient violations such as wheel slip or terrain-induced tilt. In filtering-based odometry, these violations frequently trigger divergence when they coincide with constrained geometric observability (e.g., featureless corridors). We address this challenge by proposing ToF-Wheel Odometry (TWO), a tightly-coupled filtering framework that integrates wheel kinematics with geometric constraints from Time-of-Flight (ToF) cameras. The primary contribution of this work is a degeneracy-aware, directional covariance-shaping strategy that replaces heuristic online covariance tuning with geometry-conditioned inflation. The core idea is to infer process noise from geometric corrections with a strict reliability check. We decompose the posterior-to-prior correction into a wheel-explainable subspace and an orthogonal remainder. The process covariance is anisotropically adjusted according to the observability of the ToF update: if the geometry is degenerate in non-kinematic directions, the remainder is suppressed as sensor noise; if the geometry is distinct, it is admitted as a valid kinematic violation. Additionally, we introduce an intra-frame feedback loop that retrospectively repairs the process covariance within the current frame to reduce structural lag during bursty disturbances. Extensive experiments on challenging scenarios demonstrate that TWO improves trajectory accuracy and robustness compared to static and adaptive Gaussian filtering, robustly navigating the trade-off between kinematic reliability and limited sensing capabilities.

</details>


### [47] [Load-Aware Quadruped Navigation with Online Contact-based Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14596)
*Qi Yin,XiaoLi Zhang,Yangming Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两阶段负载感知导航学习框架：Phase 1 用强化学习训练LOADNav导航策略，使四足机器人按所携物体几何尺寸调整轨迹与间隙；Phase 2 用模仿学习训练CoGE在线几何预测器，从偶发接触事件中估计未知负载几何，无需先验即可逼近几何感知策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人在杂乱环境中搬运物体仍是难题。已有负载相关研究多关注负载感知的运动步态适应以保持步态稳定，而现有自主导航系统普遍忽略所携物体几何形状的影响，采用固定的足印膨胀，导致导航过于保守。该工作旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 两阶段负载感知导航学习框架。Phase 1：通过强化学习训练负载感知导航策略LOADNav，使机器人根据所携物体的几何尺寸调整轨迹与间隙行为。Phase 2：通过模仿学习训练在线几何预测器CoGE，从偶发接触事件中估计未知负载几何。

Result: 在携带不同尺寸与形状负载的杂乱环境中评估。结果显示导航策略表现出明确的负载感知行为；在线几何预测器能够从偶发接触事件估计物体几何，逐步细化估计并相应调整导航行为。

Conclusion: 在不需要任何负载先验知识的情况下，该框架缩小了与几何感知策略之间的差距。

Abstract: Navigating cluttered environments while carrying objects remains a challenging problem for quadruped robots. Compared with works that focused on load-aware locomotion adaptation to maintain gait stability, existing autonomous navigation systems generally ignore the influence of carried objects' geometries and use fixed footprint inflation, leading to overly conservative navigation. To fill this knowledge gap, we propose a two-phase load-aware navigation learning framework. In Phase 1, we train LOADNav, a load-aware navigation policy via reinforcement learning, enabling the robot to adapt its trajectory and clearance behavior according to the geometric size of the carried object. In Phase 2, we train CoGE, an online geometry predictor via imitation learning, which estimates unknown payload geometry from occasional contact events. We evaluate the framework in cluttered environments with loads of varying sizes and shapes. The results show that our navigation policy exhibits clear load-aware behaviors, and the online geometry predictor estimates object geometry from occasional contact events, refining its estimate and adapting the navigation behavior accordingly. Without requiring any prior knowledge of the payload, it narrows the gap to geometry-aware policies.

</details>


### [48] [StateSkill-Agent: An Effective and Efficient Embodied Agent via State-conditioned Skill](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14601)
*Changxu Wei,Jingyi Zhang,Zhiyu Zheng,Tianyi Lin,Yixian Zhang,Yunpeng Qing,Jingyuan Liu,Zhi Hou,Jiaxing Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出StateSkill，将有限状态机引入具身技能建模，并配合StateSkill-Evolve与StateGRPO实现技能与模型协同进化，构建具身智能体StateSkill-Agent。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身MLLM智能体难以学习和复用可迁移、可泛化的行为模式，技能表示与构建效率不足。

Method: 提出StateSkill技能表示，将具身交互经验建模为有限状态机（任务进展状态、状态转移、状态条件引导）；设计StateSkill-Evolve经验驱动进化机制，通过回顾成功与失败轨迹迭代优化技能；引入StateGRPO，将技能进化与状态条件GRPO模型优化整合为统一迭代学习闭环。

Result: 大量实验验证了所提方法及训练模型StateSkill-Agent在多种具身任务上的有效性。

Conclusion: 状态条件化的技能与模型能力可协同进化、相互增强，为具身智能体技能学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: In this work, we aim to develop an embodied MLLM agent that understands and solves embodied tasks by learning, abstracting, and reusing transferrable and generalizable behavioral patterns. To this end, we propose StateSkill, a new skill representation for embodied agents, which introduces the concept of "finite-state machines" into skill modeling for effective and efficient skill construction and learning. The core idea of StateSkill is to model embodied interaction experiences as a finite-state machine, where a skill is organized around a set of task-progress states, state transitions, and state-conditioned guidance, enabling embodied agents to dynamically adjust their skills as the task progresses while keeping skill contexts compact and stage-relevant. We further develop StateSkill-Evolve, an experience-driven evolution mechanism that iteratively refines StateSkill through retrospective analysis of new successful and failed state-guided trajectories. Upon this, we introduce StateGRPO, which integrates StateSkill evolution and model optimization via state-conditioned GRPO into a unified iterative learning loop, enabling state-conditioned skills and model capabilities to co-evolve and reinforce each other. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our proposed methods and trained model (i.e., StateSkill-Agent) across various embodied tasks.

</details>


### [49] [AnyRank: Reliable Anytime Policy Ranking via Sequential Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14603)
*Apurva Badithela,David Snyder,Tenny Yin,Jade Sceats,George Pappas,Nikolai Matni,Masha Itkina,Haruki Nishimura,Anirudha Majumdar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyRank是一种用于多策略排序的序贯方法，能够自适应分配试验、在任意停止时刻给出（部分）排序并控制错误排序概率，还可选地利用先验信息提升硬件评估效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件实验成本高昂，机器人策略通常只能在有限的硬件试验上比较经验性能。然而有限的样本统计不确定性可能导致错误结论，尤其是在用固定一批评估数据对多个策略排序时，这一问题尤为突出。

Method: 提出AnyRank：一种多策略排序的序贯方法，结合序贯检验与多假设图检验理论。它（i）自适应地将试验分配给难以区分的策略；（ii）在任意停止时刻提供（部分）排序，同时限制错误排序的概率；（iii）可选地利用先验信息优先安排硬件评估。先验可来自专家直觉、少量留出硬件评估、仿真器或动作条件视频世界模型的策略 rollout，且允许不完美——先验不准确不会破坏统计保证。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界策略评估基准实验中，AnyRank相比现有统计严谨的方法最多可减少50%的硬件评估次数，同时避免最先进基准所用启发式方法产生的错误排序。

Conclusion: AnyRank通过序贯检验与多假设图检验的组合，实现了高效且具有统计保证的多策略排序，显著降低硬件评估成本，并可通过（不完美的）先验进一步提升效率而不损害统计保证。

Abstract: Due to the high cost of hardware experiments, robot policies are predominantly evaluated by comparing their empirical performance on a limited number of hardware trials. However, finite-sample statistical uncertainty can lead to erroneous conclusions, especially when ranking multiple policies using a fixed batch of evaluation data. We propose AnyRank: a sequential method for multi-policy ranking that (i) adaptively allocates trials to hard-to-separate policies, (ii) provides (partial) rankings at any stopping time while bounding the probability of an incorrect ranking, and (iii) optionally exploits prior information to prioritize hardware evaluations more efficiently. This prior information could be informed from expert intuition, a small set of held-out hardware evaluations, or from policy rollouts in a simulator or an action-conditioned video world model. Notably, the prior can be imperfect; while a strong prior can provide efficiency gains on hardware, any inaccuracies in prior policy rankings do not invalidate the statistical assurances of AnyRank. We achieve the aforementioned properties through novel theoretical contributions that combine sequential testing with multiple-hypothesis graphical testing approaches. Experiments in simulation and real-world policy evaluation benchmarks show that AnyRank requires up to 50% fewer hardware evaluations compared to existing statistically rigorous approaches, while guarding against the incorrect rankings produced by heuristic methods used by state-of-the-art benchmarks.

</details>


### [50] [VbotNav-0: Asynchronous Cloud-Edge Dual-Policy Navigation across Indoor and Outdoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14634)
*Shiqi Tan,Yujun Zhang,Keyu Li,Shuaixi Pan,Siyu Zhou,Hailong Qin,Yinan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出云边双策略框架VbotNav-0，通过异步策略间条件化实现室内外连续导航，闭环评测成功率55.04%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续室内外导航需要将目的地级推理与必须响应实时观测的运动决策相连接，现有方法难以在云更新间隙保持局部感知-动作循环。

Method: 云边双策略框架：云端策略依据视觉历史、导航指令与路线token生成子目标引导；边缘策略结合可用引导与当前RGB、LiDAR观测生成局部轨迹。采用异步策略间条件化，并以统一路线表示连接室内路径与室外地图路线。训练结合导航示范、纠正数据与接地VQA。

Result: 在145个场景、258个回合的闭环评测中，成功率达55.04%，按路径长度加权的成功率为55.03%。

Conclusion: 该方法通过云边异步协作与统一路线表示，实现了室内外连续导航的有效闭环，验证了双策略条件化框架的可行性。

Abstract: Continuous indoor-outdoor navigation connects destination-level reasoning with motion decisions that must respond to current observations. We present VbotNav-0, a cloud-edge dual-policy framework that links these processes through asynchronous policy-to-policy conditioning. The cloud policy produces subgoal guidance from visual history, navigation instructions, and route tokens; the edge policy combines available guidance with current RGB and LiDAR observations to generate local trajectories. This formulation allows the local perception-action loop to continue between cloud updates. A common route representation connects indoor paths and outdoor map routes, and the training recipe combines navigation demonstrations, corrective data, and grounded visual question answering (VQA). In the reported closed-loop evaluation of 258 episodes across 145 scenes, VbotNav-0 achieves 55.04% success rate and 55.03% success weighted by path length.

</details>


### [51] [Global Optimality of Pose Graph Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14655)
*Shuan Ding,Ruishuang Chen,Mengfan Cao,Zaiyue Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究PGO的优化景观，建立了全局最优性的充分条件，证明当噪声小于显式先验界时，全局最优解附近的优化景观是良性的，且盆内存在唯一的局部最优解即全局最优解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PGO是机器人状态估计中的经典问题，在定位、建图和导航中起基础作用，但其非凸性导致存在多个局部极小值，难以分析。本文旨在从理论上确认：当噪声小于显式先验界时，全局最优解附近的优化景观是良性的。

Method: 本文发展了三个主要技术结果：首先识别包含全局最优解的吸引域（BOA），并通过显式方式刻画其半径受噪声控制的几何性质；其次探索PGO的一阶最优性条件，证明若满足噪声控制准则则局部极小值即全局最优；最后结合上述结果，证明当噪声小于可先验验证的显式界时，BOA内存在唯一局部极小值且为全局最优。

Result: 证明了当噪声小于显式界时，BOA内存在且仅存在一个局部极小值，且该极小值为全局最优；数值仿真在多种随机情形下验证了该理论，表明所得条件为认证PGO全局最优性提供了有效准则。

Conclusion: 本文为PGO优化景观的良性性质提供了理论保障，给出了可先验验证的全局最优性充分条件，可用于判定PGO求解的可靠性。

Abstract: Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) is a classical and widely applied problem in state estimation for robotics, playing a fundamental role in localization, mapping, and navigation. Yet, it is nonconvex in nature and may have many local minimizers, thus difficult to analyze. In this paper, we investigate the optimization landscape and establish a sufficient condition of global optimality for PGO. The ultimate goal of this paper is to theoretically confirm that the optimization landscape is benign and attractive around the global minimizer, if noise is smaller than an explicit and a priori bound. To reveal such an insight, this paper develops three main technical results. First, we identify a proper basin of attraction (BOA) containing global minimizer, and characterize its geometric property by showing that its radius is controlled by noise in an explicit way. Then, by exploring the first order optimality conditions of PGO, we prove that a local minimizer is globally optimal if a noise-controlled criterion is satisfied. Finally, by combining above two results, we prove that one and only one local minimizer exists in the BOA and this minimizer is globally optimal, when the noise is smaller than an explicit bound that can be verified a priori. Numerical simulations validate the proposed theory under various random cases, showing that the obtained conditions provide an effective criterion for certifying the global optimality of PGO.

</details>


### [52] [Successful but Unsafe: An Analytical Benchmark for Revealing Collisions in Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14682)
*Hieu D. Pham,Quoc-An Luong,Dung D. Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出VLN-Safe基准,揭示视觉语言导航中"成功"与"碰撞安全"间的巨大差距,并给出无需训练的碰撞守卫方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLN评价只关注是否到达目标和路径效率,几乎不反映碰撞行为;且常用的滑动式评估允许智能体沿障碍物滑行,掩盖了不安全动作的后果,因此需要一个专门分析碰撞安全的评估框架。

Method: 提出VLN-Safe基准,在两种评估机制下分析碰撞安全:滑动式(反映常见做法)与阻挡式(碰撞动作阻止前进,直接暴露后果)。在R2R-CE上评测六个已发表智能体;分析预测即将碰撞的信号(深度几何、近期运动历史、策略衍生信号、安全替代动作可得性);并设计免训练的碰撞守卫:否决高风险前进动作、执行逃逸动作、再将控制权交回原策略。

Result: 滑动式下38.5–56.8%的成功回合包含接触;切换到阻挡式后成功率下降11.7–36.1个百分点。信号分析显示深度几何与近期运动历史比策略衍生信号更有信息量,且策略偏好动作附近少有安全替代。阻挡式下守卫将碰撞步数减少62–98%,成功率提升6.9–29.3个百分点(五个智能体);滑动式下成功率不变,无碰撞成功占比提升4.1–12.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 任务成功与碰撞安全导航之间存在显著差距;轻量级、碰撞感知的干预既能提升安全性也能改善导航性能。

Abstract: Vision-and-language navigation (VLN) agents are typically evaluated by whether they reach a target and how efficiently they follow an instructed route, yet these metrics provide little information about collision behavior. Moreover, continuous VLN evaluation commonly uses a sliding regime, where an agent can slide along obstacles after a collision, potentially masking the effect of unsafe actions on navigation performance. We introduce VLN-Safe, a benchmark for analyzing collision safety under two complementary evaluation regimes: sliding, reflecting common VLN evaluation practice, and blocking, where collision-inducing actions prevent forward motion and more directly expose their consequences. Across six published agents on R2R-CE, 38.5-56.8% of successful episodes contain contact under sliding, while switching to blocking decreases success rate by 11.7-36.1 percentage points. We further analyze signals for anticipating imminent collisions and find that depth geometry and recent movement history are more informative than policy-derived signals, while safe alternatives near the policy's preferred action are rarely available. Motivated by these findings, we introduce a training-free collision guard that vetoes risky forward actions, executes an escape maneuver, and returns control to the original policy. Under blocking, the guard reduces collision steps by 62-98% and improves success rate by 6.9-29.3 percentage points across five recurrent, topological, and vision-language-action agents. Under sliding, it leaves success rate unchanged while raising the share of collision-free successes by 4.1-12.4 points. These results reveal a substantial gap between task success and collision-safe navigation and show that lightweight collision-aware intervention can improve both safety and navigation performance.

</details>


### [53] [DexCast: Learning Real-World Dexterous Control from Human Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14691)
*Zihan Gao,Yuhong Zhang,Mengzhe Wang,Kaisheng Liu,Shengpeng Li,Siyao Xiao,Runqing Cheng,Xiao Lin,Zhifang Liu,Haoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DEXCAST是一个直接从单目RGB-D视频学习灵巧手控制、无需遥操作的框架，并提出DMPM模块捕捉关节协调与时序依赖以条件化扩散策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有灵巧模仿学习方法依赖昂贵且人工遥操作的机器人演示，需大量人力；同时高维机器人手部运动建模与提取时序上下文以指导未来动作生成仍是关键挑战。

Method: 构建DEXCAST框架，利用联合的手-物交互（HOI）重建，从单目RGB-D视频中直接学习灵巧机器人手控制；提出DexHand运动感知模块（DMPM），在紧凑潜在表示中捕捉关节间协调与时序依赖，并以此条件化扩散策略。

Result: 大量真实世界实验表明，DEXCAST在多种灵巧操作任务上取得优异性能。

Conclusion: 无需遥操作、仅凭单目RGB-D视频即可实现灵巧机器人手控制的模仿学习，DMPM有效建模高维手部运动，验证了该框架的实用性与有效性。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D hand-object interaction (HOI) perception have opened new opportunities for learning dexterous robotic skills from human videos. However, existing dexterous imitation learning methods often rely on costly and manually teleoperated robot demonstrations, requiring substantial human effort. Therefore, we build DEXCAST, a framework that learns dexterous robotic hand control directly from monocular RGB-D videos by leveraging joint HOI reconstructions, without requiring any teleoperation. Furthermore, how to model high-dimensional robotic hand motion and extract temporal context to inform future action generation remains a key challenge. To this end, we propose a DexHand Motion Perception Module (DMPM) to capture inter-joint coordination and temporal dependencies in compact latent representations that condition our diffusion policy. Extensive real-world experiments demonstrate that DEXCAST achieves exceptional performance across diverse dexterous manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [54] [BGC-Nav: Cross-Altitude Multi-UAV Collaborative Navigation via Belief-Guided Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14702)
*Jiuwu Hao,Yueyang Wu,Liguo Sun,Haolin Song,Zao Zhang,Pin Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的跨高度协作导航框架BGC-Nav，通过信念引导耦合实现鲁棒搜索救援。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单无人机户外搜索救援受部分可观测性限制，跨高度协作有前景，但现有协作方法依赖精确目标定位和刚性目标派发，忽视高空观测不确定性、抑制低空探索自主性。

Method: 免训练跨高度协作框架。高空UAV维护编码任务条件先验的全局信念记忆，低空智能体通过局部穷尽记忆(LEM)、局部吸引增强(LAA)和方向信念先验(DBP)将其融入决策，作为建议性偏置而非刚性指令。

Result: 在ESARBench上的大量实验表明，BGC-Nav持续优于最先进的单智能体和协作方法。

Conclusion: 信念引导耦合可有效缓解高空观测不确定性并保留低空探索自主性，实现鲁棒的跨高度协作导航。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN), single Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) search-and-rescue in outdoor scenarios remains challenging due to partial observability, making cross-altitude collaboration a promising direction. However, existing collaborative navigation methods rely on precise target localization and rigid goal-dispatching paradigms, ignoring high-altitude observation uncertainty and suppressing low-altitude exploration autonomy. To address these issues, we introduce BGC-Nav, a training-free cross-altitude collaboration framework that enables robust navigation via Belief-Guided Coupling (BGC). Specifically, the high-altitude UAV maintains a Global Belief Memory encoding task-conditioned priors, which the low-altitude agent incorporates into decision-making via Local Exhaustion Memory (LEM), Local Attraction Augmentation (LAA), and Directional Belief Prior (DBP) as an advisory bias rather than a rigid command. Extensive experiments on ESARBench show that BGC-Nav consistently outperforms state-of-the-art single-agent and collaborative methods. Code is available at https://github.com/uestchjw/BGC-Nav.

</details>


### [55] [PRISM: Precision Forcing for Generalist Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14704)
*Chenghao Yue,Peiyan Li,Hao Wang,Lei Lv,Jiahao Chen,Huaping Liu,Yu Luo,Yuanlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文指出现有VLA模型在精密操作任务中表现不佳，提出“流时间饥饿”概念，并设计PRISM方法，通过精度感知的重加权与内化提升精密操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将预训练视觉语言模型与扩散或流匹配动作专家耦合的VLA范式在精密操作（如亚毫米级线缆插入）中不可靠。作者将其归因于训练目标在不同上下文和流时间上对流匹配误差进行平均，导致对决定任务成败的关键时刻监督不足，且沿生成路径推理时，不同流时间上的同等惩罚误差会对实际执行动作产生不等影响，随着物理容差收紧，性能急剧下降。

Method: 作者提出“流时间饥饿”概念，并实例化为“精度感知重加权与内化”策略PRISM。该方法离线评估精度需求，在误差影响大且动作信号可见处加强流监督，并将该精度需求内化到VLM条件表示中，使策略能够识别何时需要更高精度。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，PRISM在驱动连接器插入、双臂连接器对接和螺钉拧紧任务上保持或提升了通用任务性能，同时将平均端到端成功率从38.3%提升至86.7%，且无需新传感器或观察模态。

Conclusion: PRISM通过将监督分配和表示学习与上下文相关的精度需求联合对齐，显著扩展了现有具身策略的精度边界，证明了精度感知训练策略在精密操作任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Coupling a pretrained vision-language model (VLM) with a diffusion or flow-matching action expert has become the mainstream vision-language-action (VLA) paradigm for embodied policies, yet they remain unreliable once a task demands precise manipulation, e.g., submillimeter cable insertion. We attribute this weakness to a training objective that averages flow-matching errors across contexts and flow times, allocating little supervision to the few decisive moments determining task success. Along the generative path, inference integrates a locally supervised velocity field, so equally penalized errors at different flow times can affect the executed action unequally. Consequently, precision-task performance deteriorates as physical tolerance tightens by orders of magnitude. We call this supervision imbalance across contexts and flow times Flow-Time Starvation. The remedy is to jointly align supervision allocation and representation learning with context-dependent precision demand, enabling the policy to recognize when greater precision is needed. We instantiate this precision-forcing strategy as Precision-aware Reweighting and Internalization for Skilled Manipulation (PRISM), which scores precision demand offline, strengthens flow supervision where errors are consequential and action signals are visible, and internalizes that demand in the VLM condition representation. Simulation and real-robot experiments show that PRISM preserves or improves general-task performance while raising mean end-to-end success from 38.3% to 86.7% across drive connector insertion, bimanual connector mating, and screw tightening, substantially extending the precision boundary of existing embodied policies without new sensors or observation modalities.

</details>


### [56] [C2Flight: A Closed-Loop Co-Simulation Framework for Early-Stage Evaluation of Heterogeneous UAV Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14715)
*Xizhu Chen,Shuyuan Zhang,Yixuan Li,Huazhong Yang,Yu Wang,Jincheng Yu,Hongyang Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 C2Flight，一个面向异构无人机计算系统的闭环协同仿真框架，用参数化数据流图建模目标系统，并在早期设计阶段预测闭环飞行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构无人机计算系统的早期设计需要在完整系统可用前评估算法、硬件、通信和系统拓扑对闭环飞行的影响。但设计选择通过机器人反馈回路相互作用，难以从孤立的组件指标预测任务性能，而现有执行驱动方法要到计算系统集成后才能给出系统级设计反馈。

Method: 将目标系统表示为参数化数据流图，通过统一节点接口把设计中硬件模型与可独立访问的物理硬件集成，构建闭环协同仿真框架；其图运行时捕获缓冲、激活、资源共享、通信和局部时序对闭环飞行行为的综合影响。

Result: 与机载和无线离板物理计算流水线的验证表明，该框架具有时序保真度和闭环预测精度；两个案例研究展示了部署方案和加速器位宽选择的早期探索。

Conclusion: C2Flight 能在完整系统集成前揭示系统级设计权衡，支持快速的早期设计迭代优化。

Abstract: Early-stage design of heterogeneous UAV computing systems requires evaluating how algorithm, hardware, communication, and system topology affect closed-loop flight before the complete system is available. However, design choices interact through the robotic feedback loop, making task performance difficult to predict from isolated component metrics, while existing execution-driven approaches postpone system-level design feedback until the computing system is integrated. This work presents C2Flight, a closed-loop co-simulation framework that represents target systems as parameterized dataflow graphs and integrates models of hardware under design with independently accessible physical hardware through a unified node interface. Its graph runtime captures the combined effects of buffering, activation, resource sharing, communication, and local timing on closed-loop flight behavior. Validation against onboard and wireless offboard physical computing pipelines confirms its timing fidelity and closed-loop prediction accuracy. Two case studies demonstrate early-stage exploration of deployment and accelerator bit-width choices. These results show that C2Flight exposes system-level design trade-offs before full-system integration, enabling rapid early-stage design refinement.

</details>


### [57] [CAG-WAM: Causal Action-Gain World Action Models for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14720)
*Peizheng Guo,Cheng Zhang,Xingyu Zhang,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出因果动作增益世界动作模型（CAG-WAM），通过结构因果模型分析动作评估中的混杂路径，定义因果动作增益（CAG）来刻画候选动作引起的未来变化，并在训练与推理中加以利用，实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）将动作生成与未来预测结合，用预测未来支持动作评估与机器人决策。但候选动作对应的未来同时包含动作诱发变化和环境驱动变化，直接评估预测未来无法准确剥离动作本身带来的影响。例如，使有利环境未来变差的动作仍可能得到有利结果，反之亦然。因此需要从因果角度区分这两类变化。

Method: 作者为WAMs中的动作评估构建结构因果模型（SCM），形式化动作诱发变化与环境驱动变化如何共同影响未来评估，并揭示环境演化影响候选动作未来评估的后门路径；通过干预与后门调整处理该路径，给出因果依据。据此提出CAG-WAM：对每个候选动作，在同一当前观测下分别预测动作条件未来与环境未来，将二者评估差异定义为因果动作增益（CAG）。训练时将CAG融入组相对策略优化（GRPO）指导策略学习；推理时用CAG评估初始动作，必要时利用预测未来信息修正。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验支持了CAG-WAM的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过因果分析区分动作诱发与环境驱动变化，并以CAG刻画动作相关未来变化，可提升世界动作模型的动作评估与机器人决策性能。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) combine action generation with future prediction, allowing predicted futures to support action evaluation and robot decision making. However, the future associated with a candidate action may contain action-induced and environment-driven changes. Directly evaluating the predicted future may not fully characterize the future change associated with the candidate action. For example, a candidate action that worsens a favorable environmental future may still yield a favorable outcome, while one that improves an unfavorable environmental future may still yield an unfavorable outcome. To analyze this issue, we formulate a Structural Causal Model (SCM) for action evaluation in WAMs. The SCM formalizes how action-induced and environment-driven changes jointly shape future evaluation, and reveals a backdoor path through which environmental evolution can also affect the future evaluation associated with a candidate action. Intervention and backdoor adjustment account for this path, providing a causal basis for characterizing the future change associated with each candidate action. Based on this analysis, we propose Causal Action-Gain World Action Model (CAG-WAM). For each candidate action, CAG-WAM predicts an action-conditioned future and an environmental future under the same current observation, and defines their evaluation difference as causal action gain (CAG) for characterizing the future change associated with the candidate action. During training, we incorporate CAG into Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to guide robot policy learning. During inference, we evaluate the initial action with CAG and, when needed, revise it using predicted future information. Experiments on multiple benchmarks support the effectiveness of CAG-WAM.

</details>


### [58] [B-Human '26 - Scalable Team Play up to 11 vs. 11](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14733)
*Arne Hasselbring,Tim Laue,Thomas Röfer,Philip Reichenberg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: B-Human在2026年新成立的人形足球联赛中间组别中多次夺冠，并首次尝试11台人形机器人的演示赛，展示了行为策略灵活性与系统鲁棒性，论文介绍了从标准平台联赛转战人形联赛的调整及行为架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 介绍B-Human从标准平台联赛（机器人尺寸减半、重量不足三分之一）转战新设立的人形足球联赛所需的技术调整，并探索多机器人（11台）协同比赛的未知领域。

Method: 阐述为适配人形联赛所做的系统调整，并详细介绍其行为架构，架构设计借鉴了长期采用11人制比赛的RoboCup联赛中的相关概念。

Result: B-Human在2026年人形足球联赛中间组别中多次获得冠军，并成功完成11台人形机器人的演示赛。

Conclusion: 该系统在行为策略灵活性与整体鲁棒性方面表现出色，并为大规模多机器人协同提供了可行方案。

Abstract: B-Human won multiple competitions in the Middle Division of the new Humanoid Soccer League in 2026. In addition to regular matches with three or five players per team, we ventured into uncharted territory by playing demo matches with eleven humanoid robots, highlighting the tactical flexibility of our behavior and the overall robustness of our system. As B-Human formerly competed in the Standard Platform League with robots half the size and less than a third the weight, we begin by outlining the adjustments needed for this transition. The central section of this paper details our behavior architecture, drawing on concepts from RoboCup leagues that have used 11 players for much longer.

</details>


### [59] [BridgeWAM: Bridging Video and Action Experts via Latent Bridge Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14746)
*Jiaxin Fang,Ruitong Liu,Zeyu Lai,Xingyu Wang,Mingyuan Jia,Pingyue Sheng,Weikang Wan,Mengdi Xu,Yuxiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Bridge-of-Experts (BoE) 及 BridgeWAM，用 32 个潜在桥接查询聚合多模态先验，解耦专家深度，实现超轻动作头与高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 World Action Models 通过密集逐层 MoT 注意力耦合视频与动作专家，导致动作预测暴露于无关视觉特征，且强制专家深度匹配，阻碍视觉特征的选择性使用并带来冗余计算。

Method: 提出 Bridge-of-Experts (BoE)，实例化为 BridgeWAM：用 32 个 Latent Bridge Queries (LBQs) 将视觉、语言、本体感知先验聚合到共享的受限潜在空间；联合视频-动作监督使动作梯度引导视频专家学习更适合动作预测的表示，动作专家通过交叉注意力选择性检索任务相关信息；解耦专家深度并在动作去噪中复用 LBQs。

Result: 5.06B 模型（30 层视频专家 + 2 层动作专家）在 LIBERO 达 98.25% 成功率，LIBERO-Plus 池化成功率 72.24%；相比 Fast-WAM，仿真中单样本推理 FLOPs 降低 28.72%，真机延迟降至 77.1 ms（2.47 倍加速）；消融支持联合表示自适应与统一 LBQ 条件化；定性结果显示对目标物体和任务目的地有优先注意力。

Conclusion: BoE/BridgeWAM 通过受限潜在桥接空间解耦专家耦合，在保持强泛化与任务执行能力的同时显著提升推理效率，验证了统一 LBQ 条件化与联合表示自适应的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) commonly couple video and action experts through dense, layer-wise Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention, exposing action prediction to irrelevant visual features while enforcing matched expert depths. This coupling hinders selective visual-feature use and introduces redundant computation. We introduce Bridge-of-Experts (BoE), instantiated as BridgeWAM, which aggregates visual, language, and proprioceptive priors through 32 Latent Bridge Queries (LBQs) into a shared, limited latent space. Joint video-action supervision lets action gradients guide the Video Expert toward representations better suited for action prediction, while the Action Expert learns to selectively retrieve task-relevant information through cross-attention. Decoupling expert depths and reusing LBQs during action denoising yield a 5.06B model with a 30-layer Video Expert and a two-layer Action Expert, achieving 98.25% success on LIBERO and 72.24% pooled success on LIBERO-Plus, demonstrating effective task execution and strong generalization with an ultralight action head. Ablations support joint representation adaptation and unified LBQ conditioning. Relative to Fast-WAM, BridgeWAM reduces single-sample inference FLOPs by 28.72% in simulation profiling and real-robot latency to 77.1 ms (a 2.47-fold speedup). Qualitative experimental results further indicate preferential attention to target objects and task destinations across perturbations.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Fixed Visual History: Learning Motion-Adaptive Temporal Receptive Fields for Complex-Terrain Quadruped Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14753)
*Hao Zhang,Yuping Yan,Yitian Hong,Luying Feng,Tianhong Wang,Fengqi Dai,Wei Chen,Yaochu Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TempoLoco：体感知动态视觉感知框架，根据机器人运动状态组织视觉记忆，提升四足机器人复杂地形移动性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉控制器用固定时间模式组织深度历史，忽略了相同时间跨度在不同速度、姿态、步态相位下对应不同空间范围，导致视觉历史在某种运动状态下不足、在另一种下则过时或错位。

Method: TempoLoco从长深度缓冲区学习多个互补时间视角，在共享特征空间中编码，并利用本体感知历史与多尺度地形证据进行动态聚合，使单一策略将视觉预判与当前运动能力对齐。

Result: 成功率78.4%，优于最强固定历史基线58.2%和均匀多尺度融合62.1%；九类地形仿真完成41/45次试验，Unitree B2真实世界完成23/25次试验。

Conclusion: 通过体感知动态视觉感知，实现稳健且可部署的四足机器人在楼梯、平台、间隙、离散障碍、崎岖地面和斜坡等复杂地形上的移动。

Abstract: Versatile quadruped locomotion depends on a tight coupling between what the robot perceives and what its body can execute. Yet existing visual controllers typically organize depth history using fixed temporal patterns, overlooking that the same temporal span corresponds to different spatial ranges as the robot's speed, posture, and gait phase change. Consequently, the same visual history may be insufficient in one motion state but outdated or misaligned in another. To address this limitation, we propose TempoLoco, a body-aware dynamic visual perception framework that organizes visual memory according to the robot's current locomotion state. TempoLoco learns multiple complementary temporal views from a long depth buffer, encodes them within a shared feature space, and dynamically aggregates them using proprioceptive history and multi-scale terrain evidence. This enables a single policy to align visual anticipation with the robot's current motion capabilities across complex terrains, including stairs, platforms, gaps, discrete obstacles, rough ground, and slopes. From the experiment analysis, TempoLoco achieves a 78.4% success rate, compared with 58.2% for the strongest fixed-history baseline and 62.1% for uniform multi-scale fusion. In separate capability evaluations, it completes 41/45 simulated trials across nine terrain types and 23/25 real-world trials on a Unitree B2 with a fixed arm, demonstrating robust and deployable locomotion through body-aware dynamic visual perception.

</details>


### [61] [Scaling Latent Motor Adaptation to Coordinated Bimanual Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14833)
*Seung-Bin Joo,Alessandro Albini,Luca Wiehe,Perla Maiolino,Daniel Olmeda Reino,João F. Henriques*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 双臂RMA通过观测-动作历史与双腕深度图像实现潜在条件化，在抓取与举升任务中达到特权策略性能并完成零微调仿真到现实迁移，但长时序堆叠任务仍受限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实机器人操作中物体质量、摩擦、尺度、形状存在不确定性，固定假设下训练的策略性能会退化。基于嵌入的自适应方法（如RMA）通过交互历史推断环境动力学潜表示来条件化动作，但该范式此前主要局限于单臂场景。本文关注其能否扩展到双臂协调操作，因为双臂引入更高维控制、耦合接触与共享物体受力等额外复杂性。

Method: 在三个接触密集的ManiSkill任务上评估双臂RMA，任务中随机化YCB物体属性。双臂RMA使用观测-动作历史与双腕深度图像作为输入，通过潜表示条件化动作；并与无潜条件化的匹配静态PPO策略进行对照。进一步在双Franka举升任务上直接进行仿真到现实迁移，不做真实世界微调。

Result: 双臂RMA在双臂抓取与举升任务上保持了特权策略的性能，而匹配的静态PPO策略成功率很低；所有方法在长时序堆叠任务上均表现受限。在双Franka举升任务上实现了零微调的仿真到现实迁移。

Conclusion: 潜条件化为将鲁棒控制扩展到协调双臂系统提供了有用的结构，但更长时序任务仍需额外的时序结构支持。

Abstract: Robust manipulation under uncertain object dynamics and geometry remains a core challenge in real-world robotics. Policies trained under fixed assumptions often degrade when object mass, friction, scale, or shape vary. Embedding-based adaptation methods such as Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA) address this by conditioning actions on latent representations of environment dynamics inferred from interaction history. We investigate whether this paradigm scales from single arm settings to coordinated bimanual manipulation, where higher dimensional control, coupled contacts, and shared object forces introduce additional complexity. We evaluate bimanual RMA on three contact-rich ManiSkill tasks with randomized YCB object properties. Bimanual RMA, using observation-action history and dual wrist depth images, retains privileged policy performance on bimanual picking and lifting, while an otherwise matched stationary PPO policy without latent conditioning achieves low success; all methods remain limited on long-horizon stacking. We further demonstrate direct sim-to-real transfer on a dual Franka lifting task without real-world finetuning. Together, these results suggest that latent conditioning provides useful structure for scaling robust control to coordinated bimanual systems, while longer horizon tasks require additional temporal structure.

</details>


### [62] [Push the Limit: Learning High-Speed Sprinting and Aggressive Cornering on a Full-Scale Humanoid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14840)
*Huaxing Huang,Ruidong Li,Jinchao Han,Ziyang Dong,Wenhao Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出学习框架，实现全尺寸人形机器人7 m/s以上冲刺与25°躯干倾斜高速急转弯，并通过MHA、航向奖励与技能条件MAMP解决高速振荡与技能保真问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约4 m/s以上周期性足部冲击引发高频偏航率振荡，破坏扁平MLP策略依赖的反馈历史；传统偏航率跟踪奖励与自然振荡动力学相冲突；单一共享风格先验无法区分指令技能，损害低速技能保真度。

Method: 采用因果多头注意力（MHA）显式建模高频时序反馈；设计只监督低频速度方向的航向奖励替代振荡偏航率；引入技能条件多对抗运动先验（MAMP）判别器，使风格奖励以指令技能为条件。

Result: 消融实验表明各机制均必要：以扁平历史MLP替代结构化时序编码会限制最高速度；恢复偏航率跟踪则无法高速转弯；单一共享判别器会使原地转弯退化为准静态站立。框架在仿真与真机上实现稳定高速冲刺与激进转弯。

Conclusion: 该框架弥合了原始冲刺速度与参考运动保真度之间的差距，兼顾高速机动性与运动先验保真度。

Abstract: We present a learning-based framework that enables a full-scale bipedal humanoid to sprint at over 7 m/s with motion-prior-guided gait and perform aggressive cornering at high speed with a pronounced trunk lean of up to $25^\circ$. Achieving this combination is challenging: above approximately 4 m/s, periodic foot impacts induce severe, high-frequency yaw-rate oscillations intrinsic to high-speed gaits. This high-frequency temporal structure corrupts the history of proprioceptive feedback that flat-MLP policies rely on, while rendering conventional yaw-rate tracking rewards counterproductive, as they fight against the robot's natural oscillatory dynamics. Moreover, a single style prior shared across skills cannot tell which skill is commanded and inherently compromises the low-speed skill fidelity. To overcome these limitations, we adopt a causal multi-head attention (MHA) module to explicitly model the high-frequency temporal feedback. In place of the oscillatory yaw rate, we design a heading-direction reward that supervises only the low-frequency velocity direction, allowing agile turning at high speed without suppressing natural gait oscillations. We further introduce skill-conditioned multi-adversarial-motion-prior (MAMP) discriminators that condition the style reward on the commanded skill. Ablation experiments confirm that each mechanism is essential: replacing structured temporal encoding with a flat-history MLP caps the top speed; reverting to yaw-rate tracking prevents high-speed turning; and a single shared discriminator makes in-place turning collapse into a quasi-static stance. Our framework bridges the gap between raw sprinting speed and reference-motion fidelity, demonstrating stable high-speed sprinting and aggressive cornering in both simulation and real-robot deployment.

</details>


### [63] [Learning Dynamic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14848)
*Nga Kiu Kelly Ho,Yilin Liu,Hao Xu,Niloy J. Mitra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究从单一初始手-物体状态生成完整4D抓取序列的欠约束问题，通过条件流匹配与免训练配准解耦手部运动生成和物体运动恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实感4D手-物体抓取序列对角色动画、扩展现实和具身智能体很重要。现有方法多只建模最终静态抓取，或依赖语言、目标抓取、物体轨迹等额外条件，限制了适用性。本文希望仅从单一初始手-物体状态生成完整抓取运动。

Method: 框架将手部运动生成与物体运动恢复解耦。输入初始腕坐标系下的物体点云和初始参数化手部配置，用条件流匹配预测序列级接触图和每个归一化时间的手部姿态；该时间索引式建模避免自回归展开的误差累积。随后推断附着坐标系，并通过免训练、接触引导的加权配准恢复刚体物体运动。

Result: 在DexYCB留出物体和Open-CHOIR从野外视频重建的未见几何上评估。相比LatentHOI，各设置下手和物体运动更平滑，共享初始化下野外接触与穿透指标持平或更好，每帧生成约快30倍。15人研究中约三分之二的强制选择成对比较更偏好本文运动。新几何上的精确接触闭合仍是挑战。

Conclusion: 该方法证明仅凭初始状态即可生成完整、平滑的4D抓取序列，并在速度与主观偏好上优于基线；但新物体几何上的精确接触闭合尚未解决，是未来关键方向。

Abstract: Synthesizing believable 4D hand-object grasping sequences is important for character animation, extended reality, and embodied agents. Grasping requires a hand to approach, contact, and lift or manipulate an object coherently. Existing synthesis methods often model only the final static grasp or rely on additional conditions, such as language, target grasps, or object trajectories, to specify the interaction. We instead study the underconstrained problem of generating a complete grasping motion from a single initial hand-object state. Our framework decouples hand-motion generation from object-motion recovery. Given an object point cloud in the initial wrist frame and the initial parameterized hand configuration, conditional flow matching predicts a sequence-level contact map and a hand pose at each normalized sequence time. This time-indexed formulation avoids the error accumulation of autoregressive rollout while representing the complete motion. We then infer an attachment frame and recover rigid-object motion through training-free, contact-guided weighted registration. We evaluate on held-out DexYCB objects and unseen object geometries reconstructed from in-the-wild videos by Open-CHOIR. Compared with LatentHOI, our method produces smoother hand and object motion in every setting, matches or improves on its contact and penetration metrics in the wild under a shared initialization, and is about 30x faster per generated frame. In a 15-participant study, our motions were preferred in roughly two thirds of (forced-choice pairwise) comparisons. Precise contact closure on novel geometry, however, remains a key open challenge.

</details>


### [64] [DEFT-VLA: Stage-Localized Force Specialization for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14854)
*Zan Huang,Yibo Qiu,Kan Ding,Xuanping Zhang,Ao Tian,Ronald X Xu,Mingzhai Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DEFT-VLA双专家框架，按接触阶段分配视觉与力觉感知，显著提升接触密集型操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触密集型操作存在阶段依赖的感知冲突：非接触阶段视觉与本体感觉关键、力信息无用甚至干扰；接触后力信息关键，而无关视觉细节和相关机器人状态可能成为捷径。需要将力条件适配定位到真正需要的阶段。

Method: DEFT-VLA保留任务适配的基础专家负责视觉主导的非接触行为，引入力感知的接触专家负责关键接触阶段，实现接触局部化的力条件适配。同时提出接触局部化力关注课程，仅对接触专家进行力关注后训练，逐步恢复视觉并掩蔽状态token以抑制捷径。

Result: 在枪头安装、双臂移液枪吸液和白板擦拭三个真机实验中，DEFT-VLA有效利用力反馈提升接触阶段表现，同时保持良好非接触行为和对未见抓取高度的鲁棒性。每个任务上总体成功率比最强基线提升20-40个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果表明，按接触阶段分离视觉与力觉专家并配合接触局部化训练，能有效缓解阶段依赖的感知冲突与捷径问题，为力反馈在VLA操作中的利用提供有效方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation poses a stage-dependent sensory conflict: vision and proprioception are essential for localization and approach, when force is largely uninformative and potentially distracting, whereas force becomes critical after contact, when irrelevant visual details and correlated robot states may become shortcuts. We propose DEFT-VLA (Dual-Expert Force-specialized Training for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation), which retains the task-adapted base expert for vision-dominated non-contact behavior and introduces a force-aware contact expert for critical contact stages, localizing force-conditioned adaptation to where it is needed. We further introduce a contact-localized force-attending curriculum that applies force-attending post-training only to the contact expert, progressively restoring vision and masking state tokens to suppress shortcuts. Real-robot experiments on pipette tip attachment, bimanual pipette aspiration, and whiteboard wiping show that DEFT-VLA effectively exploits force feedback to improve contact-stage performance while maintaining strong non-contact behavior and robustness to held-out grasp heights. It improves overall task success by 20-40 percentage points over the strongest baseline on each task.

</details>


### [65] [Predict in Chunks, Revise in Spans: History-conditioned Action Regeneration for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14864)
*Chenyang Wang,Yuntian Wang,Jiaying Tang,Chunzheng Li,Yang Liu,Siao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CMAP 提出一种基于历史条件局部再生成的视觉运动策略，通过不确定性引导的片段重构来细化动作块，在多个仿真与真实任务中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作分块方法在预测未来动作序列时提升了时间连贯性，但许多策略在误差仅局部出现时仍会重新生成整个动作块，可能替换掉原本有效的预测，造成不必要的整体重算。

Method: 提出 Contextual Masked Action Policy（CMAP）。其核心是历史条件动作带：将已执行命令与掩码的未来位置结合，生成粗动作预测及每步不确定性分数；随后由不确定性引导的片段再生成机制选取高不确定性的连续片段，并利用已执行历史和保留的未来预测作为上下文进行重构。通过联合重构相邻动作建模局部时间依赖，同时保留所选片段之外的所有预测。

Result: 在九个仿真任务和四个真实世界操作任务上，CMAP 的整体性能优于多个竞争基线；消融研究验证了关键组件的互补贡献；留出轨迹分析表明局部再生成能降低所选片段内的预测误差。

Conclusion: CMAP 通过历史条件的局部片段再生成，而非整体重算动作块，有效提升了视觉运动模仿学习的性能，并保持了片段外的有效预测。

Abstract: Action chunking improves temporal coherence in visuomotor imitation learning by jointly predicting sequences of future actions. However, many existing policies regenerate entire action chunks even when errors are localized, potentially replacing otherwise valid predictions. We introduce Contextual Masked Action Policy (CMAP), a visuomotor policy that refines continuous action chunks through history-conditioned local regeneration. Specifically, CMAP introduces a history-conditioned action tape that combines executed commands with masked future positions to produce coarse action predictions and per-step uncertainty scores. An uncertainty-guided span regeneration mechanism then selects a high-uncertainty contiguous span and reconstructs it using the executed history and retained future predictions as context. By jointly reconstructing neighboring actions, CMAP models local temporal dependencies while preserving all predictions outside the selected span. Extensive experiments on nine simulated and four real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate that CMAP achieves superior overall performance over several competitive baselines. Ablation studies validate the complementary contributions of its key components, while held-out trajectory analysis shows that local regeneration reduces prediction errors within selected spans.

</details>


### [66] [Verify, Act, Distill: Turning Test-Time Compute into Test-Time Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14885)
*Kaneyoshi Hiratsuka,Satoshi Yamamori,Jun Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出"验证-执行-蒸馏"(Verify, Act, Distill)框架，将测试时计算(TTC)的增益通过蒸馏固化进VLA模型本身，无需强化学习或专门教师rollout，显著提升单候选生成的执行成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTC方法在推理时对多个候选动作打分以提升VLA模型执行性能，但策略始终保持冻结，导致每次执行都必须重新计算增益，效率低下且无法累积改进。作者希望将TTC的增益内化到VLA模型中。

Method: 固定验证器对flow-matching VLA生成的候选动作打分，执行加权聚合结果；将成功适应回合的观测和聚合动作块直接用标准flow-matching损失蒸馏进LoRA参数，复用TTC执行中已产生的监督信号，无需额外强化学习或专门生成教师的rollout。

Result: 在LIBERO 40个任务上，以SmolVLA 5k检查点，每任务仅用10个回合蒸馏，可将无验证器单候选生成的平均成功率从54.1%提升至70.6%，保留约74%的八候选聚合增益。对蒸馏后策略再施加聚合，成功率从76.4%升至82.3%，在LIBERO-Long上提升尤为显著(44.0%→57.8%)，单候选策略在含分布偏移的LIBERO-plus上也有改进。

Conclusion: 将验证作为学习信号而非仅作执行时选择器，既能保留TTC的改进效果，又能再次放大后续TTC的作用，证明了TTC增益可被有效固化进VLA模型。

Abstract: Test-time compute (TTC), which scores multiple candidate actions at inference time, is a powerful way to improve the execution performance of vision-language-action (VLA) models, but existing methods keep the policy frozen, so the gain must be recomputed at every execution. We propose Verify, Act, Distill, a framework that consolidates the gains of TTC into the VLA itself. A fixed verifier scores candidates generated by a flow-matching VLA, the weighted aggregate is executed, and the observations and aggregated chunks from successful adaptation episodes are distilled directly into LoRA parameters with the standard flow-matching loss. This reuses supervision that TTC has already produced during execution, without additional reinforcement learning or rollouts dedicated to teacher generation. On the 40 LIBERO tasks with a SmolVLA 5k checkpoint, distilling from only 10 episodes per task raises the mean success rate of verifier-free single-candidate generation from 54.1% to 70.6%, retaining about 74% of the improvement obtained by aggregating eight candidates. Applying the same aggregation to the distilled policy further raises the success rate from 76.4% to 82.3%, with a particularly large gain on LIBERO-Long (44.0% to 57.8%), and the single-candidate policy also improves on LIBERO-plus, which contains distribution shifts. These results show that using verification as a learning signal, rather than only as an execution-time selector, both retains the improvements of TTC and re-amplifies the effect of subsequent TTC.

</details>


### [67] [LETS: Low-Overhead External-Torque Sensing for Force-Feedback Bilateral Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14889)
*Zonghao Guo,Dongchen Guan,Yubo Wang,Peng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LETS是一种物理引导学习的外力矩估计方法，将刚体动力学交由递归牛顿-欧拉逆动力学处理，仅用学习补偿机器人特定残差，仅需URDF和关节反馈，在双侧遥操作中实现稳定力反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双侧力反馈遥操作对外力矩估计质量要求高，但商用机械臂厂商提供的扰动扭矩估计常不准确，而加装末端六维力/扭矩传感器会增加成本与负载。

Method: LETS将刚体动力学分配给递归牛顿-欧拉逆动力学，仅对机器人特定残差使用学习；仅使用机器人URDF模型和关节反馈，刻意排除控制指令以将外力矩估计与控制输入解耦。使用12分钟自由运动数据，训练时间不到1分钟，无需专用末端六轴力/扭矩传感器。

Result: 在双侧遥操作中实现实时外力矩估计并支持稳定力反馈；自由空间误差比厂商估计低69%，显著减少主从间耦合振荡；接触误差低28%，触觉反馈更准确。

Conclusion: LETS以物理引导学习方式，在低成本、无额外传感器的条件下显著提升外力矩估计精度，改善双侧遥操作的稳定性与触觉反馈质量。

Abstract: Bilateral teleoperation with force feedback is becoming increasingly important, and the quality of external-torque estimation is critical to this process. However, manufacturer-provided disturbance-torque estimates on commercial robotic arms are often inaccurate, while the use of end-effector force/torque sensors adds cost and payload. We propose LETS, a physics-guided learning approach that assigns rigid-body dynamics to recursive Newton-Euler inverse dynamics and reserves learning for only the robot-specific residual. LETS uses only the robot's URDF model and joint feedback, deliberately excluding control commands and thereby decoupling external-torque estimation from control-command inputs. It trains on 12 minutes of free-motion data in under one minute, without requiring a dedicated end-effector six-axis force/torque sensor. In bilateral teleoperation, LETS achieves real-time external-torque estimation that enables stable force feedback. Its free-space error is 69% lower than the manufacturer-provided estimate, substantially reducing coupled oscillations between leader and follower; its contact error is 28% lower, yielding more accurate haptic feedback.

</details>


### [68] [Test-Time Compute for Humanoid Behavior Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14896)
*Weiji Xie,Junsong Wu,Zibo Zhou,Yueru Chen,Xingyi Wang,Zhenhua Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出测试时命令优化（TTCO），无需重训练即可缩小人形行为基础模型的指令-执行差距，通过采样优化找到适配特定BFM的输入命令。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形行为基础模型（BFMs）虽能执行出色动作，但在loco-manipulation等下游任务中需要高全身位置精度，现有BFM存在系统性的指令-执行差距，即执行动作偏离期望动作。

Method: TTCO在测试时通过采样优化，在BFM与机器人动力学的闭环仿真中搜索能产生更接近期望轨迹的输入命令，无需重训练BFM；还分析了测试时计算扩展性，推导近似计算最优策略；并可从记录的机器人轨迹生成BFM专属动作标签。

Result: 在AMASS数据集上对三个BFM测试，TTCO在仿真中将平均全局身体位置误差降低79-84%；随计算预算增加性能可持续提升；生成的标签可有效训练高层loco-manipulation策略，并支持跨BFM复用现有示范。

Conclusion: 额外的测试时计算可在不重训练BFM的情况下改善执行精度，TTCO还可生成动作标签用于策略训练，并支持跨模型复用示范数据。

Abstract: Humanoid Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) can execute impressive motions, but downstream applications such as loco-manipulation demand high whole-body positional accuracy. Existing BFMs suffer from a systematic command-execution gap, where executed motions deviate from desired ones. We show that additional test-time compute can improve execution without retraining the BFM. First, we introduce Test-Time Command Optimization (TTCO), which adapts the input command to the specific BFM to produce an executed trajectory closer to the desired motion. TTCO finds such commands by sampling-based optimization through closed-loop simulations of the BFM and robot dynamics. Across three BFMs on the AMASS dataset, TTCO reduces mean global body-position error by 79-84% in simulation. Second, we analyze test-time compute scaling and derive an approximate compute-optimal strategy that can sustain performance gains as the compute budget increases. Third, TTCO can generate BFM-specific action labels from recorded robot trajectories without requiring the original teleoperation body-motion commands. Simulation and real-robot experiments show that these labels are effective for training high-level loco-manipulation policies. This method also supports reusing existing demonstrations across BFMs.

</details>


### [69] [Scalable Multi-Human Cooperative Manipulation via Commander-Executor Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14911)
*Buzhen Huang,Xingchen Wu,Yuanwang Yang,Chenyi Xie,Kun Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层指挥官-执行器框架，实现多智能体角色依赖、长时序协同物体操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形控制方法只能处理简单协同搬运，难以应对角色依赖、长时序、序列化的协同操作任务。

Method: 指挥官结合指令与场景信息进行语义和几何联合规划，将任务分解为角色特化的子任务，生成以物体为中心的共享协调计划（执行时序、目标位置、操作动作）；执行器基于高层策略与局部观测，通过单人到协同的课程学习训练底层物理策略，以物体轨迹作为共享协调目标，并用根位置与抓取指令指定角色依赖的执行线索。

Result: 在多种物体操作任务上验证，可执行组合不同协作类型的长时序任务，并可从双智能体扩展到多智能体；实验显示成功率优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 分层指挥官-执行器框架有效解决复杂多人协同操作问题，具备可扩展性与更优性能。

Abstract: Multi-human cooperative object manipulation is a highly challenging topic in humanoid control. While existing methods can handle simple collaborative carrying tasks, they struggle with role-dependent, long-horizon, and sequential cooperative manipulation. To address this issue, we propose a hierarchical commander-executor framework that enables multiple agents to accomplish complex collaborative tasks in distinct ways. Specifically, a commander combines instructions and scene information for joint semantic and geometric planning. It then decomposes the task into role-specific subtasks and generates shared object-centric coordination plans, specifying execution timing, target positions, and manipulation actions for each agent. Conditioned on the commander's high-level strategy and each agent's local observations, the executor learns a low-level physics-based policy through a solo-to-cooperative curriculum to realize coordinated human-object interactions. The planned object trajectory provides a shared coordination target, while root positions and grasping instructions specify role-dependent execution cues. We evaluate our method on a variety of object manipulation tasks, demonstrating its ability to execute long-horizon tasks that combine different types of collaboration and to scale from two-agent to multi-agent cooperation. Extensive experiments further show that our approach achieves higher success rates compared to state-of-the-art methods. Code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/TJU-3DV/Co-Manipulation.

</details>


### [70] [Funnel planning and control for manipulators using neural Signed Configuration Distance Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14917)
*Bernhard Wullt,Christos K. Verginis,Per Mattsson,Mikael Norrlöf,Thomas B. Schön*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于神经符号配置距离函数（nSCDF）的漏斗规划与控制方法，高效估计无碰撞球与梯度，实现机械臂鲁棒运动规划，速度提升2倍、路径缩短20%、间隙提升5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KDF规划器虽具鲁棒性与无碰撞保证，但漏斗计算代价高，限制了其表达能力和性能，因此需要一种更高效的计算方案。

Method: 提出nSCDF（神经符号配置距离函数）高效估计无碰撞球与梯度；用其规划兼顾路径长度与间隙的标称轨迹，并设计利用nSCDF几何信息的漏斗控制器进行跟踪。

Result: 数值实验中，规划器产生标称路径速度提升2倍，路径长度缩短20%，间隙提升5倍；漏斗控制下运动性能与鲁棒性均有所改善。

Conclusion: nSCDF有效克服了现有漏斗计算的计算瓶颈，在保证无碰撞与鲁棒性的同时显著提升规划效率与控制性能。

Abstract: Robust motion planning and control for manipulators is the problem of guaranteeing safe execution and collision-free motion under disturbances. The Kinodynamic Motion Planning via Funnel Control (KDF) is a recently proposed planner that comes with robustness guarantees and is particularly attractive for collision-free control as it guarantees closed-loop evolution inside a funnel, i.e., a collision-free region around a nominal trajectory. Despite its attractive theoretical properties, the current approach for computing funnels is computationally demanding, which limits its expressiveness and performance. We address its current limitations by proposing a novel funnel planning and control solution. Our key tool to enable this is a neural Signed Configuration Distance Function (nSCDF) which allows us to estimate collision-free balls and gradients in a computationally efficient way. We use the nSCDF in our planner to compute a nominal trajectory, where path length and clearance are balanced. To track the trajectory with collision-avoidance guarantees, we design a funnel controller that exploits the geometric information from the nSCDF. From numerical experiments, we observe that our planner produces nominal paths $2$ times faster, while at the same time obtaining $20 \%$ shorter path length and $5$ times the clearance. Finally, we also observe improved motion performance and robustness properties when using the nSCDF for funnel control.

</details>


### [71] [Edge-Native Temporal--Graph Dual-Expert Inference for Dexterous Grasp Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14926)
*Jiajian Zheng,Bin Zhang,Wen Fan,Yanan Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出边缘原生流水线，仅用内置关节角度与执行器负载反馈，实现逐指独立Stop预测与物体识别，Flash部署精度93.78%、Stop F1 95.25%，功耗约0.80W。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多指手在不同时刻接触物体，全局闭合指令会使先接触的手指过载，而其余手指仍在运动。密集触觉阵列虽可缓解该问题，但带来硬件、标定、带宽与计算开销。因此需要一种无需额外触觉传感器、可在边缘端运行的方案。

Method: 仅使用内置关节角度和预使用标定的执行器负载反馈；取四个样本及其一阶差分构成4×20接触演化窗口。核心为异构双专家架构DualExpert：TCN建模短期接触演化并输出全部五个Stop概率，FGFN编码手指拓扑仅参与物体分类；采用固定0.7/0.3类别概率融合。

Result: 225次留出试验上轨迹级分类精度93.78%，较单独TCN与FGFN分别提升4.00与2.22个百分点；TCN Stop路径F1为95.25%。数据集含五个物体实例的1500条自动采集轨迹。Flash部署中混合加速器执行保持93.78%分类精度与94.84% Stop F1，平均推理2.890ms，平均采样到停止延迟9.172ms。50次实物试验中识别率96.00%、抓取成功率98.00%，整板功耗约0.80W。

Conclusion: 无需密集触觉阵列，仅凭内置本体反馈即可在边缘设备上实现逐指停止预测与物体识别，兼顾精度、延迟与功耗，具备实用部署价值。

Abstract: Multi-finger hands contact objects at different times, so a global close command can overload early-contact fingers while others are still moving. Dense tactile arrays address this problem but add hardware, calibration, bandwidth, and computation. We present an edge-native pipeline that uses only built-in joint-angle and pre-use-calibrated actuator-load feedback to recognize the grasped object and predict an independent Stop event for each finger. Four samples and their first differences form a 4-by-20 contact-evolution window. At its core is a heterogeneous dual-expert architecture, termed DualExpert, that assigns complementary tasks to a Temporal Convolutional Network (TCN) and a Finger Graph Filter Network (FGFN). The TCN models short-term contact evolution and supplies all five Stop probabilities, while the FGFN encodes finger topology and contributes only to object classification. Fixed 0.7/0.3 class-probability fusion reaches 93.78\% trajectory-level classification accuracy on 225 held-out trials, improving the standalone TCN and FGFN by 4.00 and 2.22 percentage points, respectively. The TCN Stop path attains 95.25\% F1. The dataset contains 1,500 automatically collected trajectories from five object instances. In Flash deployment, mixed accelerator execution retains 93.78\% classification accuracy and 94.84\% Stop F1 with 2.890~ms mean inference time and 9.172~ms mean sample-to-stop latency. Across 50 physical trials, the system achieves 96.00\% recognition and 98.00\% grasp success while the complete embedded board draws approximately 0.80~W.

</details>


### [72] [CFG-Policy: Unified Diffusion Policy Learning Framework via Classifier-free Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14936)
*Shutong Ding,Zhaoyang Liu,Yimiao Zhou,Jingya Wang,Jiayuan Gu,Ye Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CFG-Policy，将探索与利用分离为两个生成策略，通过无分类器引导合并其分数/速度场，统一在线与离线到在线的扩散强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在机器人控制、视觉-语言-动作模型和世界模型智能体中日益流行，其多模态动作分布有利于RL探索。但用RL优化扩散策略会降低行为多样性、削弱探索能力；直接微调预训练策略会破坏已有能力；动作空间残差又缺乏多模态探索的表达力。核心挑战在于：提升任务性能的同时保留行为多样性与持续适应所需的能力。

Method: CFG-Policy是一个统一、即插即用的框架，把探索和利用拆分为两个生成策略，并在采样时通过无分类器引导（classifier-free guidance）合并它们的分数场或速度场。在线RL中，冻结的探索分支维持行为多样性，利用分支从任务奖励中学习；离线到在线RL中，预训练的利用分支保持冻结，由可训练分支引导组合行为。该框架保持原有RL目标不变。

Result: 在5个MuJoCo任务上配合QSM和QVPO提升了性能；在4个RoboTwin操作任务上配合Flow-SDE+PPO提升了性能。

Conclusion: CFG-Policy通过生成式策略的解耦与引导组合，在提升任务表现的同时保留行为多样性和既有能力，为在线与离线到在线扩散RL提供统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Diffusion policies are increasingly used in robotic control and manipulation, vision-language-action models, and world-model-based agents. Their ability to represent multimodal action distributions offers rich opportunities for exploration in reinforcement learning (RL). However, optimizing diffusion policies with RL can reduce the behavioral diversity and further limit their exploration capability. In RL post-training, directly fine-tuning a pretrained policy may also disrupt its acquired capabilities, while action-space residuals may lack the expressive power for multimodal exploration. Together, these issues reveal a common challenge across online and offline-to-online diffusion RL: improving task performance while retaining the behavioral diversity and capabilities needed for continued adaptation. We introduce CFG-Policy, a unified, plug-and-play framework that separates exploration and exploitation into two generative policies and combines their score or velocity fields during sampling through classifier-free guidance. For online RL, a frozen exploration branch sustains behavioral diversity while an exploitation branch learns from task rewards; for offline-to-online RL, a pretrained exploitation branch remains frozen while a trainable branch steers the composed behavior. CFG-Policy preserves the underlying RL objectives and improves performance with QSM and QVPO on 5 MuJoCo tasks and with Flow-SDE+PPO on 4 RoboTwin manipulation tasks.
