Project page is at https://zhy-liu.github.io/cfg-policy-page.

</details>


### [73] [FoundationVIO: Enforcing Metric Scale in Feed-forward Visual-Inertial Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14951)
*Jin Rhee,Yuhao Zhang,Frank Dellaert,Maurice Fallon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FoundationVIO 将惯性传感与几何基础模型（GFM）预测结合，通过因子图方法恢复度量尺度，实现接近经典 VIO 精度的里程计与稠密重建，并加入轻量回环检测构成完整视觉-惯性 SLAM 系统，可在消费级笔记本 GPU 上近实时运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFM 能直接预测稠密几何和相机位姿，但存在几何尺度模糊问题：独立预测仅局部一致（up-to-scale），缺乏物理测量约束；即便在已知尺度数据上训练也只能提升一致性而非直接测量尺度，导致基于 GFM 的 SLAM 轨迹与重建继承了这一问题。作者受经典视觉-惯性里程计启发，希望借助惯性传感恢复度量尺度。

Method: 提出 FoundationVIO，将惯性传感与 GFM 预测集成，采用基于因子图的方法恢复度量尺度重建；并加入一个极简的、基于 GFM 的回环检测机制，构建完整的视觉-惯性 SLAM 系统，实现全局一致的位姿估计与重建。

Result: 在三个具有知名传感器系统的数据集上评估，里程计估计可与经典最先进 VIO 方法竞争，稠密重建精度优于基于 GFM 的 SLAM 方法；方法兼容常见相机与 IMU 传感器，在消费级笔记本 GPU 上以约实时速度运行。

Conclusion: 通过融合惯性传感，FoundationVIO 有效解决了 GFM 尺度模糊问题，构建出全局精确、近实时、且对通用相机-IMU 硬件友好的视觉-惯性 SLAM 系统。

Abstract: Feed-forward geometric foundation models (GFMs) directly predict dense geometry and camera poses from batches of images. Emerging visual SLAM methods chain together consecutive GFM batches to form dense point cloud maps. A core challenge is how to address the ambiguous geometric scale of GFM predictions: independent predictions are locally consistent up-to-scale but not constrained by any direct physical measurements. Models trained on data with known scale improve the consistency of GFM reconstructions but again do not directly measure it. Consequently, trajectories and 3D reconstructions from GFM-enabled SLAM methods inherit this issue. Motivated by parallels with classical visual-inertial odometry, we introduce FoundationVIO which integrates inertial sensing with GFM predictions to recover metric scale reconstruction using a factor-graph based approach. We demonstrate odometry estimates competitive with classical state-of-the-art VIO methods and accurate dense reconstructions that outperform GFM-enabled SLAM methods. We also add a minimal GFM-based loop-closure mechanism to create a full Visual-Inertial SLAM system capable of creating globally accurate reconstructions and pose estimates. We evaluate our method on three datasets with well known sensor systems and demonstrate its compatibility on ubiquitous camera and IMU sensors. The method runs at approximately real-time on a consumer-grade laptop GPU. Code: https://github.com/ori-drs/FoundationVIO.

</details>


### [74] [EnactOpt: Diagnosis-Guided Harness Evolution for Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14977)
*ZhiYuan Feng,Zhuoshi Pan,Zhaolu Kang,Zhongxia Chen,Shuo Yang,Sen Ye,Heng Wang,Han Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EnactOpt 通过联合优化可复用的文本技能与上下文构建代码，在不更新模型参数的情况下，利用有限执行反馈提升具身智能体的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于基础模型的具身智能体依赖执行框架（execution harness）组织观察、引导动作并整合执行反馈。失败不仅源于模型能力不足，也源于框架对证据的呈现与使用方式。这些模型外部机制可成为无需更新参数的适应目标，但任务级结果无法区分"证据缺失"还是"证据未被用好"，且二者相互耦合，加之任务特定经验收集成本高昂。

Method: 提出 EnactOpt：利用有限执行反馈，在不改变底层模型与运行时的前提下，联合演化可复用文本技能和上下文构建代码。方法通过将动作反馈与实际模型输入及后续动作关联，对相邻决策进行诊断；利用结构化情节摘要，通过提示级编辑算子引导联合修订；候选框架经过兼容性检查与基于验证的保留筛选。

Result: 在 VLABench、ALFWorld 和 Embodied Agent Interface（EAI）上，EnactOpt 相比初始框架将任务成功率提升约 10-27 个百分点，并优于所评估的 GEPA、TextGrad 和 Trace2Skill 基线。增益可泛化至未用于框架演化或候选选择的任务条件。

Conclusion: 结果表明了一条互补的具身适应路径：通过联合精炼"证据如何呈现"与"如何据其行动"，将有限执行经验转化为可复用的行为改进。

Abstract: Embodied agents built on foundation models rely on an execution harness to organize observations, guide actions, and incorporate execution feedback into subsequent decisions. Failures can arise not only from limited model capabilities, but also from how the harness presents evidence and guides its use. These model-external mechanisms provide an adaptation target without updating model parameters, but task-specific interaction experience can be costly to collect. Task-level outcomes alone do not reveal whether relevant evidence was missing from the model input or was available but inadequately used. Moreover, these two aspects are coupled: effective action guidance may require evidence that the current context omits, while exposing that evidence alone does not ensure an appropriate response. We introduce EnactOpt, which uses limited execution feedback to jointly evolve reusable textual skills and context-construction code while keeping the underlying models and runtime fixed. It diagnoses adjacent decisions by relating action feedback to the actual model input and subsequent action. Structured episode summaries guide joint revisions through prompt-level edit operators, and candidate harnesses undergo compatibility checks and validation-based retention. Across VLABench, ALFWorld, and Embodied Agent Interface (EAI), EnactOpt improves task success by approximately 10-27 percentage points over the initial harnesses and outperforms the evaluated GEPA, TextGrad, and Trace2Skill baselines. These gains extend to task conditions not used for harness evolution or candidate selection. The results support a complementary route to embodied adaptation: turning limited execution experience into reusable behavioral improvements by jointly refining how evidence is presented and acted upon.

</details>


### [75] [Barrier-Constrained Guidance For Real-time Asynchronous VLA Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.14997)
*Wenbo Yu,Jifan Li,Wenke Xia,Yuchen Li,Yizhuo Zhang,Pei Ren,Zhengping Che,Di hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FreeBar，一种免训练、低开销的实时推理插件，将基于流匹配的VLA异步执行中的动作块对齐问题转化为CBF引导的动作空间约束，闭式求解修正，显著提升成功率并降低时延。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步执行使VLA模型能在执行中预测下一动作块以实现实时操作，但会与已提交动作流产生边界错位。现有延迟感知方案需大量重训练、改架构或固定前缀修补，在低延迟需求下计算开销过大。

Method: FreeBar无需训练，将flow-based VLA的块对齐建模为barrier引导的动作空间约束。它引入受Control Barrier Function启发的相邻动作块一致性约束，并以闭式解直接求解修正，避免通过VLA动作头反向传播；在生成过程中逐步收紧一致性界，兼顾早期生成灵活性与块内未来动作自由度。

Result: 在Kinetix、RoboCasa及多种真实机器人平台、多个VLA骨干上，相比朴素异步执行平均提升成功率10%–15%，高动态场景增益更大；保持实时效率，每动作块推理时延比RTC降低超45%；作为通用插件在不同延迟环境、基础策略和超参数下均保持稳健。

Conclusion: FreeBar以极低额外推理开销实现鲁棒的实时异步执行，兼顾对齐精度、生成灵活性与计算效率，具有良好的通用性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: While asynchronous execution enables VLA models to operate in real-time environments by predicting the next chunk during execution, it inevitably introduces boundary misalignment with the committed action stream. Existing delay-aware solutions address this at the prohibitive cost of extensive retraining, architectural modifications, or fixed-prefix inpainting that inflates computation when low latency is vital. To address these problems, we propose FreeBar, a training-free real-time inference plugin that enables robust asynchronous execution with only marginal additional inference cost by casting chunk alignment in flow-based VLAs as barrier-guided action-space constraint. Specifically, FreeBar introduces a lightweight Control Barrier Function (CBF)-inspired consistency constraint between consecutive action chunks and solves the resulting correction in closed form, avoiding costly backpropagation through the VLA action head. By progressively tightening the consistency bound during generation, FreeBar aligns generated chunks with the committed execution stream while preserving early-stage generative and within-chunk future action flexibility,. Across Kinetix, RoboCasa, and diverse real-world robotic platforms with multiple VLA backbones, FreeBar improves success rates over naive asynchronous execution by 10%--15% on average, with larger gains in highly dynamic scenarios. It also preserves real-time computational efficiency, decreasing inference latency per action chunk over 45% than RTC. Additionally, as a universal plugin, FreeBar remains robust across distinct latency environments, base policies, and hyperparameter settings.

</details>


### [76] [Design and Evaluation of a Collaborative Teleoperation Interface for Multi-Operator Multi-Robot Systems in Challenging Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15001)
*Alperen Kenan,Juan José García Cárdenas,Adriana Tapus,Paul Bremner,Manuel Giuliani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究设计并评估了一种共享可视化协同遥操作界面，通过16人对比实验证明：同地+共享界面条件在任务完成时间、碰撞次数和情境意识上均最优，物理共处与共享界面分别有助于降低认知负荷和提升相互感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人系统在复杂、难以进入的环境中执行任务时至关重要，但当前部署通常为每个机器人配备一名操作员，协作依赖操作员之间的沟通，异构平台接口不兼容，需人工协调。这种依赖操作员的方式增加了认知负荷，并限制了不同厂商机器人系统的可扩展性。因此需要一种改进的协同遥操作界面来提升操作员协调、情境意识和任务绩效。

Method: 开展16名参与者的对比用户研究，比较三种遥操作条件：同地操作员+共享界面、远程操作员+共享界面、同地操作员+独立界面。参与者在核电站数字孪生环境中控制两个仿真机器人完成灾难响应任务，并记录绩效指标、生理数据和行为测量，以评估任务绩效、认知负荷和操作员意识。

Result: 同地+共享界面条件任务完成最快（292.8秒），优于远程操作（351.6秒）和独立界面（385.1秒），碰撞次数最少（每试次0.9次），情境意识准确率最高（91.1%）。物理共处与认知负荷降低相关，NASA-TLX分数从同地的38.2升至远程操作的57.7，生理指标也一致支持该结论。

Conclusion: 共处（co-presence）与共享界面各自提供互补优势：共处有助于调节工作负荷，共享界面有助于提升相互意识，二者共同支持多操作员、多机器人遥操作。

Abstract: Multi-robot systems that combine the complementary capabilities of specialised platforms are critical for complex missions in challenging, inaccessible environments. Current deployments assign an operator or operator team to each robot, with collaboration relying on operator-to-operator communication to manually coordinate heterogeneous platforms with incompatible interfaces. This operator-dependent approach increases cognitive load and limits scalability across robots from different manufacturers. This study designs and evaluates a shared-visualisation collaborative teleoperation interface to improve operator coordination, situational awareness, and the task performance in multi-operator, multi-robot systems.
  A comparative user study with 16 participants compared three teleoperation conditions: co-located operators with a shared interface, remote operators with a shared interface, and co-located operators with individual interfaces. Participants controlled two simulated robots in a nuclear power plant digital twin to complete disaster response tasks. Performance metrics, physiological data, and behavioural measures were recorded to assess task performance, cognitive load, and operator awareness across conditions. The co-located shared-interface condition achieved the fastest mission completion (292.8 s), outperforming remote operation (351.6 s) and individual interfaces (385.1 s), with the fewest collisions (0.9 per trial) and highest situational awareness accuracy (91.1 %). Physical co-presence was associated with reduced cognitive load, with NASA-TLX scores increasing from 38.2 in co-located to 57.7 in remote operation, supported by consistent physiological indicators. These results demonstrate that co-presence and shared interfaces provide complementary benefits, supporting workload regulation and mutual awareness respectively in multi-operator, multi-robot teleoperation.

</details>


### [77] [Training-Free Multi-Agent Cooperative Embodied Search from Uncertain Vision-Language Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15038)
*Fan Pu,Keshu Wu,Jiwan Jiang,Shiyu Shen,Zhiwen Fan,Zhengzhong Tu,Jiachen Li,Yang Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种轻量、免训练、不确定性感知的多智能体多目标搜索框架，将高层语义推理与底层执行分离，用图优化实现协同导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实视觉语言导航在部分可观测下，地图结构、语义线索和目标位置可能不完整或被基础模型错误推断，需要既鲁棒又无需任务特定训练的协同搜索方法。

Method: MLLM仅作为假设生成器，提出语义区域、目标位置线索和可通行连接；这些输出作为不确定先验，用在线观测和图上下文证据精炼。协同导航建模为滚动时域多智能体混合整数线性规划，采用字典序目标：先最大化目标导向奖励，再减少行程、不确定边使用和重复访问。

Result: 在100个Matterport3D测试用例上，目标覆盖率和搜索效率优于代表性基线，共享信息与联合规划使各智能体负载更均衡。

Conclusion: 框架在抽象图上运行，不依赖具身特定策略参数，可扩展至不同智能体与目标数量，泛化到未见环境，并适配不同感知模块和底层控制器而无需任务特定重训练。

Abstract: Real-world Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires agents to act under partial observability, where map structure, semantic cues, and target locations may be incomplete or incorrectly inferred by foundation models. This paper presents a lightweight, training-free, and uncertainty-aware framework for cooperative multi-agent multi-target search in mapless environments. Instead of learning an end-to-end navigation policy, the framework separates high-level semantic reasoning from low-level execution through a scalable graph-optimization backbone. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) serve only as hypothesis generators, proposing semantic zones, target-location cues, and possible navigable connections. These outputs are treated as uncertain priors and further refined using online observations and graph-context evidence. Cooperative navigation is then formulated as a rolling-horizon multi-agent mixed-integer linear program with a lexicographic objective that first maximizes target-directed reward and then reduces travel distance, uncertain-edge use, and repeated visits. Experiments derived from 100 Matterport3D test cases show that the proposed framework improves target coverage and search efficiency over representative baseline methods, while shared information and joint planning enable more balanced workloads across agents. By operating on an abstract graph without relying on embodiment-specific policy parameters, the framework scales across varying numbers of agents and targets, generalizes to unseen environments, and accommodates different perception modules and low-level controllers without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [78] [Analytic Robot Self-Model: Learning Screw-Axis Kinematics for Rendering and Model-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15558)
*Chen Xu,Ning Tan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种显式解析机器人自建模方法，无需形变网络或URDF/CAD模板，可从多视角图像与关节读数中恢复度量化的螺旋轴参数，得到闭式正运动学与解析雅可比，并在仿真与真实机械臂上验证精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉自建模方法多隐式编码运动学，不暴露可用于计算和控制的度量关节参数；且图像拟合良好并不等于运动学准确。

Method: 利用多视角图像与关节读数建立高斯外观并为刚性连杆分配提供运动证据，再用稀疏末端执行器位姿独立辨识度量螺旋轴，从而得到闭式正运动学与解析雅可比。

Result: 在仿真七轴臂、十二关节四足和真实六轴臂上验证；仿真实现毫米级到达、抓放和四足闭环行走；真实臂末端预测中位误差6.4 mm（标定名义模型11.7 mm）；对照实验显示两模型PSNR仅差0.54 dB，但末端预测误差相差13.7倍。

Conclusion: 显式解析自模型可在外推范围评估并支持控制，且证明单纯图像拟合不足以保证运动学准确。

Abstract: Visual self-modelling lets robots learn body models from observations, but such models usually encode kinematics implicitly rather than exposing metric joint parameters for computation and control. Moreover, a good fit to images does not by itself establish accurate kinematics. Here we present an explicit analytic robot self-model that requires neither deformation networks nor URDF/CAD templates. Multi-view images and joint readings establish Gaussian appearance and provide motion evidence for rigid link assignments, whereas sparse end-effector poses independently identify metric screw axes. The resulting closed-form forward kinematics and analytic Jacobian can be evaluated beyond the fitting ranges. We validate the model on a simulated seven-axis arm, a simulated twelve-joint quadruped and a real six-axis arm. In simulation, it achieves millimetre-level reaching and pick-and-place, as well as closed-loop quadruped walking. On the real arm, it renders held-out configurations, predicts end-effector positions with a median error of 6.4 mm, compared with 11.7 mm for a calibrated nominal model, and supports reaching with external marker feedback. A controlled comparison shows that image fit alone is not enough: two Panda models differing only in their joint axes are 0.54 dB apart in PSNR, yet their end-effector prediction errors differ 13.7-fold.

</details>


### [79] [Stereo-Planner: Learning Stereo-to-Trajectory Planning for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15560)
*Hongming Shen,Junjie Lu,Xun Chen,Wei Wang,Xinhang Xu,Bailing Tian,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Stereo-Planner，从立体RGB实现零样本sim-to-real的端到端视觉到轨迹规划框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航系统依赖专用深度传感器或显式深度重建，直接从RGB图像学习轨迹生成因sim-to-real域差距而困难。

Method: 将轨迹生成建模为状态与尺度条件化的潜在立体几何查询；用预训练视觉基础模型构建Scene Representor提取跨域几何线索；轻量Trajectory Decoder通过交错注意力分离轨迹-状态交互与场景特征检索，并以立体基线作为尺度线索。

Result: 仅用仿真数据训练即可直接部署真机；仿真中8 m/s命令速度下成功率95%，通用性强；紧凑四旋翼上达7 m/s自主飞行，机载推理平均24.27 ms。

Conclusion: 验证了仅用立体RGB即可实现零样本sim-to-real规划，与深度方法竞争，代码与仿真硬件将开源。

Abstract: Existing navigation systems commonly rely on dedicated depth sensors or explicit depth reconstruction, while learning to generate trajectories directly from RGB images remains challenging due to the sim-to-real domain gap. In this work, we propose Stereo-Planner, an end-to-end vision-to-trajectory framework that enables zero-shot sim-to-real planning from stereo RGB observations. The key idea is to formulate trajectory generation as state- and scale-conditioned querying of latent stereo geometry. A Scene Representor incorporates a pretrained vision grounding foundation model to extract geometric cues shared across simulated and real-world environments, supporting generalization across visual domains. A lightweight Trajectory Decoder then translates these representations into trajectories through interleaved attention, separating trajectory-state interaction from scene-feature retrieval and incorporating the stereo baseline as a metric-scale cue. With these designs, Stereo-Planner is trained entirely on simulated data by minimizing differentiable trajectory costs and deployed directly in real-world environments without fine-tuning. Benchmark results demonstrate strong cross-scene generalization, with a 95% success rate in simulation environments at a commanded speed of 8 m/s, and competitive performance against depth-based planners using only stereo RGB observations for scene perception. Furthermore, deployment on a compact quadrotor equipped with an NVIDIA Orin NX enables autonomous flight at speeds of up to 7 m/s, with a mean onboard vision-to-action inference time of 24.27 ms. The software, simulation environments, and hardware designs will be open-sourced at https://github.com/Stereo-Planner/Stereo-Planner.

</details>


### [80] [Anticipatory Textile MMG-Strain Sensing for Confirm-Then-Assist Soft Knee Exosuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15563)
*Zheng Ma,Xiao Jin,Zhenhua Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种针织织物接口，在同一500 Hz时钟下同步采集三通道MMG阵列与前置应变传感器信号，实现运动起始前的意图识别与软体机器人辅助触发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体可穿戴机器人需在运动起始时即提供助力，但sEMG易受汗液与电极位移影响，运动学传感器又滞后于肢体运动，亟需一种能早期、鲁棒检测神经肌肉驱动的信号方案。

Method: 设计针织织物接口同步采集三通道机械描记图（MMG）与前置应变传感器；5名被试完成坐-站转移实验，比较MMG激活与应变检测的膝关节运动的时序；利用同一时钟下的应变斜率符号判定运动方向，并构建两阶段"确认-辅助"策略驱动软体气动膝关节。

Result: MMG激活较应变检测的膝关节运动提前322-344 ms（各通道中位数）；应变斜率符号在650次完成转移中649次正确判定方向，无需训练或个体校准，四分类平衡准确率由90.3%提升至95.6%。4名被试240次试验中，控制器在80次坐-站试验中78次进入辅助状态，80次等长共收缩中0次误触发；但在80次站-坐试验中27次进入辅助状态并直至hold超时才退出。

Conclusion: 在同一时钟下共采样肌肉声学与织物应变信号，可使充气指令在运动起始附近发出；站-坐方向歧义需引入拮抗屈肌测量加以解决。

Abstract: Soft wearable robots must commit to assistance around movement onset, when the available signals are ambiguous. Surface electromyography (sEMG) detects neuromuscular drive early but degrades under sweat and electrode shift, and kinematic sensors respond only once the limb moves. This letter presents a knitted textile interface that co-samples a three-channel mechanomyography (MMG) array and an anterior strain sensor on a single synchronous 500 Hz clock. Across five participants, MMG activation preceded strain-detected knee motion during sit-to-stand by per-channel medians of 322-344 ms. Sampled on the same clock, the sign of the strain slope resolved movement direction on 649 of 650 completed transitions with no training or per-participant calibration, raising four-class balanced accuracy from 90.3% to 95.6%. The fused signals drove a two-stage confirm-then-assist policy for a soft pneumatic knee. Across 240 trials in four participants, the onboard controller entered the assist state on 78 of 80 sit-to-stand trials and on none of the 80 isometric co-contractions. It also entered the assist state on 27 of 80 stand-to-sit trials and left it at the hold timeout, motivating an antagonistic flexor gauge to resolve this direction at onset. Co-sampling muscle acoustics with textile strain on one clock lets the inflation command be issued around movement onset.

</details>


### [81] [Look Before You Move: Agent-Interface-Aligned Supervision for Open-World Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15572)
*Ronghe Qiu,Teli Ma,Jiaming Zhou,Zeying Gong,Yiting He,Qiang Huang,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出AIDA数据采集pipeline，让teacher直接在已部署agent的技能接口上选择执行动作，并加入模板化空间推理监督，训练出AIDA-OWMM agent，在开放世界移动操作任务上取得较好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界移动操作要求机器人根据语言指令找到物体、接近并搬运到目标容器，训练VLM agent需要动作选择和主动视觉的示范。但现有pipeline多将规划轨迹转换为高层动作标签，最短路径进度对"该看哪里"的监督有限。

Method: 提出Agent-Interface Data Alignment (AIDA)数据采集pipeline：teacher通过已部署agent的技能接口直接选择并执行动作；为移动操作设计能维持目标可见性、找回丢失目标、接近以抓取或放置的teacher；加入基于模板的空间推理监督；使用共享harness组织训练与推理时的观测、历史与输出。

Result: AIDA-OWMM在170个held-out OWMM episodes上达到71.76%全任务成功率。Teacher敏感性分析显示主动视觉示范提升student成功率。仿真训练的agent在真实移动机器人上无需额外训练达到38%端到端成功率。将AIDA应用于EmbodiedBench的导航子集也验证了该pipeline对导航任务的效用。

Conclusion: AIDA通过教师直接接口操作与主动视觉示范数据采集，为开放世界移动操作和导航任务提供了有效的agent训练方案，并具备仿真到真实的迁移能力。

Abstract: Open-world mobile manipulation requires a robot to find an object, approach it, and move it to a target receptacle from a language instruction. Training vision-language model (VLM) agents for these tasks requires demonstrations of both action selection and active vision. Existing pipelines often convert planned trajectories into high-level action labels, but shortest-path progress provides limited supervision for deciding where to look. We introduce Agent-Interface Data Alignment (AIDA), a data collection pipeline in which a teacher directly selects and executes actions through the deployed agent's skill interface. For mobile manipulation, we design teachers that maintain target visibility, recover lost targets, and approach for picking or placing. We add template-based spatial reasoning supervision and use a shared harness to organize observations, history, and outputs during training and inference. The resulting agent, AIDA-OWMM, achieves 71.76% full-task success on 170 held-out OWMM episodes. Teacher sensitivity analysis shows that active-vision demonstrations improve student success. The simulation-trained agent achieves 38% end-to-end success on a real mobile robot without additional training. Applying AIDA to a navigation subset of EmbodiedBench further demonstrates the pipeline's utility for navigation tasks.

</details>


### [82] [SIGN-MAPPO: State-Intent-Aware Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving under Imperfect V2X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15577)
*Zide Fan,Jiahui Yuan,Kaixin Zhang,Junhao Fan,Xiaohe Li,Yiru Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SIGN-MAPPO，用状态-意图感知图多智能体强化学习解决不完美V2X下混合交通无信号交叉口协同驾驶问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合交通无信号交叉口协同驾驶面临三大挑战：V2X通信不完美、人类驾驶车辆意图无法直接传达、独立生成的穿越决策可能互不兼容。

Method: 三个协同机制：1）每个有决策资格的CAV基于自身状态和可得的同伴状态记录重建接收者特定的交通图，由共享图注意力策略生成带HV意图预测特征的原始Stop/Go意图；2）CAV意图通过带时间戳的V2V消息交换；3）本地冲突感知仲裁层将意图转化为安全可执行动作，以应对同伴信息可能不一致的情况。

Result: SUMO主评估中达到97.53%完成率、0.10%碰撞率和35.28秒平均穿越时间；消融实验证实HV意图预测与CAV意图交换的互补贡献；在测试的通信延迟和丢包范围内保持稳健；跨场景测试实现完全清空交通，但在复合不可预测HV行为下安全裕度收窄。

Conclusion: SIGN-MAPPO通过图注意力、意图预测与冲突仲裁的协同，有效提升了不完美V2X条件下混合交通无信号交叉口协同驾驶的安全性与效率。

Abstract: Cooperative driving at mixed-traffic unsignalized intersections is challenged by imperfect vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, human-driven vehicle (HV) intentions that cannot be directly communicated, and the potential incompatibility of independently generated crossing decisions. This paper proposes State-Intent-Aware Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Driving under Imperfect V2X (SIGN-MAPPO) to address these challenges through three coordinated mechanisms. Each decision-eligible connected and automated vehicle (CAV) first reconstructs a receiver-specific traffic graph from its ego state and the peer-state records available to it, from which a shared graph-attention policy generates a raw Stop/Go intention augmented with predicted HV-intent features. CAV intentions are then exchanged through timestamped vehicle-to-vehicle (V2V) messages, and a local conflict-aware arbitration layer converts each CAV's intention into a safe executable action despite potentially inconsistent peer information. In the principal Simulation of Urban MObility (SUMO) evaluation, SIGN-MAPPO achieves a 97.53% completion rate, a 0.10% collision rate, and a mean intersection traversal time of 35.28 s. Ablation studies further confirm the complementary contributions of HV-intent prediction and CAV-intent exchange to this performance. The trained policy also remains robust under the evaluated communication-delay and packet-loss ranges, and cross-scenario tests show complete traffic clearance across the tested settings, though safety margins narrow under compound unpredictable HV behavior.

</details>


### [83] [StreamingWAM: Streaming World Action Modeling via Chunk-Wise Staircase Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15585)
*Shaojie Ren,Jingwen Sun,Zezhi Liu,Hongjin Chen,Xin Jin,Wei Zou,Zhibo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 StreamingWAM，一个面向世界动作模型的流式扩散框架，通过噪声阶梯、前缀展开与密集视频历史记忆，同时解决推理效率与跨块动作连续性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预训练视频生成世界模型的 WAM 是通用机器人控制的有前景框架，但实际部署中执行不连续、抖动明显。现有方法要么提升推理效率，要么增强跨预测块的连续性，未能同时解决这两个挑战。

Method: StreamingWAM 采用流式扩散框架，包含三个核心组件：用于块级动作预测的噪声阶梯（noise ladder）、用于跨块连续性的前缀展开模块（prefix rollout），以及用于时间一致条件建模的密集视频历史记忆（dense video-history memory）。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 2.0 及真实机器人任务上的实验表明，StreamingWAM 在静态与动态场景中均改善了动作连续性，性能具有竞争力，稳态重规划延迟在仿真中降至 72.9 ms、真实机器人上为 57.3 ms；动作过渡更平滑，且流式阶梯降低了对执行视界 K 的敏感性。

Conclusion: StreamingWAM 通过联合优化推理效率与跨块连续性，有效缓解了 WAM 机器人控制的抖动与不连续问题，并在仿真与真实任务中兼顾了性能与低延迟。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs), often built upon pretrained video-generation world models, have emerged as a promising framework for general-purpose and open-ended robot control. However, their real-world deployment often suffers from discontinuous and jerky execution. Existing methods typically address this issue by either improving inference efficiency or enhancing action continuity across prediction chunks, without jointly addressing both challenges. To bridge this gap, we propose StreamingWAM, a streaming-diffusion framework for WAM-based robot control. StreamingWAM combines a noise ladder for chunk-wise action prediction, a prefix rollout module for cross-chunk continuity, and dense video-history memory for temporally consistent conditioning. Experiments on LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, RoboTwin 2.0, and real-world robotic tasks demonstrate that StreamingWAM improves action continuity across static and dynamic settings, achieving competitive performance while reducing steady-state replanning latency to 72.9 ms in simulation and 57.3 ms on the real robot. The method also produces smoother action transitions, while the streaming staircase reduces sensitivity to the execution horizon K.

</details>


### [84] [Imitation Learning via Discrete and Rhythmic Primitive Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15639)
*Rafid Saif,Auke Ijspeert,Hsiu-Chin Lin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出神经动态组合（NDC），一种监督框架，仅用原始轨迹（每个基元一个示例）即可组合动态运动基元，统一离散与节律运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序机器人操作由语义子任务组成，运动基元（MPs）可建模这些子任务，但其组合通常依赖人工设计或样本效率低的强化学习。现有监督方法受限于标准数据集缺少MP标签，且难以在离散与节律运动间泛化。

Method: 利用DMP并行组合作为类别式基元选择的可微松弛；采用翘曲不变（warp-invariant）损失，在空间或时间变化下识别基元；使用分阶段训练课程学习选择策略与DMP参数，将离散与节律运动统一到一个框架。

Result: 在2D手写与3D机器人任务上评估，方法在多数任务和指标上优于基线；消融研究显示各设计选择对系统性能的贡献。

Conclusion: NDC证明仅凭原始轨迹与少量示例即可监督地组合DMP，统一离散与节律运动，并在多任务上取得更优表现。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation is inherently composed of semantically meaningful sub-tasks. While Movement Primitives (MPs) can model these components, their composition typically relies on manual engineering or sample-inefficient reinforcement learning. Existing supervised methods are limited by the lack of MP labels in standard datasets and often fail to generalize across discrete and rhythmic movements. We propose Neural Dynamic Composition (NDC), a supervised framework to compose Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) using only raw trajectories for supervision, given a single example of each primitive. Our approach exploits parallel combinations of DMPs as a differentiable relaxation of categorical primitive selection, and utilizes a warp-invariant loss formulation to identify primitives despite spatial or temporal variations. A staged training curriculum is employed to learn the selection policy and DMP parameters, unifying discrete and rhythmic movements in one framework. Evaluated on 2D handwriting and 3D robotic tasks, our method outperforms baselines on most tasks and metrics. An ablation study demonstrates how each design choice contributes to the system's performance.

</details>


### [85] [Recovering Low-Bit Failures in Vision-Language-Action Policies via Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15675)
*Seongmin Park,Jaewon Lee,Yujun Heo,Yoonsik Kim,Seheon Choi,Hyoung-rock Kim,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究了VLA策略量化对任务完成度的影响，并提出基于离线强化学习的量化条件方法QCRL，在INT4与NVFP4设定下逼近甚至超越全精度性能，同时显著降低显存与提升速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化能降低VLA策略部署成本，但会损害任务完成能力。现有QAT虽优于PTQ，却在长时序任务及视觉、语义分布偏移下仍与全精度存在较大差距；其模仿学习目标未显式利用 rollout 成功与否及部分完成信息。

Method: 提出Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning（QCRL）离线强化学习方法：结合基于进度的优势估计与量化感知源条件；利用全精度rollout提供超越动作级蒸馏的参考经验；用进度缩放的终端惩罚区分部分完成；通过量化感知条件在量化策略内选择有利的参考行为。

Result: 在5个VLA模型、4050次真实试验上评估。INT4 W4A16用于π0.5与GR00T N1.5，NVFP4 W4A4用于π0.5。仅用分布内数据，π0.5 INT4下QCRL达到FP平均得分的99.3%，节省42.9%模型内存；NVFP4 W4A4下达到FP基线的100.7%，配合NVFP4/FP8部署获得1.73倍加速（硬件增益相对Jetson Thor上的FP16）。

Conclusion: 利用结果信号的QCRL可有效弥合量化VLA与全精度之间的性能差距，在低比特设定下实现接近甚至超越全精度的任务表现，同时带来显存节省与推理加速。

Abstract: Quantization lowers deployment costs of vision-language-action (VLA) policies but can compromise task completion. Across five VLA models and 4,050 real-world trials, we systematically reveal that quantization-aware training (QAT) generally improves on post-training quantization (PTQ) but leaves substantial gaps to full precision (FP) on long-horizon tasks and under visual and semantic shifts, while its imitation-based objective does not explicitly use rollout success or partial completion. To exploit these outcome signals, we introduce Quantization-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning (QCRL), an offline RL method coupling progress-based advantage estimation with quantization-aware source conditioning. FP rollouts supply reference experience beyond action-level distillation, progress-scaled terminal penalty distinguishes partial completion, and quantization-aware conditioning selects advantageous reference behavior within the quantized policy. We evaluate weight-only INT4 W4A16 on $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 and joint weight-activation NVFP4 W4A4 on $π_{0.5}$. Using only in-distribution data, QCRL reaches 99.3% of FP's mean score across three visual-shift conditions for $π_{0.5}$ with INT4 W4A16, alongside 42.9% model-memory savings. With NVFP4 W4A4, it reaches 100.7% of the same FP baseline, alongside a $1.73\times$ speedup using NVFP4/FP8 deployment. Hardware gains are relative to FP16 on Jetson Thor.

</details>


### [86] [Geometry-Aware Predictive Control for Walk-to-Run Bipedal Obstacle Crossing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15735)
*Aashish Sahu,R. Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种几何感知的预测控制框架，实现双足机器人在行走与奔跑模式下跨越障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为双足机器人在行走与奔跑两种运动模式下的障碍跨越提供紧凑、可解释且计算高效的控制基础，解决跨越过程中的协调与平滑模式切换问题。

Method: 将相结构化轨迹生成器与带约束的模型预测控制（MPC）跟踪层结合，生成协调的骨盆与摆动腿运动。跨越过程分为五个可解释阶段：跨前、前腿越障、跨立、后腿越障与恢复。摆动腿抬升高度随障碍高度自适应；横向站立加宽与骨盆重定位作为障碍深度的函数以改善横向间隙与身体对齐。通过连续步态参数演化实现行走到奔跑的平滑过渡，无需离散控制器切换。生成的参考轨迹由MPC跟踪，用于调节运动平滑性、间隙可行性、触地行为与横向可接受性。

Result: 在降阶仿真中，针对五个逐渐加难的障碍在行走与奔跑条件下进行评估。机器人成功跨越前四个障碍，最小身体-障碍间隙分别为0.036、0.036、0.059和0.017米。第五个也是最具挑战性的障碍导致碰撞，标定了所提降阶公式的当前工作边界。

Conclusion: 该方法为智能化的障碍感知双足运动提供了一种紧凑、可解释且计算高效的基础。

Abstract: This paper presents a geometry-aware predictive control framework for bipedal obstacle crossing across walking and running regimes. The proposed approach integrates a phase-structured trajectory generator with a constrained model predictive control (MPC) tracking layer to generate coordinated pelvis and swing-foot motions during obstacle traversal. The crossing process is represented through five interpretable phases: pre-crossing, leading-leg clearance, straddle, trailing-leg clearance, and recovery. Within this structure, swing-foot elevation is adapted as a function of obstacle height, while lateral stance widening and pelvis repositioning are introduced as functions of obstacle depth to improve transverse clearance and body alignment. A continuous gait-parameter evolution enables smooth walk-to-run transition without discrete controller switching. The generated references are tracked using MPC to regulate motion smoothness, clearance feasibility, touchdown behavior, and lateral admissibility. The framework is evaluated in reduced-order simulation over five progressively difficult obstacles under walking and running conditions. The robot successfully crosses the first four obstacles, achieving minimum body-obstacle clearances of 0.036, 0.036, 0.059, and 0.017 m, respectively. The fifth and most demanding obstacle leads to collision, identifying the current operating boundary of the proposed reduced-order formulation. Overall, the method provides a compact, interpretable, and computationally efficient basis for intelligent obstacle-aware bipedal locomotion.

</details>


### [87] [Distributed area coverage path planning subject to the inclusion of a headland path](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15739)
*Mogens Plessen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定性分布式区域覆盖路径规划方法，将地头路径纳入规划约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域覆盖路径规划中，地头路径的纳入对实现完整覆盖至关重要，而现有基于Boustrophedon模式的方法在此方面存在不足。

Method: 针对一般非凸区域（可含障碍），在给定多机器人、至少一个机器人仓库、以及工作宽度、工作容量限制、行驶容量限制、非完整动力学约束和可能的起止仓库分配等条件下，为各机械计算分布式路径。

Result: 所计算的分布式路径能够协同实现全区域覆盖；讨论了若干变体，特别是在无约束仓库分配情况下可达到全局最优解的场景，并对比了基于Boustrophedon模式的替代方法。

Conclusion: 纳入地头路径对区域覆盖具有重要意义，且相比Boustrophedon模式方法具有明显优势。

Abstract: A deterministic method for the problem of distributed area coverage path planning subject to the inclusion of a headland path is presented. Given a, in general, non-convex area to be covered that may, in general, include obstacle areas, multiple robots, at least one robot depot, and constraints on each machinery such as a working width, work capacity limits, travel capacity limits, nonholonomic dynamical constraints and, possibly, a robot start and end depot assignment, distributed paths for each machinery are calculated that collectively enable full area coverage. Variations are discussed, in particular, a scenario where unconstrained depots assignment can attain a global optimal solution to the problem. The importance of inclusion of a headland path for area coverage, as well as the benefits in comparison to an alternative Boustrophedon-pattern based approach are highlighted.

</details>


### [88] [Multimodal Sensor-Gated Mid-Air Launch and Vision-Based Recovery of a Child UAV on a Hovering Mothership Drone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15740)
*Aashish Sahu,R. Prasanth Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种母-子四旋翼系统，通过非视觉互补传感对任务转换进行门控，以可靠确认子机就位。户外十次任务中九次成功，仿真中触地距中心0.077 m，载机推力在释放/捕获时变化与子机悬停推力吻合（误差6%内）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从悬停载机上发射并回收微型无人机时，在载机下降前必须可靠确认子机已安全就位。但视觉仅提供相对引导，无法直接反映载荷转移，且朝下的相机在接近触地时可能丢失基准标记，因此需要非视觉传感来辅助判断。

Method: 构建母-子四旋翼系统，用互补非视觉传感触发任务转换：回收甲板上的朝上单束LiDAR通过迟滞和去抖间隔给出二值占用状态（参数由子机遮挡足迹和穿越速度推导）；实时动态（RTK）定位约束载机悬停；载机推力提供载荷转移的本体感知。并进行MuJoCo全自主视觉基准回收仿真。

Result: 户外十次任务（F450级母机、Q250级子机、视觉引导回收）中九次成功，释放与捕获全程保持RTK固定解。仿真中全自主基准回收触地距甲板中心0.077 m，标记检测在距对接构型最后约0.2 m内丢失；载机推力在释放时变化-17.0 N、捕获时+17.3 N，与子机18.0 N悬停推力吻合在6%以内。

Conclusion: 结果支持一种传感架构：视觉负责引导接近，而测距与本体感知信息用于佐证最终对接决策，从而提升回收过程的可靠性。

Abstract: Launching a micro unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) from a hovering carrier and recovering it in flight requires reliable confirmation that the child is securely seated before the carrier is allowed to descend. This is challenging because vision provides relative guidance but does not directly indicate load transfer, and a downward-facing camera can lose its fiducial near touchdown. This letter presents a mothership-child quadrotor system in which mission transitions are gated by complementary nonvisual sensing. An upward-facing single-beam LiDAR on the recovery deck provides a binary occupancy state using hysteresis and a debounce interval derived from the child's occluding footprint and crossing speed; real-time kinematic (RTK) positioning conditions the carrier hover; and carrier thrust provides a proprioceptive indication of load transfer. In ten outdoor missions using an F450-class mothership and a Q250-class child with camera-guided recovery, nine completed successfully while RTK-fixed positioning was maintained through release and capture. A complementary MuJoCo study of fully autonomous fiducial-based recovery achieved touchdown 0.077 m from the deck center, while marker detection was lost within the final approximately 0.2 m above the docked configuration. In simulation, carrier thrust changed by -17.0 N at release and +17.3 N at capture, agreeing within 6% with the child's 18.0 N hover thrust. The results support a sensing architecture in which vision guides the approach, while range and proprioceptive information corroborate the final docking decision.

</details>


### [89] [Model-Based Trailer-Sway Suppression for Autonomous Passenger Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15747)
*Alec McFadyen,Pablo Covarrubias De La Torre,Connor McGovern*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对轻型车辆拖挂时的"拖车摆振"问题，建立了以舌载（tongue load）为显式稳定性参数的横摆平面线性状态空间模型，设计LQR控制器抑制铰接角发散，并用仅安装在牵引车上的传感器经卡尔曼滤波估计拖车状态，在MuJoCo仿真中验证了显著抑制摆振的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拖车摆振是轻型拖挂车辆严重事故的持续诱因，但在自动驾驶系统进入新时代的背景下仍未得到有效解决。货物置于拖车轴后方会降低甚至反转舌载，导致牵引车后轴垂直载荷与侧向轮胎力下降，使耦合系统横摆稳定性恶化，产生发散的侧向振荡，极端情况下会发展为折叠（jackknifing）。现有消费级拖车不具备传感器，因此需要一种仅依赖牵引车测量、能应对负舌载工况的抗摆振方法。

Method: 1）建立轻型车—拖车组合体横摆平面动力学的线性状态空间模型，参数化使舌载成为显式的稳定性决定量；2）基于该模型综合线性二次型调节器（LQR），用于抑制铰接角发散；3）由于消费级拖车无传感器，控制器所需的拖车横摆率和铰接角两个状态由卡尔曼滤波从仅安装在牵引车上的测量中估计得到；4）在MuJoCo多体仿真中实现并评估，覆盖多种纵向车速和舌载条件，包括负舌载工况，控制输入为牵引车转向，测量来自牵引车IMU与LiDAR。

Result: 结果显示，在使用牵引车IMU和LiDAR数据的LQR控制给定车辆转向时，拖车振荡显著降低；该方法在包括负舌载在内的多种车速与载荷条件下均有效。

Conclusion: LQR控制的抗摆振系统是未来自动驾驶车辆一种可行的控制方法，表明其在自动驾驶时代具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Trailer sway is a persistent contributor to severe crashes among light passenger vehicles towing trailers, yet it remains largely unaddressed by the autonomous vehicle systems now entering this new era of automation. Improper cargo placement rearward of the trailer axle reduces, and can invert, the tongue load transmitted through the hitch. The resulting loss of vertical load at the tow vehicle's rear axle diminishes the lateral tire force available there and degrades yaw stability of the coupled system, admitting a divergent lateral oscillation of the trailer known as trailer sway. Under adverse combinations of speed and loading, this oscillation can progress to jackknifing, in which the articulation angle diverges rapidly and directional control is lost. This work develops a linear state-space model of the yaw-plane dynamics of a light vehicle-trailer combination, parameterized to expose tongue load as an explicit stability-determining quantity. From this model a linear quadratic regulator is synthesized to suppress articulation-angle divergence. Because consumer trailers carry no sensors, the two trailer states the controller needs, trailer yaw rate and articulation angle, are estimated by a Kalman filter from measurements taken only on the tow vehicle. The controller is then implemented and evaluated in a MuJoCo multibody simulation across a range of longitudinal speeds and tongue-load conditions, including the negative-tongue-load regime. Results show a significant decrease in trailer oscillation when controlling a given vehicle's steering with an LQR using tow-vehicle IMU and LiDAR data. These findings indicate that LQR controlled anti-sway systems are a viable control method for future autonomous vehicles.

</details>


### [90] [Kinematic Reward, Measured Effort: Hardware-Validated RL Morphology Optimization of a Planar 2R Manipulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15756)
*Gunesh Gupta,Arvind Kumar Mishra,Sohom Chakrabarty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于RSL-RL PPO的强化学习框架，以Yoshikawa可操作度指标为核心奖励优化2R SCARA机械臂构型，并在硬件上验证其降低关节负载的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上以Yoshikawa可操作度指数确定机械臂最优构型，但此类优化几乎都是纯运动学仿真研究，很少在硬件上验证其实际效果。

Method: 采用RSL-RL PPO强化学习框架，以Yoshikawa运动学可操作度指数为核心奖励，优化2R SCARA型机械臂的构型；先用圆形任务（有解析闭式最优解）验证方法能否恢复该最优解，再应用于无解析最优解的椭圆和矩形路径；最后将RL优化的构型迁移到2R硬件机械臂上验证关节负载降低效果。

Result: 相比最差构型，本方法得到的最优构型使第二关节负载在椭圆路径下降38.4±0.6%、矩形路径下降41.3±0.3%，第一关节负载下降15%。

Conclusion: 该方法能恢复圆形任务的解析最优解，并可在无解析解的路径上找到有效构型，且优化结果在真实硬件上得到验证，能显著降低关节负载。

Abstract: Yoshikawa's Manipulability Index is generally used as a classical measure to define an optimal morphology of a manipulator. However, any such optimization is almost always studied as a purely kinematic quantity in simulation and is rarely validated on hardware. We present a reinforcement-learning framework with RSL-RL PPO that optimizes the morphology of a 2R SCARA-type manipulator arm with Yoshikawa's kinematic manipulability index as a core reward, and the analytically derived closed-form optimum for the circular task is used to verify that our method recovers it. Then the same method is applied to custom elliptical and rectangular paths having no analytical optimum. The RL-optimized morphologies are then transferred to a 2R hardware manipulator to validate its effect in reduction of joint load in the hardware. Compared to the worst morphology, the optimum morphology delivered by our method reduces second joint load by 38.4 +/- 0.6% for the ellipse path and 41.3 +/- 0.3% for the rectangle, and first joint load by 15%.

</details>


### [91] [Competitive Task Allocation with Temporal Proposal Windows and Improved Artificial Potential Fields for Dual-Arm Industrial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15763)
*Surya Prakash S. K,Bhuvan Narula,Jagannath Prasad Sahoo,Jayakumar VK,Praful Hambarde,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于距离的竞争式任务分配框架，结合实时视觉感知与无碰撞运动规划，实现双臂协同操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统依赖预定轨迹和刚性分配策略，导致工作空间利用率不佳和碰撞风险。

Method: 采用时间窗机制（连续5帧视觉检测）进行动态重分配与空间冲突解决（0.01m阈值精度），并改进人工势场（iAPF）实现指数目标吸引、臂间斥力、归位及速度阻尼力。集成YOLOv8 OBB检测（30 FPS）与10 Hz控制回路。

Result: 仿真验证在距离效率、碰撞风险和工作空间覆盖率上优于市场型、轮询和固定分配策略。硬件实验（两个6-DOF Kinova Gen3 Lite）在同质场景下平均速度提升约47%且零碰撞；混合物体场景验证通用处理能力，时间开销约增加11%，但安全裕度更优。

Conclusion: 该框架具备工业装配与分拣操作的实用部署能力。

Abstract: Modern industrial automation demands autonomous collaborative systems capable of coordinated manipulation in workspaces with heterogeneous object arrangements. Existing systems rely on predetermined trajectories and rigid allocation strategies, causing suboptimal workspace utilization and collision risks. This paper presents a distance-based competitive task allocation framework for dual-arm systems, integrating real-time vision perception with collision-free motion planning. A temporal window mechanism enables each arm to evaluate object detections over five consecutive vision frames before committing to optimal distance-based selection, featuring dynamic reallocation and spatial conflict resolution at 0.01m threshold accuracy. An improved Artificial Potential Field (iAPF) framework provides coordinated dual-arm movements through exponential goal attraction, inter-arm repulsion, home-seeking, and velocity damping forces. Simulation results validate superiority over market-based, round-robin, and fixed allocation strategies in distance efficiency, collision risk, and workspace coverage. Hardware experiments using two 6-DOF Kinova Gen3 Lite manipulators demonstrate approximately 47% average velocity improvement over fixed allocation in homogeneous scenarios with zero collisions, while mixed object scenarios confirm universal handling with approximately 11% time overhead offset by superior safety margins. The framework integrates YOLOv8 OBB detection at 30 FPS with 10 Hz control loops, demonstrating practical deployment capability for industrial assembly and sorting operations.

</details>


### [92] [SurgiCLAW: An Interpretable Atomic-Skill Agent for Precision Robotic Micromanipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15766)
*Angen Ye,junjie xie,Yujia Song,Junkai Huang,Dapeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SurgiCLAW：分层技能框架，MLLM负责视觉理解与技能选择，确定性程序负责执行，实现高精度手术操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外科机器人中端到端低级动作预测存在精度不足、决策不透明、数据稀缺等问题。

Method: 将MLLM限定于术中视觉理解、结构化解释与原子手术技能选择，执行交由可验证的确定性控制程序；构建状态-技能调用数据集并用视觉与状态扰动扩增；先监督微调，再用动作正确性、操作前置条件与格式有效性三项奖励优化。

Result: 固定测试集上技能选择准确率94.6%、宏平均F1为93.0%；50次闭环真实机器人植入试验任务成功率88.0%。

Conclusion: 分层技能框架可提升动作选择、操作安全性与输出可解析性，在精密手术任务中有效。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and vision--language--action models have provided robots with new capabilities for long-horizon task understanding and planning. In surgical robotics, however, end-to-end prediction of low-level actions remains limited by insufficient precision, opaque decisions, and scarce data. This paper presents SurgiCLAW, a hierarchical skill-based framework for precision surgical manipulation. SurgiCLAW confines an MLLM to intraoperative visual understanding, structured explanation, and atomic surgical-skill selection, while delegating physical execution to verifiable deterministic control programs. For brain--machine-interface implantation, a representative high-precision surgical task, we construct a state-to-skill invocation dataset and expand it through visual and state perturbations. The model first learns structured skill invocation through supervised fine-tuning and is then optimized using three rewards for action correctness, operation prerequisites, and format validity, improving action selection, operational safety, and output parseability. We evaluate subtask success, offline model comparison, real-robot performance, and ablations. On a fixed test set, SurgiCLAW achieves 94.6% skill-selection accuracy and 93.0% macro-averaged F1 (Macro-F1). Across 50 closed-loop real-robot implantation trials, it achieves an 88.0% task success rate.

</details>


### [93] [Hand2Robot: Object-Centric 4D Hand Reconstruction from Human Demonstrations for Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15769)
*Zetao Du,Weiqing Wang,Hanbing Zhang,Yimiao Zhou,Ye Shi,Liang Wang,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Hand2Robot是一个以物体为中心的4D手部重建框架，通过手-物交互优化与运动重定向，将人手演示视频转化为机器人动作监督，用于真实机器人灵巧操作策略的后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人手演示可捕捉与任务相关的操作技能且无需目标机器人，但记录的手部运动无法直接作为机器人动作监督。手部重建与运动重定向提供了监督生成途径，然而仅恢复手部运动并不能保证物理一致的手-物交互。

Method: 给定人类执行目标任务的多视角视频，框架提取交互片段、重建物体几何，并通过手-物交互优化和运动重定向生成机器人动作监督。物体跟踪上，结合观测置信度与时序运动约束，从逐帧位姿候选中选出全局一致的物体位姿轨迹。手部精修上，以物体几何与运动为参考，结合分阶段2D对齐与物体局部接触约束，细化手部轨迹与手指关节配置。

Result: 在DexYCB与HO3D-v3数据集上，物体位姿精度与手-物交互质量均有提升；在三个真实机器人灵巧操作任务上，重建并重定向的人类演示能有效补充有限的机器人数据，用于目标任务策略后训练。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的手部重建与交互优化可生成物理一致的动作监督，将人类演示用于机器人策略后训练，有效缓解真实机器人数据稀缺问题。

Abstract: Human demonstrations can capture task-relevant manipulation without using the target robot, but the recorded hand motions cannot directly serve as robot action supervision. Hand reconstruction and motion retargeting provide a direct route to such supervision, yet recovering hand motion alone does not ensure physically consistent hand-object interactions. To address this issue, we introduce Hand2Robot, an object-centric 4D hand reconstruction framework for robot policy post-training. Given videos of humans performing the target task, Hand2Robot extracts interaction clips, reconstructs object geometry, and generates robot action supervision through hand-object interaction optimization and motion retargeting. For object tracking, we combine observation confidence with temporal motion constraints to select a globally consistent object pose trajectory from per-frame pose candidates. For hand refinement, we use object geometry and motion as a reference and combine staged 2D alignment with object-local contact constraints to refine hand trajectories and finger joint configurations. Experiments on DexYCB and HO3D-v3 show improved object pose accuracy and hand-object interaction quality. Experiments on three real-robot dexterous manipulation tasks further show that reconstructed and retargeted human demonstrations can effectively supplement limited robot data for target-task policy post-training.

</details>


### [94] [$Δ$ Flow Matching for Robot Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15794)
*Kuangji Zuo,Tuo An,Xinying Guo,Gen Li,Mengfei Zhao,Jiaqi Bai,Bofan Lyu,Zhuoyuan Yu,Jinghan Yang,Jiayi Li,Jindou Jia,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Δ-flow，用近期历史加轻噪声替代固定高斯先验作为生成源，使源更接近目标，将传输简化为可高效学习的残差 Δ，并支持单步推理，显著提升机器人基础模型的训练效率、推理速度与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有流匹配机器人基础模型（VLA 与 WAM）均从固定高斯先验开始生成，忽略了过去与未来状态、动作之间的时间连续性。这导致训练成本高、采样需迭代，尤其对带有额外视觉分支的 WAM 更为昂贵。作者希望利用时间连续性来缩小源与目标之间的传输距离。

Method: Δ-flow 从近期历史构建带轻噪声的源，而非固定高斯先验，使源靠近目标，从而只需学习一个小残差 Δ，实现高效学习与单步推理。作者将 Δ-flow 集成到 VLA 策略 π0.5 以及 WAM 策略 ImageWAM 和 Fast-WAM 中，并研究动作表示与视觉信息对历史到未来预测的影响；同时探索动作历史源在无需重训练情况下用于预训练 WAM 策略。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上，ImageWAM 一个 epoch 即达 90% 成功率（原需六个），Fast-WAM 两个 epoch 达 95%（原需八个）。LoRA 微调下，π0.5 一个 epoch 即超过基线五个 epoch 的性能。单步推理分别在 π0.5、ImageWAM、Fast-WAM 上带来 1.54×、2.49×、4.45× 加速。动作平滑性保持良好，动作 jerk 相比一步高斯采样降低 4.5×。在 LIBERO-Plus 上达到最先进泛化性能。

Conclusion: Δ-flow 简单而有效，通过历史构建源缩小传输距离，提升了机器人基础模型的训练与推理效率及泛化能力。动作历史源可无需重训练地应用于预训练 WAM 策略；推理时用动作源替代高斯源可在大幅减少采样步数的同时保持甚至略微提升任务成功率。

Abstract: Flow matching is a common generative recipe for robot foundation models. Vision-language-action models (VLAs) generate action chunks, while world-action models (WAMs) additionally generate future visual states. Both start generation from a fixed Gaussian prior, despite temporal continuity between past and future states and actions. This requires costly training and iterative sampling, especially for WAMs with an additional visual branch. We introduce $Δ$-flow, which constructs the source from recent history with mild noise rather than from a fixed Gaussian prior. This moves the source closer to the target, reducing transport to a small residual $Δ$ that can be learned efficiently and enables single-step inference. We integrate $Δ$-flow into the VLA policy $π_{0.5}$ and the WAM policies ImageWAM and Fast-WAM. On LIBERO, ImageWAM reaches 90% success in one epoch rather than six, and Fast-WAM reaches 95% in two epochs rather than eight. With LoRA fine-tuning, $π_{0.5}$ exceeds its baseline's five-epoch performance after one epoch. Single-step inference gives $1.54\times$, $2.49\times$, and $4.45\times$ speedups on $π_{0.5}$, ImageWAM, and Fast-WAM, respectively. It preserves action smoothness, achieving a $4.5\times$ reduction in action jerk compared with one-step Gaussian sampling. $Δ$-flow reaches state-of-the-art generalization performance on LIBERO-Plus. Action-history sources can be applied to pretrained WAM policies without retraining. Replacing the Gaussian action source at inference largely preserves task success with a single sampling step and can slightly improve it. We further study how action representation and visual information in action learning affect history-to-future prediction in $Δ$-flow. These results show that $Δ$-flow is simple yet effective, improving the efficiency and generalization of robot foundation models.

</details>


### [95] [ReTrace: Cross-Stage Failure Attribution for Self-Improving Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15814)
*Yue Yu,Bin Zhu,Junhui Li,Jiayu Wang,Shijie Zhou,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReTrace 框架，让机器人通过自主物理交互与回溯推理从失败中学习，无需演示或更新模型参数，即可将失败追溯为早期错误决策并生成可复用的操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态基础模型已能直接控制机器人，但通过自主交互提升其操作能力仍很困难。核心难点在于跨阶段依赖任务中的失败学习：某个动作局部成功，却导致后续阶段失败，这类失败难以归因。

Method: ReTrace 通过自主物理交互与回溯推理实现自我改进，不依赖给定演示，也不更新模型参数。它把下游失败回溯归因到更早的决策，提出针对性修正，并通过后续交互评估修正效果；在多次尝试中将验证有效的修正逐步整合为可复用的操作技能，用以指导后续执行。

Result: 在 Franka Research 3 机器人上对四项真实操作任务进行评估。在两项具有强跨阶段依赖的任务中，GPT-6 Astra-low 在插头插入任务上成功率从 0% 提升至 80%、倒球任务从 0% 提升至 100%；GPT-6 Sol-xhigh 分别从 0% 提升至 10% 和从 0% 提升至 90%。所学技能可跨模型与推理配置迁移，并在多数评测设置下降低推理时决策成本。

Conclusion: ReTrace 表明，无需演示或参数更新，仅靠自主交互与回溯推理即可显著提升机器人在强跨阶段依赖任务中的操作成功率，且学到的技能具备跨配置迁移性和降低决策成本的潜力。

Abstract: General-purpose multimodal foundation models are increasingly capable of directly controlling robots, yet improving their manipulation capabilities through autonomous interaction remains challenging. A key challenge to such self-improvement is learning from failures in complex manipulation tasks involving cross-stage dependencies, where an action may succeed locally but cause a later stage to fail. To address this challenge, we introduce ReTrace, a self-improvement framework for robotic manipulation through autonomous physical interaction and retrospective reasoning, without provided demonstrations or model-parameter updates. ReTrace retrospectively traces downstream failures to earlier decisions, proposes targeted corrections, and evaluates them through subsequent interactions. Across attempts, ReTrace progressively consolidates validated corrections into a reusable manipulation skill that guide subsequent execution. We evaluate ReTrace on four real-world manipulation tasks using a Franka Research 3 robot. On the two tasks with strong cross-stage dependencies, GPT-6 Astra-low improves from 0% to 80% success on plug insertion and from 0% to 100% on ball pouring, while GPT-6 Sol-xhigh improves from 0% to 10% and 0% to 90%, respectively. The learned skills also transfer across model and reasoning configurations and reduce inference-time decision cost in most evaluated settings.

</details>


### [96] [P-BFM: Perceptive Behavioral Foundation Models for Humanoid Control via Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15833)
*Xue Wang,Yitang Li,Yiming Zhang,Siqin Wang,Junhan Sun,Zhihao Liu,Yaojie Shen,Jingbo Yuan,Hao Zhao,Qingdu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: P-BFM 将人形机器人控制分解为"地形无关意图"与"地形条件实现"，并结合显式根指令，实现无需地形配对演示即可在复杂地形上生成多样化全身行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实环境下相同的运动意图在不同地形约束下需要完全不同的动作，用与地形匹配的演示来覆盖这种变化需要枚举行为与地形几何的组合空间，成本极高。

Method: 提出感知式行为基础模型 P-BFM：前向-后向表示将运动参考、目标状态与奖励函数映射到共享的无地形信息意图空间；感知策略学习在周围地形几何下实现该意图；并引入显式根指令（ERI），把全局运输（朝向、平面速度）从行为意图中分离，局部全身适应交给感知策略。

Result: 在多种挑战性地形上，P-BFM 在运动保真度、根控制精度与扰动恢复上优于强基线；单一冻结策略支持运动跟踪、目标到达、奖励推断、遥操作与地形感知恢复，并通过机载深度感知零样本迁移到 Unitree G1。

Conclusion: 将意图与地形实现解耦、并显式分离全局根控制，可让固定意图在不同地形下自然产生地形适配行为（步高、步幅、姿态、落脚点、接触变化），无需地形专用演示，展现出传统 BFM 不具备的能力。

Abstract: Behavioral foundation models (BFMs) provide humanoids with reusable repertoires of whole-body behaviors; in real-world humanoid control, the same behavioral intent may require fundamentally different motions under different terrain constraints. Covering such variation with terrain-matched demonstrations requires enumerating a combinatorial space of behaviors and terrain geometries. We introduce P-BFM, a perceptive behavioral foundation model that factorizes humanoid control into terrain-invariant intent and terrain-conditioned realization. A forward-backward representation maps motion references, target states, and reward functions into a shared intent space without terrain information, while a perceptive policy learns how to realize each intent under the surrounding terrain geometry. We further identify a complementary limitation of purely latent control: behavioral latents can preserve local motion patterns while losing precise global progress. We therefore introduce Explicit Root Instructions (ERI), which separates global transport (e.g., heading and planar velocity) from behavioral intent, while leaving local whole-body adaptation to the perceptive policy. This decomposition leads to a characteristic capability absent from conventional BFMs: one fixed intent induces different, terrain-appropriate behaviors, including changes in step height, stride, posture, foothold placement, and contacts, without terrain-specific demonstrations. Across challenging terrains, P-BFM improves motion fidelity, root-control precision, and perturbation recovery over strong baselines. A single frozen policy further supports motion tracking, goal reaching, reward inference, teleoperation, and terrain-aware recovery, and transfers zero-shot to a Unitree G1 using onboard depth sensing.

</details>


### [97] [REFLEX: A Learned On-Board Reflex for Robust Autonomous Collision Recovery of Soft Quadrotors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15867)
*Jakob Hermeler,Luca Girardi,Gabriel Maquignaz,Stefan Leutenegger,Stefano Mintchev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REFLEX是一种完全运行于飞控上的碰撞恢复飞行模式，无需接触传感器或伴飞计算机；其本体感受循环策略在无位置/速度/航向/高度估计下稳定飞行器，并在交还控制前通过信任门校验估计器一致性。硬件实验中22次撞墙恢复19次（5.03±0.09 m/s），同期原厂自动驾驶仅14次中1次，且实现单次飞行15次连续恢复，最高恢复速度达6.05 m/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抗碰撞机体能让四旋翼在撞击后存活，但无法保证自主的碰撞后恢复。现成机载估计器中的创新门控在高速撞击后常拒绝有效的外部速度测量，从而损害恢复能力。因此需要一种不依赖接触传感器、伴飞计算机，也不依赖位置/速度/航向/高度估计的自主恢复方法。

Method: 提出REFLEX碰撞恢复飞行模式，全部运行在飞控上。其本体感受循环策略负责在无位置、速度、航向、高度估计的情况下稳定机体；在REFLEX激活期间重新初始化平移估计；返回控制给原厂自驾仪前，用信任门检查估计器一致性。策略在仿真中用PPO训练，训练回合的初始状态来自47次真实碰撞后测得的撞击后状态。

Result: 硬件实验中，REFLEX在5.03±0.09 m/s速度下对22次撞墙实现19次恢复，而匹配的原厂自驾仪仅为14次中1次；其中包括单次飞行的15次连续恢复。另一次探索性飞行在同一飞行内以6.02和6.05 m/s完成两次额外恢复，比此前报道的自主撞墙恢复速度高出64%。

Conclusion: REFLEX表明，仅靠飞控上的本体感受循环策略即可实现高速撞击后的自主恢复，无需接触传感器或伴飞计算机，也不依赖状态估计；通过平移估计重初始化与信任门校验可实现安全交还控制，显著优于原厂自驾仪并提升可恢复速度上限。

Abstract: Collision-resilient airframes allow quadrotors to survive impacts, but do not ensure autonomous post-collision recovery. Innovation gating in stock onboard estimators often rejects valid external velocity measurements after high-speed impacts, compromising recovery. We present REFLEX, a collision-recovery flight mode that runs entirely on the flight controller, requiring neither contact sensors nor a companion computer. Its proprioceptive recurrent policy stabilizes the vehicle without position, velocity, heading, or altitude estimates. The translational estimate is reinitialized while REFLEX is active, and a trust gate checks estimator consistency before returning control to the stock autopilot. The policy is trained in simulation using proximal policy optimization, with episodes initialized from post-impact states measured in 47 real collisions. In hardware experiments, REFLEX recovered 19 of 22 wall impacts at 5.03 $\pm$ 0.09 m/s, compared with 1 of 14 for the matched stock autopilot. These included 15 consecutive recoveries in a single flight. A separate exploratory flight demonstrated two additional recoveries at 6.02 and 6.05 m/s within the same flight, exceeding the previously reported velocity for autonomous wall-collision recovery by 64%.

</details>


### [98] [Efficient Hybrid Hard--Soft Signal Temporal Logic MPC for Driving under Multimodal Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15934)
*Kumar Manas,Adrian Paschke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出混合硬-软STL MPC框架，在多种行为假设下优化单一共享轨迹，硬约束保障安全、软鲁棒性项优化舒适与交互，并通过筛选与向量化降低符号图构建开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式交通中的运动规划需同时满足安全、遵守交规与舒适通行，且面临多模态行为不确定性。将全部要求设为硬约束会降低可行性，全部设为代价又会使安全被通行性能权衡，因此需要一种能区分"不可让步"与"可优化"要求的规划框架。

Method: 采用混合硬-软信号时序逻辑（STL）MPC框架，对每个保留的行为假设优化同一条共享自车轨迹：将最小距离与速度谓词作为显式硬约束，将软STL鲁棒性项用于塑造间隙、舒适性与交互质量；避免分支特定的应急轨迹。同时引入可达性认证的相关性筛选和向量化符号构建，以减少符号图构建开销，并保持可行集与目标不变。

Result: 在六个高速与路口场景的闭环蒙特卡洛评估中，与对比规划器相比取得最高严格成功率，未观察到碰撞或违规，规划时延约为未优化公式的四分之一；消融研究将两层分离：硬约束界定安全包络，软项在其内提升间隙与运动质量。

Conclusion: 硬-软分层设计可在多模态交互不确定性下同时保证安全与通行质量，配合筛选与向量化实现计算效率的大幅提升，实现在线可用的交互式运动规划。

Abstract: Motion planning for automated vehicles in interactive traffic has to simultaneously ensure safety, respect traffic rules, and maintain comfortable progress under multi-modal behavior uncertainty. Imposing all these requirements as hard constraints reduces feasibility, whereas treating them all as costs allows safety-critical requirements to be traded against progress. We propose a hybrid hard--soft Signal Temporal Logic (STL) MPC framework that optimizes a single shared ego trajectory against every retained behavior hypothesis: minimum-distance and speed predicates are imposed as explicit hard constraints, while soft STL robustness terms shape clearance, comfort, and interaction quality within the resulting feasible driving corridor. This retains robustness to discrete interaction modes without branch-specific contingent ego policies. We further identify substantial symbolic graph-construction overhead and introduce reachability-certified relevance screening and vectorized symbolic construction to reduce computational cost while preserving the feasible set and objective. Closed-loop Monte Carlo evaluation over six highway and intersection scenarios shows the highest strict success rate among the compared planners with no observed collisions or rule violations, at roughly a quarter of the planning latency of the unoptimized formulation. An ablation study separates the two layers: the hard constraints define the safety envelope, while the soft terms improve clearance and motion quality within it.

</details>


### [99] [Jacobian State Augmented Viable Kinematic Safety Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15936)
*Siddharth Singh,Joshua Vaughan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于雅可比的运动学安全滤波器，通过嵌套二次规划与在线可行规划器，在保证关节位置和速度约束的同时提升滤波可行性，计算效率优于传统PSF与参考调节器方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测安全滤波器（PSF）虽能改善约束满足并减少因短视单步滤波导致的可行性损失，但现有基于可行性的方法需近似可行性核，计算代价高昂；因此需要一种既能保持低层滤波器可行性、又计算高效的安全滤波方案。

Method: 提出基于雅可比的运动学安全滤波器，引入增广状态变量（含任务空间速度），对任务空间加速度进行滤波以保持低层滤波器可行性并施加关节位置与速度约束；构造嵌套二次规划（QP）形式，定义可行性包络的保守内近似，并在线计算可行规划器。

Result: 在简化2R平面机械臂仿真中验证，相较基线CBF-QP滤波器有显著改进；计算时间优于传统PSF与参考调节器方法，且可行性违规显著减少；在仿真6自由度机械臂上的简化演示显示了方法向更高自由度场景扩展的潜力。

Conclusion: 该方法通过嵌套QP与保守可行性包络内近似，在保证安全约束的同时兼顾计算效率与可行性，具备向高自由度机械臂系统扩展的潜力。

Abstract: Safety Filters (SF) are leveraged to design certifiably safe controllers, which ensures forward invariance on a defined safe set when the filter is feasible. Predictive Safety Filters (PSF) embed these constraints to a closed-loop model-based optimization method to improve constraint satisfaction, and reduce feasibility loss caused by myopic, one-step filtering. Related viability-based methods emphasize approximating a viability kernel which ensures admissible control for all future time, but requires significant computational expense. This work introduces a Jacobian-based kinematic safety filter leveraging an augmented state variable with task-space velocity and filters task-space acceleration to preserve the feasibility of a low-level filter while enforcing joint position and velocity. The proposed method introduces a nested Quadratic Program (QP) formulation, defines a conservative inner approximation of viability envelope and computes a viable planner online. The method is validated in a simplified 2R planar manipulator simulation environment and shows significant improvement compared to a baseline Control Barrier Function Quadratic Program (CBF-QP) based filter. The experiments highlights the improvements in terms of computation time compared to traditional PSF and Reference Governor approach while also showing significant reduction in feasibility violations. Lastly, a simplified demonstration on a simulated 6-DoF manipulator highlights the potential scalability of the method to higher degree-of-freedom setups.

</details>


### [100] [A Hardware-Partitioned micro-ROS Mini-Vehicle for Operator-Supervised Neural Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.15966)
*Gustavo Claudio Karl Couto,Eric Aislan Antonelo,Gabriel George Zipperer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种低成本微型阿克曼转向车辆平台，面向嵌入式神经驾驶研究，采用"感知-决策-执行"硬件分区架构，验证了在无线闭环神经驾驶中该平台的可行性与可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向嵌入式神经驾驶研究需要一个低成本、可复现、且能隔离实时安全控制与学习型组件的实验平台，以支持嵌入式AI、无线控制与有人监督的自动驾驶实验。

Method: 将系统硬件分区为感知-决策-执行三层：ESP32摄像头节点经Wi-Fi将帧流传输到主机，主机运行神经推理与状态估计；ESP32-S3底盘控制器在micro-ROS客户端下运行100 Hz逐轮PID回路，执行安全关键作动，由游戏手柄监督。在真实闭环神经驾驶下对全流程进行表征。

Result: 无线感知而非神经推理主导延迟，捕捉到作动的中位延迟为59.5 ms；固件看门狗在无线波动时保持最后安全指令，使平台在12次自主会话中实现零碰撞、零人工干预；编码器-IMU融合使里程计闭环误差降低22%。

Conclusion: 经精心分区的微控制器级车辆能够支持嵌入式AI、无线控制与有人监督自动驾驶的可复现实验，平台软件已开源。

Abstract: We present a low-cost miniature Ackermann vehicle for embedded neural-driving research, organized as a hardware-partitioned perceive-decide-act agent: an ESP32 camera node streams frames over Wi-Fi to a host computer, where neural inference and state estimation run, while an ESP32-S3 chassis controller executes safety-critical actuation in a 100 Hz per-wheel proportional-integral-derivative (PID) loop beneath a micro-ROS client. The partition isolates real-time control and safety on the vehicle while learning-based components run off-board, supervised by an operator from a gamepad. We characterize the complete pipeline during real closed-loop neural driving. Wireless sensing, rather than neural inference, dominates latency, with a 59.5 ms median capture-to-actuation delay; the platform absorbed wireless variability by holding the last safe command under a firmware watchdog, operating across twelve autonomous sessions with zero collisions or manual interventions; and encoder-inertial-measurement-unit (IMU) fusion cut odometry loop-closure error by 22%. These results indicate that carefully partitioned microcontroller-class vehicles support reproducible experiments in embedded AI, wireless control, and operator-supervised autonomous driving. The platform software is open source.

</details>


### [101] [PathLift: Depth-Conditioned Metric Lifting for Visual-Navigation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16103)
*Gwanjun Shin,Hyeongseok Suh,Hongmo Jung,Jongwoo Lim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PathLift：一种逐路点的深度条件适配器，将预训练视觉导航策略的无单位轨迹“抬升”为可被控制器跟踪的度量路径，一次前馈即可完成，在仿真与真实轮式机器人上均提升导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用视觉导航策略只告诉机器人方向，不告诉距离，需额外的“抬升”步骤把无单位轨迹转为度量路径。现有方法不足：标量抬升器只做统一缩放，忽略逐路点修正；把单目深度注入策略的做法则继承了深度模型在障碍物附近和近距离处的预测误差。

Method: PathLift 作为逐路点、深度条件的适配器，消费显式传感器级深度源（RGB-D、立体或投影 LiDAR），并针对规范化后的先验轨迹，拟合每个路点的幅值与横向残差。

Result: 在仿真中跨多种视觉导航策略、以及在真实轮式机器人上（立体与 LiDAR 两种深度）的闭环导航均获得一致提升；无需测试时优化即可提升真实场景成功率。

Conclusion: 通过单次前馈把策略的无单位意图转化为深度可行的度量路径，PathLift 有效克服了统一缩放与单目深度误差的局限，是一种通用、可即插即用的抬升方案。

Abstract: Generalist visual-navigation policies tell a robot which direction to go but not how far. A separate lifting step is needed at deployment to convert their unitless trajectory into a metric path that a controller can track; existing scalar lifters apply uniform scaling that misses per-waypoint corrections, while in-policy approaches that inject monocular depth into the policy inherit the prediction errors of the depth model near obstacles and at close range.
  We present PathLift, a per-waypoint depth-conditioned adapter that lifts a pre-trained visual-navigation policy by consuming an explicit, sensor-grade depth source (RGB-D, stereo, or projected LiDAR) and fitting per-waypoint magnitude and lateral residuals to a canonicalized prior.
  PathLift consistently improves closed-loop navigation across diverse visual-navigation policies in simulation and on a real wheeled robot under both stereo and LiDAR depth. By turning the policy's unitless intent into a depth-feasible metric path in a single feed-forward pass, the adapter lifts real-world success without test-time optimization.

</details>


### [102] [Constrained Differentiable Policy Learning for Safe Quadrotor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16119)
*Yan Rui Tan,Praveen Krishnapur,Zeyu Feng,Han Bao,Yew-Soon Ong,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文将避障作为显式约束，通过可微仿真与原始-对偶梯度下降-上升训练四旋翼策略，提出有界间隙约束函数与LME聚合，实现安全等级直接指定并兼顾任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微仿真虽能借助解析梯度高效优化策略，但安全性多以加权惩罚形式引入，需要手动调节任务性能与约束满足之间的权衡，缺乏对安全等级的显式控制。

Method: 将避障建模为显式约束，在可微仿真中用原始-对偶梯度下降-上升训练四旋翼策略；设计有界间隙（clearance）约束函数以在安全边界附近提供有效梯度并抑制大违规导致的不稳定；引入log-mean-exp（LME）聚合，将轨迹与批次内关键的逐步安全违规平滑地整合为单一约束，由单一对偶变量控制。

Result: 实验显示相比固定惩罚与现有策略学习基线，安全-性能权衡更优，并能泛化到更密集的分布外环境；学习到的策略无需额外微调即可零样本迁移到Gazebo/PX4与实体四旋翼。

Conclusion: 在温和假设下理论证明了所提可微仿真优化收敛到可行策略；该框架可直接指定目标安全等级并平衡约束满足与任务完成。

Abstract: Differentiable simulation enables efficient policy optimization through analytic gradients, yet safety is commonly incorporated as a weighted penalty, requiring manual tuning of the trade-off between task performance and constraint satisfaction. We instead formulate obstacle avoidance as an explicit constraint and train quadrotor policies using primal--dual gradient descent--ascent through a differentiable simulator. We introduce two key components to enable effective constrained training. First, we propose a bounded clearance-based constraint function that provides informative gradients around the safety boundary while preventing large violations from destabilizing primal--dual optimization. Second, we introduce log-mean-exp (LME) aggregation to smoothly emphasize critical per-step safety violations across a trajectory and training batch within a single constraint governed by one dual variable. Together, these components enable the desired safety level to be specified directly while balancing constraint satisfaction and task completion. We theoretically prove that the proposed optimization with differentiable simulation converges to feasible policy under mild assumptions. Experiments demonstrate improved safety--performance trade-offs over fixed-penalty and existing policy-learning baselines, as well as generalization to denser out-of-distribution environments. Finally, the learned policy transfers zero-shot to Gazebo/PX4 and a physical quadrotor without additional fine-tuning.

</details>


### [103] [Directional Pullback Regularization for Long-Horizon Rollout of Latent World Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16139)
*Shatadal Mishra,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Nejib Ammar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出方向性拉回正则化(DPR)，在潜空间中引入运动学感知的黎曼度量以改进自动驾驶长时程滚动预测质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶世界模型多在无约束潜空间中学习，但所代表的物理系统受严格非完整约束限制：地面车辆无法实现瞬时纯横向运动。作者探究能否通过为潜转移赋予运动感知几何结构（而非重新设计预测器架构）来提升长时程潜滚动建模质量。

Method: 从无约束潜滚动基线出发，提出训练时方法DPR：在解码状态空间定义运动学感知的各向异性黎曼度量，并通过解码器雅可比将其拉回到潜流形。所得潜空间度量对解码为车辆横向运动的方向赋予更高代价，同时保留原有编码器-动力学-解码器骨干。由此形成ULR（无约束潜滚动）、EPR（欧氏拉回正则化消融）与DPR（完整方向性方法）的三方对比。

Result: 在统一10秒测试时域的开环自车运动滚动上评估，训练滚动长度设为1、3、5、10步。在四种训练设置下，DPR的10秒最终位移误差均低于ULR和EPR。相对精度提升最显著的是3步滚动监督训练，DPR较ULR降低约44%的10秒最终位移误差。较长滚动训练（尤其10步）获得最强的整体控制相关权衡，DPR在位移误差、横向运动一致性和前向运动保持三方面同时优于ULR和EPR。

Conclusion: 通过为潜转移赋予运动学感知的各向异性几何（DPR），可在不改变模型架构的前提下显著提升长时程潜滚动预测的精度与控制相关指标，为自动驾驶世界模型提供简单有效的训练时正则化手段。

Abstract: Learned world models for autonomous driving operate in unconstrained latent spaces, yet the physical systems they represent are governed by strict non-holonomic constraints: ground vehicles cannot realize instantaneous pure lateral motion. We study long-horizon latent rollout modeling for autonomous driving and ask whether rollout quality can be improved by assigning a motion-aware geometry to latent transitions, rather than by redesigning the predictor architecture. Starting from an unconstrained latent rollout baseline, we introduce Directional Pullback Regularization (DPR), a training time method that defines a kinematics-aware anisotropic Riemannian metric in decoded state space and pulls it back through the decoder Jacobian to the latent manifold. The resulting latent-space metric assigns higher cost to latent directions that decode to lateral vehicle motion, while preserving the original encoder-dynamics-decoder backbone. This yields a clean three-way comparison among an Unconstrained Latent Rollout model (ULR), an Euclidean Pullback Regularization ablation (EPR), and the full directional method (DPR). We evaluate all models on open-loop ego-motion rollout over a common 10-second test horizon, using training rollout lengths of 1, 3, 5, and 10 steps. Across all four training regimes, DPR achieves lower 10-second final displacement error than both ULR and EPR. The strongest relative accuracy gain appears for models trained with 3-step rollout supervision, where DPR reduces the 10-second final displacement error by approximately 44 percent relative to ULR. The strongest overall control-relevant tradeoff is obtained for longer rollout-trained models, especially at 10 steps, where DPR simultaneously improves displacement error, lateral motion consistency, and forward motion preservation relative to both ULR and EPR.

</details>


### [104] [FlowVLA: Enabling World Dynamics Understanding through Pluggable 3D Flow Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16160)
*Junhyeong Go,Mingyu Jung,Woobin Im,Jongbin Ryu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowVLA是一个重建引导、可插拔的3D流学习框架，仅在训练阶段利用时间对齐的3D流，为VLA模型注入动作相关动力学理解，推理时无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLA模型主要依赖2D图像-文本先验，无法准确表征与物理动作相关的3D世界基本动力学，对静态2D视觉线索的过度依赖限制了机器人操作的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出FlowVLA框架，无需显式3D传感器输入或改变推理架构，仅在训练时使用时间对齐的3D流。设计模块化3D流学习框架，采用双层流监督：点级流预测任务关键3D点的未来位移，TCP级流聚合这些运动以对齐机器人TCP轨迹。训练时仅添加轻量级辅助头。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中的广泛评估表明，FlowVLA持续提升操作性能、零样本鲁棒性，并优化数据效率。

Conclusion: FlowVLA推理时丢弃辅助头，不引入额外3D传感器输入、架构修改或推理时计算，同时有效耦合场景级运动与精确控制信号。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D reconstruction enable the extraction of temporally consistent 3D motion from RGB observations; however, current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models still predominantly rely on 2D image-text priors. Therefore, they do not accurately represent the fundamental dynamics of the 3D world associated with physical actions. This over-reliance on static 2D visual cues limits the capacity of VLA models in achieving robust robot manipulation. To address these limitations, we introduce FlowVLA, a reconstruction-guided, pluggable 3D flow learning framework aimed at empowering VLA models with an inherent understanding of action-relevant dynamics. Instead of requiring explicit 3D sensor inputs or altering the inference-time architecture, our method is designed to utilize temporally aligned 3D flow only during training. This allows the model to learn the dynamic evolution of the physical scene while preserving the computational efficiency of conventional 2D VLA backbones. To bridge scene-level dynamics with robotic manipulation, we propose a modular 3D flow learning framework with dual-level flow supervision. Specifically, point-level flow guides the model to forecast the future displacements of task-critical 3D points, while TCP-level flow aggregates these motions to align with the robot's TCP trajectories. This dual-level flow supervision encourages the model to focus on semantically meaningful dynamic regions, effectively coupling scene-level motion with precise control signals. Extensive evaluations across simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that FlowVLA consistently improves manipulation performance, zero-shot robustness, and optimizes data efficiency. FlowVLA adds only lightweight auxiliary heads during training and discards them at inference. Therefore, FlowVLA introduces no additional 3D sensor inputs, architectural modifications, or inference-time computation.

</details>


### [105] [Extending Wire-Driven Parallel Robots to Multi-Floating-Link Systems with an Inter-Body Wire](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16173)
*Shintaro Inoue,Kento Kawaharazuka,Temma Suzuki,Sota Yuzaki,Keita Yoneda,Kei Okada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过引入"体间线缆"，将传统单一末端执行器的并联线驱动系统扩展为多浮动连杆系统，并成功实现救援搬运任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统并联线驱动系统通常假设单一末端执行器，由于共享工作空间中线缆与连杆之间存在干涉，难以同时操控多个浮动连杆。作者希望突破这一限制，将并联线驱动从单末端执行器扩展到多浮动连杆系统，以支持更广泛的任务。

Method: 引入"体间线缆"（inter-body wire），实现多浮动连杆的并联线驱动，并搭建真实机器人进行实验验证。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中，两个机体协同支撑并抬起了一个人体模型，将其转移到担架上，完成了一次救援动作。

Conclusion: 表明并联线驱动可以从单一末端执行器扩展到多浮动连杆系统，并显示出其在大物体操控和救援支持等更广泛任务中的潜力。

Abstract: Parallel wire-driven systems are a framework in which multiple wires are connected to an end effector, and the end effector is driven by the tensions of those wires. Because they can transmit large forces, up to the breaking strength of the wires, over lengths on the order of several tens of meters, they can realize powerful actuation in large workspaces. In recent years, studies have also investigated connecting wires to external environments such as trees and columns and forming parallel wire actuation on site. However, conventional parallel wire-driven systems often assume a single end effector, and because of interference between wires and links in a shared workspace, it has been difficult to handle multiple floating links simultaneously. In this study, we realize multi-floating-link parallel wire actuation by introducing an inter-body wire. In real-robot experiments, the two bodies supported and lifted a mannequin and transferred it onto a stretcher as a rescue motion. This shows that parallel wire actuation can be extended from a single end effector to a multi-floating-link system, and indicates its potential for broader tasks such as the manipulation of large objects and rescue support.

</details>


### [106] [One-Shot Learning for Double-Shot Latte Art: Adapting Cobot Pouring Skills with Dynamic Movement Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16179)
*Zain Khan,Victor Hernandez Moreno,Dinh Tung Le*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分阶段笛卡尔动态运动基元(CDMP)的单次学习系统，用于拉花(latte art)任务的机器人复现与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拉花需要高度灵巧性与精确性，是具商业价值但难度很大的任务；同时单次演示学习在复杂多阶段任务上面临精度不足与难以适应不同条件的挑战。

Method: 将单次咖啡师演示分割为五个功能不同的阶段，每阶段编码为独立的CDMP并赋予各自参数；与单一CDMP基线对比；通过单一空间偏移与缩放配方实现从280mL到400mL杯子的迁移，无需重新演示。

Result: 相比单CDMP基线，分阶段编码将过渡端点误差降低3.2–4.5倍、图案笔画形状误差降低2.7倍；在倾倒阶段壶身倾角复现RMS低于1度；无需重新演示即可从280mL迁移至400mL杯子。

Conclusion: 分阶段CDMP方法可推广到任何各阶段具有不同空间或时间要求的演示任务，并能根据任务需求调整复现，具备良好的泛化与适应能力。

Abstract: Latte art is a complex task requiring dexterity and precision, making it a challenging and commercially relevant task. A one-shot learning system is employed in which a single barista demonstration is segmented into five functionally distinct phases, each encoded as an independent Cartesian Dynamic Movement Primitive (CDMP) with its own parameters. Against a single-CDMP baseline, per-phase encoding reduces transition endpoint error by 3.2-4.5 times and art-stroke shape error by 2.7 times, reproducing jug tilt to below 1 degree RMS in the pouring phases. The learnt pour scales from a 280mL to a 400mL cup through a single spatial offset and a scaled recipe, with no re-demonstration. The phase-based CDMP approach generalises beyond latte art to any demonstration task whose phases have distinct spatial or temporal requirements whilst demonstrating the ability to adapt reproductions to suit different task requirements.

</details>


### [107] [MSC: Multi-Stage Caster-aware Control Framework with Uncertainty-Aware NMPC and RL-based Execution Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16199)
*Jinho Park,Jeongtae Lee,Jeonghyeok Lim,Keun Ha Choi,Kyung-Soo Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出多阶段脚轮感知控制（MSC），通过不确定性感知NMPC与强化学习执行层，缩小规划-执行差距，提升脚轮敏感操作的跟踪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业AMR/SDV广泛使用自由旋转脚轮，但低速或大曲率操作中脚轮错位引发强接触非线性，导致指令与实际运动偏差；现有脚轮感知策略依赖状态估计可靠性，在不确定过渡区间执行不稳定。

Method: MSC规划-执行一体化分层框架，含两个机制：(1) 不确定性感知NMPC（CU-MPC），利用预测不确定性动态调度脚轮感知代价与输入正则化，抑制过激操作；(2) 基于强化学习的执行层，在部分可观测下估计与预测脚轮状态，将高层速度指令转换为适应当前接触条件的可执行轮速。

Result: 在Isaac Sim与真实机器人上验证，MSC相较现有解耦补偿方法具有明显优势，显著提升脚轮状态估计精度、减少错位并增强脚轮敏感操作的跟踪鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MSC通过规划与执行一体化及不确定性感知机制，有效缓解脚轮错位引起的规划-执行差距，提升复杂操作下移动机器人的执行稳定性与跟踪性能。

Abstract: Freely swiveling casters are widely adopted in industrial AMRs/SDVs for their mechanical simplicity and load capacity. However, caster misalignment during low-speed or high-curvature maneuvers induces strong contact nonlinearities, widening the planning-execution gap between commanded $(v, ω)$ and realized motion. Existing caster-aware strategies are highly sensitive to state-estimation reliability, causing unstable execution in uncertain transition regimes. To address this, we present Multi-Stage Caster-aware Control (MSC), a planning-execution integrated hierarchical framework featuring two core mechanisms. First, an uncertainty-aware NMPC (CU-MPC) dynamically schedules caster-aware costs and input regularization using predictive uncertainty to suppress overly aggressive maneuvers. Second, a reinforcement learning-based execution layer estimates and predicts caster states under partial observability, transforming high-level velocity commands into physically executable wheel speeds adapted to current contact conditions.
