Validations in Isaac Sim and real robots demonstrate that MSC offers a clear comparative advantage over existing decoupled compensation approaches by significantly improving caster-state estimation accuracy, minimizing misalignment, and enhancing tracking robustness in caster-sensitive maneuvers.

</details>


### [108] [Embodied Skill Bench: Benchmarking How Well Agents with Embodied Skills Perform on Physical Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16238)
*Zhi Han,Zihan Guo,Chenxi Zeng,Zicai Cui,Yue Xin,Ming Zhou,Dongzhan Zhou,Weinan Zhang,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将机器人控制相关模块封装为带具身假设与使用条件的"具身技能"，构建250个技能与EmbodiedSkillBench基准，在100个LIBERO-Pro扰动任务上评测四种Agent的三种模式。结果显示技能收益取决于其超出Agent本身能力的程度：技能专用执行显著提升弱Agent并节省token，但会拖累强Agent；提出的ADR模式让强Agent保留原生控制并选择性调用技能，可恢复至接近原生水平同时降低token与时间成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent越来越多地通过VLA策略、感知模型和程序性知识控制机器人，但这些模块因接口、具身假设和使用条件不同而难以复用，且它们在物理任务上的实际收益尚不清楚。因此作者希望建立统一的具身技能封装方式与可评测框架，厘清技能对Agent的真实价值。

Method: 作者将模块封装为"具身技能"，显式标注具身假设与使用条件，从1052个资源中构建250个动作、感知与经验技能；提出EmbodiedSkillBench，在闭环观察-动作循环中记录成功率、动作、token和实际耗时，支持原生控制、仅技能执行和作者提出的Agent-Directed Reuse（ADR）三种模式，ADR下Agent保留原生控制并自行决定每个技能是调用、修改还是忽略。

Result: 在100个扰动LIBERO-Pro任务上评测四种Agent：仅技能执行将GPT-5.6-Sol从11%提升至63%，Kimi-K3和Claude-Opus-5各提升约20个百分点，同时token减少58%–85%；但四者最终均落在61%–64%，而GPT-6-Astra从原生控制下的92%降至61%。ADR下GPT-6-Astra恢复至85%，距原生控制仅7个百分点，token减少14.1%、时间减少11.9%；以Jev作为选择运动方向与夹爪动作的技能时，达到81%且token减少50%、时间减少57%。

Conclusion: 具身技能对Agent的帮助取决于其超出Agent既有能力的程度。随着Agent变强，具身技能的角色从"补充缺失能力"转向"降低成本"：强Agent原生控制已能解决大部分任务，仅限技能反而会削减其能力。ADR通过保留原生控制并选择性复用技能，在性能与成本之间取得更优折中。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly control robots using VLA policies, perception models, and procedural knowledge. These modules are difficult to reuse because their interfaces, embodiment assumptions, and conditions of use differ, and their benefits on physical tasks remain unclear. We package them as embodied skills that extend agent skills with explicit embodiment assumptions and conditions of use, building 250 action, perception, and experience skills from 1,052 resources. On this basis we introduce EmbodiedSkillBench, which runs an agent in a closed observation-action loop and records success, actions, tokens, and wall-clock time. It supports three modes: native control, skill-only execution, and Agent-Directed Reuse (ADR), a mode we propose in which the agent keeps its native controls and decides for each skill whether to invoke, modify, or ignore it. Across four agents on 100 perturbed LIBERO-Pro tasks, we find that skills help an agent only as far as they exceed what it can already do. Skill-only execution lifts GPT-5.6-Sol from 11% to 63% and Kimi-K3 and Claude-Opus-5 by about 20 points, while cutting their token usage by 58% to 85%. Yet all four agents then end up between 61% and 64%, and GPT-6-Astra falls from 92% under native control to 61%. This suggests that as agents grow stronger, the role of embodied skills shifts from supplying missing capability to reducing cost: GPT-6-Astra already solves most tasks through native control, so restricting it to skills removes more than it adds. Under ADR, which lets it keep native control and call skills selectively, GPT-6-Astra recovers to 85%, within 7 points of native control, while using 14.1% fewer tokens and 11.9% less time. With Jev as a skill choosing motion directions and gripper actions, it reaches 81% with 50% fewer tokens and 57% less time. The code is available at https://github.com/HanZhi306/EmbodiedSkill-Bench.

</details>


### [109] [Same Goal, Different Words: Spatial Instruction Sensitivity in World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16242)
*Ali-Adeeb Abbas,Esmaeil Seraj,Behrad Toghi,Taskin Padir*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过864次rollout评估世界-行动模型对等价空间指令描述的一致性，发现表述方式会显著影响任务成功率，且聚合分数会掩盖措辞敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-行动模型应能在同一空间目标的等价描述下保持一致的操控行为，但现有评估可能无法揭示模型对指令措辞的敏感性，因此需要系统性检验其可操控性、语言理解与grounding。

Method: 在四个RoboLab场景中，固定初始状态与任务目标，对三个模型进行864次rollout；比较通过参考物体与待移动物体描述关系的差异，并单独测试策略的语言理解能力。

Result: 通过参考物体描述关系会使稳定放置率平均下降12.2个百分点；指令改写的影响因空间关系而异；语言理解测试发现关系反转，加入图像会干扰原本正确的纯文本理解；稳定放置率从任意时刻的43.2%降至episode结束时的21.8%。

Conclusion: 应更全面地评估世界-行动模型的可操控性、语言理解与grounding，需测试等价指令一致性、确认目标物体是否移动，并检验请求的空间布局是否持续到rollout结束。

Abstract: World-action models should preserve the intended manipulation across equivalent descriptions of a spatial goal. We evaluate this consistency in 864 rollouts from three models across four RoboLab scenes, holding initial states and task goals fixed. Describing relations through the reference object rather than the object-to-move lowers the rate of reaching a stable placement by 12.2 percentage points on average. The effect of instruction rephrasing varies across spatial relations, so aggregate scores can hide sensitivity to wording. Standalone tests of the policies' language understanding identify relation reversals and show that adding images can disrupt otherwise correct text-only interpretations. Rollout analysis also distinguishes spatial placement from instruction following: moving the wrong object can satisfy the requested relation, and rates of stable placement fall from 43.2% at any time to 21.8% at episode end. These findings motivate a more thorough evaluation of world-action models' steerability, language understanding, and grounding. Evaluations should test consistency across equivalent instructions, verify that the intended object moved, and check whether the requested arrangement persists to the end of the rollout.

</details>


### [110] [PACE: Temporal-Role Tactile Conditioning for Chunk-Based Insertion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16308)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Shixiong Xu,Shuai Chen,Yangwei You*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACE将触觉反馈按时间角色分为持续接触状态、突发接触事件与接触后果预测三条通路，用于chunk式插入策略，提升成功率并跨信号、跨架构泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的插入任务需结合视觉对齐与触觉调整，但现有visuotactile chunk策略把触觉反馈压缩到单一生成时融合通路，导致持续接触状态、突发接触事件与未来接触后果被隐式建模在同一表示中。

Method: 提出PACE（Phase-Aware Contact-Event Conditioning）时间角色触觉条件框架：将共享触觉流组织为三种时间角色——持续接触状态条件化动作chunk生成；突发接触事件校正已生成chunk；接触后果预测在训练时塑造动作表示，从而保持chunk级动作连续性并分配不同通路。

Result: 在五项真实机器人插入任务中平均成功率90.7%，比仅视觉与生成时触觉融合基线高出超44个百分点；ManipulationNet基准六种几何间隙配置下保持88.7%成功率，含工业级0.1mm间隙。消融验证三角色互补，机制分析显示PACE区分正常插入进展与需即时校正的接触事件，跨信号与跨架构实验表明该时间角色原则可扩展至不同物理反馈信号与策略骨干。

Conclusion: PACE通过按时间角色分离触觉条件通路，有效提升接触丰富插入任务的成功率与泛化能力，并验证三通路设计的互补性与可迁移性。

Abstract: Contact-rich insertion requires robots to combine visual alignment with tactile adjustment. However, existing visuotactile chunk-based policies collapse tactile feedback into a single generation-time fusion pathway, leaving persistent contact states, abrupt contact events, and future contact consequences implicitly modeled in a single representation. We present PACE (Phase-Aware Contact-Event Conditioning), a temporal-role tactile conditioning framework for chunk-based insertion policies. PACE organizes a shared tactile stream into three temporal roles: persistent contact states condition action-chunk generation, abrupt contact events correct the generated chunk, and contact-consequence prediction shapes action representations during training. This design preserves chunk-level action continuity while assigning distinct pathways to different temporal roles of contact feedback. Across five real-robot insertion tasks, PACE achieves an average success rate of 90.7%, outperforming vision-only and generation-time tactile-fusion baselines by over 44 percentage points; on the ManipulationNet benchmark, PACE maintains 88.7% success across six geometry-clearance configurations, including an industrial-grade 0.1-mm clearance. Ablations confirm the complementary contributions of the three temporal roles, mechanistic analyses show that PACE separates normal insertion progress from contact events requiring immediate correction, and cross-signal and cross-architecture experiments indicate that this temporal-role principle extends across different physical feedback signals and policy backbones. Videos and materials are available at https://pace-insertion.github.io/.

</details>


### [111] [ReAP: From Visual Failure Recognition to Action-Conditioned Risk Prediction for Runtime Failure Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16309)
*Jingyuan Liu,Yunpeng Qing,Changxu Wei,Zhi Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReAP 是一个面向通用机器人策略的未来感知运行时监控框架，结合视觉失败识别与动作条件失败预测，在动作执行前预判风险，兼顾检测准确性与预警及时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略执行时易失败、较为脆弱。现有监控器多依赖当前观测或策略信号估计风险，未显式预测所提议动作的未来后果，无法在动作执行前提前预警。

Method: ReAP 先在成功与失败的 rollout 上训练视觉检测器，学习与失败相关的表征空间并估计当前风险；再用动作条件预测器在该空间中预测未来表征，从而估计未来失败风险。运行时检测器与预测器提供互补信号，分别实现当前状态失败识别与未来感知风险评估。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的多种机器人策略上实验表明，ReAP 能有效平衡失败检测准确性与预警及时性。

Conclusion: 通过显式预测动作的未来后果，ReAP 可在动作执行前实现及时失败预警，为通用机器人策略的可靠部署提供有效运行时监控方案。

Abstract: Despite rapid progress, generalist robot policies remain brittle during execution. Reliable deployment therefore requires runtime monitoring that provides timely warnings of failure. Existing monitors commonly estimate risk from current observations or policy signals without explicitly predicting the future consequences of proposed actions. To anticipate such risks before action execution, we introduce ReAP, a future-aware runtime monitoring framework that combines visual failure recognition with action-conditioned failure prediction. Specifically, ReAP first trains a visual detector on successful and failed rollouts to learn a failure-relevant representation space and estimate current risk. An action-conditioned predictor then forecasts the resulting future representation in this space to estimate future failure risk. At runtime, the detector and predictor provide complementary signals for current-state failure recognition and future-aware risk assessment. Experiments across diverse robot policies in simulation and the real world demonstrate that ReAP effectively balances failure-detection accuracy and warning timeliness.

</details>


### [112] [Listen Before Act: Audio-Conditioned Robot Manipulation with ListenVLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16313)
*Jiaqi Fan,Rui Xu,Jiakun Xia,Xuyu Fan,Shuailei Ma,Yuefeng Wang,Qing Wang,Jiarui Zhang,Yili Fu,Xu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出音频条件视觉-语言-动作模型ListenVLA，结合语义音频嵌入与波形声学线索，通过音频token与特征调制生成动作，并设计音频-动作绑定目标，构建LIBERO-Audio基准，在仿真与真实任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖视觉与语言，但许多操作任务依赖视觉难以推断、需通过物理交互激发的听觉线索，存在听觉信息缺失的缺口。

Method: 基于GR00T N1.7构建音频条件策略ListenVLA：融合语义音频嵌入与波形声学线索，使用因果音频历史；通过音频token与特征调制条件化连续动作生成；提出音频-动作绑定目标，用上下文匹配演示对齐听觉差异与动作差异，在共享纯噪声动作输入与固定非音频观测下监督不同音频条件间流速度预测的短时差异，并辅以全序列动作监督。

Result: 在LIBERO-Audio（八任务基准，涵盖基于声音的目标选择、听觉事件响应与揭示声学证据的物理交互）及三个真实世界操作任务上取得优异性能；消融实验支持绑定监督与音频调制的贡献，音频互换测试表明声音会影响动作预测。

Conclusion: ListenVLA证明音频可作为VLA策略的有效条件模态，音频-动作绑定监督与音频调制是关键，为声音引导的机器人操作提供了新基准与方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have advanced robotic manipulation by connecting visual perception and language instructions to action generation. However, many manipulation tasks depend on auditory cues that cannot be reliably inferred from visual observations and may need to be elicited through physical interaction. To address this gap, we present ListenVLA, an audio-conditioned VLA policy built on GR00T N1.7 for sound-guided manipulation. ListenVLA combines semantic audio embeddings with waveform-derived acoustic cues over a causal audio history and conditions continuous action generation through audio tokens and feature modulation. Moreover, we introduce an audio-action binding objective that uses context-matched demonstrations to link auditory differences to demonstrated action differences. The objective supervises short-horizon differences between flow-velocity predictions under different audio conditions, with shared pure-noise action inputs and fixed non-audio observations, alongside full-chunk action supervision. Furthermore, we develop LIBERO-Audio, an eight-task benchmark covering sound-based target selection, responses to auditory events, and physical interactions that reveal acoustic evidence. Experiments on LIBERO-Audio and three real-world manipulation tasks show excellent performance. Ablation studies support the contributions of binding supervision and audio modulation, while audio swap tests show that sound influences action predictions. Our code and videos are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/listenvla-F18D

</details>


### [113] [Constraint-Decomposed Impedance Control from Generative Policy for Articulated Object Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16316)
*Kyungseo Park,Byeongdo Lim,Jungbin Lim,Minseok Choi,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种约束分解阻抗控制方法，利用生成式策略的采样多样性从末端执行器轨迹中推断可行/受约束扭转子空间，并用PCA构造固定的6×6阻抗增益矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铰接物体操作任务对末端执行器运动施加运动学约束，神经策略可能难以满足；阻抗控制虽优于混合力位控制，但选取合适的阻抗增益对策略而言仍具挑战。

Method: 对同一观测下策略生成的多条轨迹集合中的末端扭转做PCA，得到可行扭转子空间并据此形成增益矩阵；对可行子空间随构型变化的情况，估计包含局部可行子空间的高维构型无关子空间，从而沿用固定增益策略。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界的单自由度和多自由度物体实验中，相较各向同性阻抗控制，该方法在保持任务执行的同时一致地降低了违反约束的接触载荷。

Conclusion: 通过约束分解与一次性估计固定阻抗增益，可在低计算开销下改善铰接物体操作中的约束满足与接触力表现。

Abstract: Articulated object manipulation tasks such as opening doors, tightening bolts, and pulling drawers impose kinematic constraints on a robot end-effector's motion that neural policies may fail to satisfy. Unlike hybrid force-position control that strictly separates force and position control into orthogonal subspaces, impedance control modulates a mechanical impedance system in the form of a virtual inertia, damping, and stiffness. However, selecting task-appropriate impedance gains remains challenging for robot policies. In this paper, we address this problem for the case of manipulating articulated objects. Our constraint-decomposed impedance control method uses the sampling diversity of a generative policy to estimate the feasible motion directions in twist space. The key idea is to infer the feasible and constrained subspaces of twists from an ensemble of trajectories generated by the policy from the same observation. Principal component analysis (PCA) of the end-effector twists determines the feasible twist subspace, which is then used to form 6x6 impedance gain matrices. For many common articulated objects, the feasible twist subspace is independent of the object configuration, so the subspace and resulting gains are estimated once and held fixed during manipulation, minimizing computational overhead. For less common cases where this independence does not hold, we instead estimate a configuration-independent twist subspace of higher dimension that contains the local feasible subspaces, allowing the same fixed-gain strategy to be used. Experiments across simulated and real-world single- and multi-DoF objects show that the proposed method consistently reduces constraint-violating contact loads relative to isotropic impedance control while maintaining task execution.

</details>


### [114] [Imitation Learning of Avoidance Modes for Efficient Multimodal Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16326)
*Changyu Lee,Byung-Kwan Lee,Kanghoon Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SideNet-MPC，用模仿MM-MPC专家的图神经网络模式决策替代避障侧组合枚举，每控制周期最多求解两次OCP，将计算量从指数级降为常数级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模型预测控制（MM-MPC）需为每种避障侧组合求解一个最优控制问题，计算成本随决策关键障碍物数量指数增长，难以实时应用。

Method: 将MM-MPC作为专家，用图神经网络模仿其最小代价模式选择（每个障碍物一个避障侧）。网络边编码每对障碍物在车辆首次到达任一障碍物时刻的预测间隙，使阻挡优先通道的邻居可改变所提议的侧向。保留专家比较最小代价模式与上一执行模式的选择规则，仅用网络提议替代最小代价模式，故每周期最多求解两个OCP，且所有实际输入仍来自带控制障碍函数约束的OCP。

Result: 100组种子匹配的闭环蒙特卡洛混合交通实验中，SideNet-MPC达到专家成功率的93%，单线程每控制周期18.6ms，比专家16线程并行35.5ms少48%。在受控间隙扫描中，其提议侧向与专家在相同间隙区间发生翻转；而无成对边、用相同数据训练的其他提议器从不翻转，并在确定性场景的阻挡门口全部死锁。

Conclusion: 学习式模式决策可在保持OCP约束安全性的同时大幅降低MM-MPC计算成本，成对障碍物边对间隙驱动的侧向翻转与避免死锁至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal model predictive control (MM-MPC) avoids local minima among moving obstacles by solving one optimal control problem (OCP) per combination of avoidance sides, so its computational cost grows exponentially with the number of decision-critical obstacles. We propose SideNet-MPC, which replaces this enumeration with a mode decision learned by imitating MM-MPC as an expert. A graph neural network is trained to predict the expert's minimum-cost mode, one avoidance side per obstacle. Its edges encode, for each obstacle pair, the gap predicted for the moment the vehicle first reaches either obstacle, so a neighbor that blocks a preferred passage can reverse the proposed side. The expert's selection rule compares its minimum-cost mode with the previously executed mode; SideNet-MPC keeps this rule and substitutes the network proposal for the minimum-cost mode. Hence at most two OCPs are solved per control period, and every applied input still comes from an OCP with control barrier function constraints rather than from the network. In $100$ seed-matched closed-loop Monte Carlo runs of mixed traffic, SideNet-MPC reaches $93\%$ of the expert's success rate and needs \SI{18.6}{ms} per control period on a single thread, 48\% less than the expert's \SI{35.5}{ms} on sixteen parallel threads. In a controlled gap sweep, its proposed side reverses in the same gap interval as the expert's, whereas proposers without pairwise edges, trained on the same data, never reverse and all deadlock at the blocked gates of a deterministic scene.

</details>


### [115] [Toward Study on the Effects of Robot's Humorous Apology on Human's Trust Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16334)
*Gayoung Kim,Go-Eum Cha,Wonse Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究通过2x2被试间实验，探讨幽默型机器人道歉对信任恢复的影响，以及错误严重程度的调节作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人错误会降低用户信任与使用意愿，道歉可能有助于信任恢复，但其效果可能随道歉策略与错误严重程度而变化，因此需检验幽默道歉的作用及其边界条件。

Method: 采用2x2被试间因子设计，交叉机器人错误严重程度（低vs.高）与道歉策略（通用vs.幽默）。使用开源Warehouse Robot Interaction Simulator开展在线实验，参与者完成协作任务、经历机器人错误及随后的道歉，并报告信任、信任恢复、交互体验与复用意向。

Result: 本文为研究设计/计划，尚未报告实际实验结果。

Conclusion: 假设：低严重错误后，幽默道歉比通用道歉带来更强的信任恢复；但高严重错误后，幽默的优势会减弱甚至反转。

Abstract: Robot errors can disrupt human-robot collaboration by reducing user trust and willingness to rely on the robot. Robot apologies can support trust recovery after such errors, but their effects may vary with apology strategy and error severity. This study therefore presents a user experiment based on a 2x2 between-subjects factorial design to examine the effect of humorous robot apologies on user trust recovery and the moderating role of error severity. The design crosses robot error severity (low vs. high) with apology strategy (generic vs. humorous). An online experiment will be conducted using the open-source Warehouse Robot Interaction Simulator. Participants will complete a collaborative task, experience a robot error followed by an apology, and report trust, trust recovery, interaction experience and reuse intention. We hypothesize that humorous apologies will produce greater trust recovery than generic apologies after low-severity errors, but that the advantage of humor will weaken or reverse after high-severity errors.

</details>


### [116] [PINT: A Unified Model with Shared Predictive Intent for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16355)
*Xinyu Guo,Chao Jing,Li Yi,Xingyu Chen,Zhengxing Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PINT是一个通过共享"预测意图"（Predictive Intent）来协调语义推理、世界建模与动作生成的统一机器人操作模型，在保留各专家专用通路的同时显著提升分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一模型在多层级通过共享注意力直接融合视觉语言模型（VLM）与视频生成模型（VGM），会削弱二者各自的专长能力，因此需要一种既能协调互补能力又保留专家专用通路的方案。

Method: PINT在VLM序列中插入可学习的Intent Tokens，形成共享的分块级Predictive Intent表征，用以条件化VGM和动作专家（AE）。它仅替换VGM的prompt条件方式并在推理时保留VGM，AE通过单向注意力读取其表征。该意图不由人工指定，而是从VLM、VGM、AE三者原生预测目标中联合学习。训练分两阶段：Stage 1冻结VGM，让未来视频预测塑造含任务相关动态的意图；Stage 2固定已学意图，微调VGM与AE。

Result: 无需具身预训练，PINT取得强分布内（ID）性能并大幅提升分布外（OOD）泛化：RoboTwin 2.0-Hard达15.5%（Fast-WAM为1.8%、StarVLA为3.2%），LIBERO-Plus达79.5%（对比51.5%和75.0%）。五项真实双臂任务中，ID/OOD成功率为68%/24%，对比预训练Motus的35%/9%和预训练π_{0.5}的67%/35%。

Conclusion: 通过共享的Predictive Intent协调VLM、VGM与AE的学习，PINT在不进行具身预训练的情况下实现了更强的分布内表现与显著更优的分布外泛化，验证了保留专家通路并联合学习共享意图的有效性。

Abstract: Generalist robot manipulation can benefit from pretrained vision-language models (VLMs) for semantic reasoning and video generation models (VGMs) for world modeling. However, existing unified models that directly fuse them through shared attention at multiple layers can make it harder to draw on their specialized capabilities. We introduce PINT, a unified model that coordinates semantic reasoning, world modeling, and action generation through a shared chunk-level Predictive Intent while preserving expert-specific pathways. PINT inserts learnable Intent Tokens into the VLM sequence to form this representation, conditioning both the VGM and an Action Expert (AE). It replaces only the VGM's prompt conditioning and retains the VGM at inference, where the AE reads its representations through one-way attention. Rather than prescribing what Predictive Intent should encode, PINT learns it jointly from the native prediction objectives of the VLM, VGM, and AE, which provide complementary views of the same upcoming task execution. Training uses two stages: Stage 1 freezes the VGM so that future video prediction shapes Predictive Intent with task-relevant future dynamics, while Stage 2 fixes the learned Intent and adapts the VGM and AE. Without embodied pretraining, PINT achieves strong in-distribution (ID) performance and substantially improves out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization, reaching 15.5% on RoboTwin 2.0-Hard compared with 1.8% for Fast-WAM and 3.2% for StarVLA, and 79.5% on LIBERO-Plus compared with 51.5% and 75.0%, respectively. Across five real-world bimanual tasks, PINT reaches 68% ID and 24% OOD success, compared with 35%/9% for pretrained Motus and 67%/35% for pretrained $π_{0.5}$.

</details>


### [117] [RoboBugBench: Benchmarking Automated Repair of Real Robot Software Under an Audited Behavioral Oracle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16369)
*Alexey Tuzikov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboBugBench：33 个真实、经执行验证的 ROS 2 导航栈缺陷基准，用编译+物理仿真行为而非单元测试判分，并审计判分标准本身的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言智能体在改机器人软件，但"能否修复"没有被测量过：该领域没有可继承的判分参照（oracle）。机器人缺陷表现为行为，仓库里几乎没有能捕获它的测试。因此需要一个人工构造判分机制、并对其可靠性做对抗审计的基准。

Method: 构建 33 个真实、执行验证的缺陷（固定版本 ROS 2 导航栈）。候选补丁的判分方式是编译并通过物理仿真驱动机器人，而非跑单元测试。用三个前沿模型各 1 次尝试测试；随后对构造出的判分标准做对抗审计，检验"冒牌补丁"（压制失败表象但未真正修复）、并提出同一数据上的分布式判据；最后分析残余误差的观测上界。

Result: 三个模型修复率 24.2%–36.4%，两两差异不显著；难度跟判分方式走而非领域难度：过程存活型判分 66.7%，行为结果型判分 22.2%（实例级 p = 0.0027）。对抗审计：冒牌补丁被出厂规则全部接受 11/11，而同数据的分布式规则拒绝 4 个且不增加误拒。

Conclusion: 残余误差是观测性上界——观测通道丢弃的信息无法被任何谓词恢复，因此判别力必须靠插桩获取而非加严标准；报告的修复率只是上下界：被判分标准无法区分的情况抬高，被"一致性拒绝正确修复"（运行间噪声）压低。发布语料、判据电池、冒牌补丁套件与缓存遥测。

Abstract: Language agents increasingly modify robot software, but whether they can repair it is unmeasured, because the domain supplies no oracle to inherit: a robot bug manifests as behavior, and robot repositories ship almost no tests that capture it. We present RoboBugBench, a benchmark of 33 real, execution-validated defects in a pinned ROS 2 navigation stack, in which a candidate patch is graded by compiling it and driving the robot through a physics simulation rather than by running a unit test. Three frontier models, each given 1 attempt per instance, repair 24.2%-36.4%, with no pairwise difference significant; difficulty tracks the oracle rather than the domain, at 66.7% on instances graded by process survival against 22.2% on those graded by what the robot did (instance-level p = 0.0027). Because the oracle is constructed rather than inherited we audit it adversarially, and report the decomposition that audit yields: counterfeit patches which suppress a failure without repairing the defect are accepted by the shipped rule 11/11, while a distributional rule over the same data rejects 4 at no cost in false rejects. The residual is an observability bound (what the observation channel discards, no predicate over it can recover), so discrimination must be bought with instrumentation rather than stricter criteria, and our repair rates are bounds: inflated by what the oracle cannot distinguish, deflated by unanimity's rejection of correct fixes under run-to-run noise. We release the corpus, criterion batteries, counterfeit suite and cached telemetry.

</details>


### [118] [LABIT: Long-Horizon Robotic Assembly Benchmark for Industrial Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16398)
*Constantin Schempp,Christian Friedrich,Luca Kofler,Laura Suhm*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LABIT——一个面向长时序操作的工业机器人装配基准，涵盖DIN标准装配任务，并提供评估协议与性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人装配基准多聚焦单一任务，难以反映现代制造流程中多步骤、相互关联的复杂性，因此需要一个覆盖更广装配与子装配步骤的基准来评估智能机器人系统的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出LABIT基准，包含DIN规范装配任务（拧紧、放置、插入、卡扣），分别在高容差与低容差条件下进行；任务具有连续性，前期误差会影响后续操作；设计了基于装配复杂度分类的基准测试协议与新性能指标，并配套零件清单与CAD模型。

Result: 使用传统力位控制作为基线，按基元、子任务、装配类别和子装配报告了可靠性及评分指标，验证了该基准的可用性。

Conclusion: LABIT为长时序工业装配研究提供了标准化评估工具，尤其适用于考察智能机器人系统在连续装配中的鲁棒性，相关数据已公开。

Abstract: Robot learning has undergone significant advancements in the domain of compliant control and robotic assembly. The development of novel methodologies has transcended the capacity to address discrete tasks, such as grasping or inserting, and has evolved to encompass the comprehensive construction of entire assemblies. Conventional benchmarks, which are predominantly focused on single tasks, are no longer adequate to address the complexity and interconnected nature of modern manufacturing processes. Consequently, the necessity for benchmarks to encompass a broader spectrum of assembly and subassembly steps is becoming apparent. In light of these developments, we propose LABIT, an industrial robotic assembly benchmark for long-horizon manipulation. This benchmark encompasses DIN-norm assembly tasks, including screwing, placing, inserting, and snapping, under both high and low tolerance conditions. The successive nature of these assembly steps implies that prior errors can influence subsequent manipulation. Consequently, this benchmark is particularly relevant for assessing the robustness of intelligent robotic systems. The proposed benchmarking protocols and novel performance metric, based on a taxonomy of assembly complexity in conjunction with the comprehensive list of parts and their corresponding CAD models, serve as essential tools for research and development in the field. To provide a baseline, we evaluate the benchmark using traditional force-position control and report reliability and score metrics per primitive, subtask, assembly category, and subassembly. Accompanying data is available at https://irp-hka.github.io/robotic-assembly-benchmark/.

</details>


### [119] [Estimating the Causal Impact of Social Navigation Signals in Corridor Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16411)
*Francesco Baldo,Luca Castri,Gloria Beraldo,Nicola Bellotto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究人机共享环境下的社交导航信号决策问题，指出经典基于观测的推断会因混杂因素导致错误结论，并采用因果推断框架重新评估社交信号的真实效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在人类共享环境中部署时，不仅需要决定如何导航，还需判断何时值得发出专门的社交信号（如社交导航信号）。传统的基于观测的推断容易受到混杂因素的影响，从而错误估计信号与任务结果之间的关系。为了正确评估社交信号是否真正有助于任务成功，需要一种能够消除混杂路径影响的因果推断方法。

Method: 作者在走廊场景中研究人机交互，将交互建模为一个因果DAG，节点包括机器人动作、行人占用情况、可用空间、任务结果以及一个受控混杂变量（静态障碍物）。通过该因果模型估计干预效应，并将其与仅以机器人动作为条件得到的观测估计进行比较。

Result: 结果显示，考虑混杂变量后结论发生逆转：基于观测的估计错误地表明社交信号有害，而因果干预估计则显示社交信号是有益的。这一差异说明忽略混杂因素会导致错误结论。

Conclusion: 该工作表明在社交机器人导航中，忽略混杂因素会导致错误判断，因果模型对于实现情境感知的信号决策至关重要，为社交导航中“何时发出信号”的问题提供了更可靠的推理框架。

Abstract: Deploying robots in human-shared environments requires deciding not only how to navigate, but also when dedicated social signals are worth emitting. Classic observation-based inference can be misleading in such settings: confounding factors may bias the estimated relationship between a signal and the task outcome, leading to incorrect decisions. Causal inference provides a framework for disentangling these relationships by modelling the mechanisms linking observations, actions, and outcomes, and by estimating interventional effects after accounting for confounding paths. We study a human-robot interaction scenario in a corridor and use a causal model to estimate whether a social navigation signal causally improves transit success. We represent the interaction as a causal DAG involving robot action, pedestrian occupancy, available space, task outcome, and a controlled confounder (static obstacles). We compare the interventional estimate with the observational estimate obtained by conditioning only on the robot action, which does not explicitly model interventions. Our results show that accounting for confounding variables can reverse the conclusions drawn from naive observational analysis: the observational estimate wrongly suggests that the social signal is detrimental, whereas the causal estimate shows that it is beneficial. This discrepancy highlights how failing to account for confounding can lead to incorrect conclusions and demonstrates the importance of causal models for context-aware signalling decisions in social robot navigation.

</details>


### [120] [SHOES: Sim-to-real Hardware-adaptation via On-policy Expansion of Skills for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16457)
*Kota Tsuruhara,Tsung-Yuan Chen,Kento Kawaharazuka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SHOES框架，通过冻结仿真训练的名义策略并仅微调轻量级、恒等初始化的调制模块，使四足机器人能高效适应鞋类引起的形态变化，并引入SafeBox采样器约束在线适应工作空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类通过穿鞋扩展运动能力，而运动控制器通常针对固定形态训练，当鞋子改变足部几何、质量和摩擦时性能会下降。需要一种样本高效的方法让策略适应鞋类引起的形态变化。

Method: SHOES：冻结仿真训练的名义策略，仅微调轻量级、恒等初始化的调制模块，保留初始行为同时实现高效硬件适应；引入SafeBox——一种具备工作空间感知的路径点采样器，将在线适应约束在用户定义的工作空间内。

Result: 在装备鞋子的Unitree Go2仿真中，SHOES以有限的额外适应显著恢复运动性能；相同适应预算下优于仅FiLM、仅线性适配器和残差RL基线。真实Unitree Go2穿戴人类鞋子的实验进一步验证了硬件部署的有效性。

Conclusion: SHOES是一种样本高效的运动策略适应框架，能有效应对鞋类引起的形态变化，并在仿真与真实硬件上得到验证。

Abstract: Humans expand their locomotion capability by wearing appropriate footwear, such as hiking boots for mountains or skates for ice. Footwear changes the ground-contact interface and enables traversal of otherwise difficult terrain. In contrast, locomotion controllers are typically trained for a fixed morphology, and their performance can degrade when shoes alter foot geometry, mass, and friction. We propose SHOES, a sample-efficient framework for adapting locomotion policies to footwear-induced morphology changes. SHOES freezes a simulation-trained nominal policy and fine-tunes only lightweight, identity-initialized modulation modules on the robot, preserving the initial behavior while enabling efficient hardware adaptation. We also introduce SafeBox, a workspace-aware waypoint sampler that constrains online adaptation within a user-defined workspace. The simulation results with a shoe-equipped Unitree Go2 show that SHOES substantially recovers locomotion performance after footwear-induced morphology changes using limited additional adaptation. Under the same adaptation budget, SHOES outperforms FiLM-only, Linear Adapter-only, and residual-RL baselines. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 fitted with human shoes further evaluate the effectiveness of SHOES for hardware deployment.

</details>


### [121] [DeTac: Dual-branch Decoupling of Texture and Force Signals in Vision-Based Tactile Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16463)
*Zhirui Zhang,Yunkai Tang,Jiahang Li,Fan Fei,Jinnuo Zhang,Kairan Ding,Jieji Ren,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DeTac双分支生成框架，从单一混合触觉图像中同时提取干净阴影和标记图像，实现触觉信号解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于标记的视触觉传感器将表面纹理与接触力信号耦合在单次观测中，传统数据驱动解耦依赖串行掩码修复，存在掩码尺寸困境、误差累积及缺乏真实配对监督数据的问题。

Method: DeTac采用双分支生成框架并发提取阴影与标记图像；利用全图合成规避掩码依赖，引入跨分支协同优化实现互相监督以抑制误差保留；并通过定制硬件构建严格对齐的真实配对触觉数据集提供高质量监督。

Result: 实验表明DeTac达到最先进的解耦精度，显著促进下游表面重建与力估计任务，并具备跨传感器泛化能力。

Conclusion: DeTac为视触觉信号解耦提供了无掩码依赖、可互相监督的新范式，并推动下游操作任务性能提升。

Abstract: Vision-based tactile sensors enable fine-grained perception for dexterous robotic manipulation, but current marker-based designs tightly couple surface texture and contact force signals within a single observation. Existing data-driven decoupling methods rely on serial mask-based inpainting, which suffers from mask-sizing dilemmas caused by physical indentation effects, error retention, and lack of real paired data for supervision. To address these challenges, we propose DeTac, a novel dual-branch generative framework that concurrently extracts clean shading and marker images from a single mixed tactile image. DeTac leverages full-image synthesis to naturally bypass mask dependency and introduces a cross-branch synergistic optimization that enables mutual supervision, effectively preventing error retention. Furthermore, we construct a strictly aligned, real-world paired tactile dataset using custom hardware to provide high-quality supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeTac achieves state-of-the-art decoupling accuracy, significantly facilitating downstream surface reconstruction and force estimation tasks while exhibiting cross-sensor generalization.

</details>


### [122] [Trust-Calibrated VLA Policy Post-Training with Evolving Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16517)
*Wenzhan Li,Yiran Qin,Heng Zhou,Huirui Wang,Yulan Guo,Ruimao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MERL框架，通过可信度校准耦合策略优化与在线更新的模拟器，缓解VLA模型RL后训练中可靠性与效率的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型多以模仿学习预训练，部署超出示范覆盖时需RL后训练；现有精炼范式存在可靠性—效率权衡：无模型方法交互密集，基于模型方法可能利用不可靠的想象。

Method: MERL将策略优化与在线更新的模拟器（世界模型+奖励代理头）耦合。跨阶段用模拟器可靠性控制想象数据比例与rollout时域；rollout内用chunk级信任控制回放选择与优化权重；并用残差预测器在无真实后续时估计信任。

Result: 在扰动LIBERO-PRO上平均成功率79.7%，高阈值学习速度比匹配的无模型对照提升10.9%，并在三个种子下优于同协议外部基线；50条示范/任务固定数据适配在两个真机任务上达80.0%平均成功率。

Conclusion: 可信度校准的模型演化RL能兼顾可靠性与效率，提升VLA后训练表现并泛化到真机任务。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are often pretrained via imitation learning, making reinforcement learning (RL) post-training important when deployment moves beyond demonstration coverage. Existing refinement paradigms expose a reliability--efficiency tension: model-free refinement avoids simulator bias but is interaction-intensive, whereas model-based refinement can exploit unreliable imagination even as its simulator adapts. We propose Model-Evolving Reinforcement Learning (MERL), a trust-calibrated framework that couples policy optimization with an online-updated simulator comprising a world model and a reward proxy head. Across stages, simulator reliability controls the imagined-data ratio and rollout horizon; within rollouts, chunk-level trust controls replay selection and optimization weight. For recursive imagination without exact grounded continuations, a residual predictor estimates trust without querying future ground truth. On perturbed LIBERO-PRO tasks, MERL reaches 79.7% average success, improves high-threshold learning speed by 10.9% over the matched model-free control, and outperforms same-protocol external baselines across three training seeds. Fixed-data adaptation from 50 demonstrations per task achieves 80.0% average success on two physical-robot tasks.

</details>


### [123] [Learning Reachability-Based Safety Representations for Real-Time Robot Navigation in Unknown and Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16522)
*Bojan Derajić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套基于神经隐式表示的HJ可达性学习方法，将离线求解的安全值函数用于实时优化控制，实现未知动态环境下的安全机器人导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知动态环境中的安全导航不能仅靠几何碰撞检测：机器人即使离障碍物较远，也可能因自身速度、朝向或障碍物运动而无法避碰。HJ可达性分析能刻画这一点（值函数符号区分可安全延续与必然碰撞的状态），但在线对每个新观测环境求解代价过高。

Method: 离线训练神经隐式表示来近似可达性解，供优化控制器实时使用。利用值函数不超过约束这一性质，将其分解为直接计算的约束（如符号距离）减去非负的动力学修正项，仅学习该修正项，从而在存在近似误差时仍保证障碍物位于预测安全集之外。前四种方法分别把学习函数用作MPC终端约束（静态局部环境、预测障碍运动）和CBF安全滤波中的障碍候选（静态环境、相对坐标下的成对障碍以处理数量变化的动态障碍）。第五种方法针对控制仿射动力学与多面体输入，利用HJ哈密顿量在顶点取最大的性质，通过可并行树搜索生成训练标签，结合HJ变分不等式损失，扩展至13维状态系统。

Result: 地面与空中机器人的仿真和硬件实验表明，相比几何方法与手工设计基线，该方法碰撞避免表现更好，尤其在短预测时域和移动障碍场景下。第五种方法将适用范围从低维模型扩展到最多13个状态的系统。

Conclusion: 通过仅学习非负动力学修正项，可在保证安全集保守性的同时实现实时安全的优化控制；顶点树搜索与变分不等式损失进一步将方法推广到高维控制仿射系统。

Abstract: Safe robot navigation in unknown, dynamic environments requires more than geometric collision checking: a robot still far from an obstacle may be unable to avoid it because of its speed, orientation, or the obstacle's motion. Hamilton--Jacobi (HJ) reachability analysis captures this in a value function whose sign separates states that admit a safe continuation from those where collision is unavoidable, but computing it online for every newly observed environment is too expensive. This dissertation trains neural implicit representations offline on reachability solutions to give optimization-based controllers this information in real time. Since the value function never exceeds the constraint, it equals a directly computed constraint, such as a signed distance, minus a nonnegative dynamics correction. Learning only this correction keeps represented obstacles outside the predicted safe set despite approximation errors. The first two of five methods use the learned function as a terminal constraint in model predictive control, for static local environments and predicted obstacle motion. The next two use it as a barrier candidate in control barrier function safety filters, for static environments and, through pairwise barriers in relative coordinates, for a varying number of moving obstacles. Simulations and hardware experiments on ground and aerial robots show better collision avoidance than geometric and hand-designed baselines, especially for short horizons and moving obstacles. The fifth method replaces the grid-based offline solver, which limits the first four to low-dimensional models. For control-affine dynamics with polytopic inputs, the HJ Hamiltonian is maximized at a vertex, so a parallelizable tree search over vertex inputs can generate training labels. With a loss from the HJ variational inequality, these labels yield barrier candidates for systems with up to 13 states.

</details>


### [124] [Design and Analysis of a Nonlinear Parallel Mechanism Coupling for Dynamic Humanoid Legs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.16604)
*Rohit Kumar,Heiner Peters,Felix Wiebe,Franek Stark,Janne Janzen,Dennis Mronga,Shubham Vyas,Frank Kirchner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种串联-并联腿部设计，结合膝、踝并联机构与非线性膝-踝耦合，将所有驱动器靠近髋部布置，实现低远端惯性与高效力传递。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人对轻量、敏捷、节能的腿部机构需求增加，通过近端驱动降低远端惯性成为常见目标，但兼具紧凑、保持扭矩能力与高效力传递的传动仍难实现。

Method: 设计含膝关节并联机构、踝关节并联机构及非线性膝-踝耦合的腿，驱动器集中靠近髋部；建立耦合的非线性传动模型，并通过仿真与硬件验证。

Result: 膝屈曲时被动辅助踝背屈以助抬脚，膝伸展时辅助踝跖屈以助蹬地；共享传动使两关节互相借用驱动能力，缓解膝峰值扭矩与踝峰值速度需求；6kg腿在足端有效反射质量仅0.37kg。

Conclusion: 该串联-并联腿设计可显著降低远端惯性，验证了共享传动在动态人形机器人中的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Humanoids increasingly demand lightweight leg mechanisms for agile, dynamic, and energy-efficient locomotion. Reducing distal inertia through proximal actuation has thus become a common design goal. Yet, compact transmissions that preserve torque capability and efficient force transmission remain hard to achieve. This paper presents a novel series-parallel leg design combining a knee parallel mechanism, an ankle parallel mechanism, and a nonlinear knee-ankle coupling where all actuators placed closer to the hip. As the knee flexes, the coupling passively assists ankle dorsiflexion for foot clearance and, as it extends it assists plantarflexion for push-off, redistributing ankle load through the knee transmission. We derive the coupling's nonlinear transmission models and validate them in simulation and on hardware. The design shows that the shared transmission lets both joints draw on each other's actuator capacity by easing the knee's peak torque demand under load and the ankle's peak speed demand during fast motion. %Together With the leg's proximal actuator placement, the leg achieves an effective reflected mass of 0.37kg at the foot for a 6kg leg, demonstrating very low distal leg inertia for dynamic humanoid robots.

</details>


### [125] [Force-Modulated Dexterity: Unified Compliance for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17161)
*Ying Yuan,David Held,Jitendra Malik,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FOMO-Dex统一柔顺性框架，通过力驱动运动修正提升遥操作接触丰富操作的演示质量与策略学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作需要运动与接触力的协同控制，但遥操作中操作者只能指定运动，难以在无接触力反馈的情况下复现人类细微的力调整，导致接触力过大、演示质量低。

Method: FOMO-Dex将名义动作表达在由任务几何与接触结构定义的坐标中，沿选定方向施加力驱动的运动修正，其余指令分量保持不变，形成统一的柔顺性公式。

Result: 在卡片拾取、剪纸、拧螺丝和螺母螺栓装配任务中，相比刚性控制降低了过大的接触力；用户研究显示操作者经简短练习后每小时可采集更多成功演示数据；模仿修正动作较模仿原始操作指令在卡片拾取上成功率更高；基于FOMO-Dex演示训练的策略在螺母螺栓和拧螺丝任务中获得更大旋转量。

Conclusion: FOMO-Dex通过力驱动运动修正有效改善接触丰富遥操作的力控制与数据采集效率，并提升下游策略学习表现。

Abstract: Teleoperation is widely used to collect high-quality demonstrations for robot learning. Dexterous manipulation, however, requires coordinated control of motion and contact forces. Operators can specify motion, but struggle to reproduce the subtle force adjustments of human manipulation without feeling the robot's contacts. We introduce Force-Modulated Dexterity (FOMO-Dex), a unified compliance formulation for contact-rich manipulation. FOMO-Dex expresses nominal actions in coordinates defined by task geometry and contact structure. It applies force-driven motion corrections along selected directions while leaving the remaining command components unchanged. Across card picking, paper cutting, screwdriving, and nut-and-bolt assembly, FOMO-Dex reduces excessive contact forces compared with stiff control. A user study shows that operators collect more successful demonstration data per hour after brief practice. For policy learning, imitating the corrected actions improves card-picking success over imitating raw operator commands. Policies trained on FOMO-Dex demonstrations also achieve more rotation in nut-and-bolt manipulation and screwdriving than those trained on demonstrations collected with the stiff controller. Videos are shown on https://yingyuan0414.github.io/fomo-dex/ .

</details>


### [126] [Generative Flow on Symbolic Abstractions for Embodiment Capability Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17174)
*Youwei Yu,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FOSA框架，通过生成流网络学习具身特定的任务生成器，发现可执行任务并蒸馏为语言条件策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练任务分布多为手工设计或由LLM采样，依赖语义合理性而非具身可行性，导致课程过难、冗余或忽略可学习的行为，难以实现复杂长时程机器人行为。

Method: 将抽象策略参数化为生成流网络（Generative Flow Network），构建符号化任务骨架，每个骨架编译为仿真环境，以底层策略学习性能作为抽象策略的奖励信号。

Result: 在异构机器人平台上，FOSA发现了多样且可执行、接近机器人经验能力前沿的任务；将发现的策略蒸馏为单一语言条件策略后，在未见复杂与长时程任务上性能提升。

Conclusion: 基于具身可行性驱动任务发现可有效提升机器人复杂长时程任务的学习与泛化能力，代码与演示已公开。

Abstract: Achieving complex, long-horizon robot behavior remains challenging because successful execution depends on skills grounded in a robot's physical affordances. Existing training-task distributions are often handcrafted or sampled from large language models (LLMs), relying on semantic plausibility rather than embodiment-specific feasibility. This mismatch can produce curricula that are too difficult, redundant, or blind to non-obvious but learnable behaviors. We propose Generative Flow on Symbolic Abstractions (FOSA), a framework for discovering embodiment-specific capabilities by learning a grounded task generator. FOSA parameterizes an abstract policy as a Generative Flow Network that constructs symbolic task skeletons. Each skeleton is compiled into a simulation environment, where low-level policy-learning performance provides the reward signal for the abstract policy. Across heterogeneous robot platforms, FOSA discovers diverse and executable tasks near the robot's empirical capability frontier. After distilling the discovered task policies into a single language-conditioned policy, FOSA improves performance on unseen complex and long-horizon tasks. The code and demonstration videos are available at https://www.youwei-yu.com/fosa.

</details>


### [127] [DexNinja: Learning Robust Dexterous Cutting Policy with a Real-to-Sim-to-Real Data Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17208)
*Haozhe Lou,Runyi Yang,Wanqi Zhong,Caiyun Liu,Yicheng Liu,Wenhao Liu,Wenlong Ma,Jiawei Xia,Kanzhi Wu,Danda Pani Paudel,Luc Van Gool,Hang Zhao,Yiming Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexNinja：从少量真实演示生成密集逼真训练分布的real2sim2real框架，用于灵巧食品切割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人切割食品是高度接触的交互，涉及可变形的弹塑性物体，其行为随姿态、接触模式和切割阶段变化。真实数据采集昂贵且浪费（每次试验污染或损坏食物），模拟虽是好替代但物理与感知存在巨大sim-to-real差距，对切割这类拓扑变化交互尤为严重。

Method: 提出DexNinja框架：(i)从真实捕获重建物体实例；(ii)在类别级约束下随机化物理上有意义的参数；(iii)用自定义可微分模拟器结合机器人动力学、触觉接触和可变形切割，生成增强的灵巧操作片段。

Result: 在灵巧食品切片任务上评估：(1)数十个真实演示加数百个增强模拟数据，成功率超过60%；(2)仅用真实数据时，sim-to-real增强数据持续提升成功率超过20%；(3)所得策略能泛化到形状和大小不同的分布外物体。

Conclusion: DexNinja能有效将少量真实演示转化为密集逼真训练分布，显著提升灵巧切割任务的成功率并具备泛化能力。

Abstract: Cutting a piece of food looks effortless in human hands. For a robot, however, every cut is a high-contact interaction with a deformable, elasto-plastic object whose behavior changes with pose, contact mode, and cutting stage. Collecting real-world data is expensive and wasteful: each trial typically contaminates or damages the food, so obtaining scalable real world data for dexterous cutting is expensive and difficult. Simulation is an appealing alternative, but a large sim-to-real gap exists in both physics and perception. This gap is especially severe for topology-changing interactions like cutting, where small errors in contact, friction, or material and elasticity responses quickly compound. We propose \textsc{DexNinja}, a real2sim2real framework that turns a handful of real demonstrations into a dense and realistic training distribution. \textsc{DexNinja} (i) reconstructs object instances from real captures, (ii) randomizes physically meaningful parameters under category-level constraints, and (iii) produces augmented dexterous manipulation episodes using a custom differentiable simulator that couples robot dynamics, tactile contact, and deformable cutting. We evaluate \textsc{DexNinja} on a dexterous food-slicing task and show that (1) with tens of real demonstrations and hundreds of augmented simulator data, the success rate could reach more than 60\%. (2) the augmented data consistently improves success rates when only using real data with more than 20\% via sim-to-real, and (3) the resulting policies generalize to out-of-distribution objects with varying shapes and sizes.

</details>


### [128] [LiDAR-based Humanoid Navigation in Dynamic, Non-Convex Environments via Spatio-Temporal Safe Corridors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17232)
*Shubham S. Kumbhar,Panagiotis Artemiadis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种实时人形机器人导航框架，从基于LiDAR的粒子表示直接构建时空安全走廊，并与LIP规划及全身控制耦合，实现动态非凸环境中的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在动态、非凸环境中导航，需要将机载感知与足步规划及动态运动控制紧密耦合。现有方法常通过简化的凸障碍边界丢弃复杂自由空间，且未考虑实时动态占据预测。

Method: 构建基于LiDAR粒子表示的时空安全走廊，无限制性几何假设；将其与基于线性倒立摆（LIP）的规划及全身控制（WBC）耦合，实现迭代式多面体生成以在移动非凸结构周围及内部导航。

Result: 在Digit人形机器人仿真中，于24×24 m²场地内绕行及进入移动非凸障碍物的成功率达96%。消融实验表明迭代多面体生成在狭窄走廊显著优于单次迭代基线；评估显示对杂乱、传感器噪声、控制延迟和力扰动具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了无需限制性几何假设的实时人形导航，能够处理移动非凸障碍物，且具备较好的鲁棒性与成功率。

Abstract: Navigating humanoid robots through dynamic, non-convex environments requires tightly coupling onboard perception with footstep planning and dynamic locomotion control. Existing methods frequently discard complex free space through simplified convex obstacle bounds and fail to account for real-time dynamic occupancy predictions. This paper presents a real-time framework for humanoid navigation that directly constructs spatio-temporal safe corridors from LiDAR-based particle representations and couples them with Linear Inverted Pendulum (LIP)-based planning and Whole Body Control (WBC) to enable navigation around and within moving non-convex structures without restrictive geometric assumptions. Simulations on the Digit humanoid yield a 96% success rate in navigating around and inside moving non-convex obstacles in a 24 by 24 m^2 arena. Ablation studies confirm that the proposed iterative polytope generation significantly outperforms single-iteration baselines in narrow corridors, while evaluations demonstrate robustness to clutter, sensor noise, control delays, and force perturbations.

</details>


### [129] [Value-Preserving Action Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17252)
*Max Muchen Sun,Jueun Kwon,Benjamin Riviere,Todd Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出"保值动作量化"(VAQ)：以状态可达性重构误差为准则对连续动作空间离散化，并证明值函数误差被状态可达性重构误差紧致约束，将其与MCTS结合成VAQ-MCTS用于行为克隆，在物体推动任务上匹配或超越现有方法并提升数据效率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于搜索的策略学习（如MCTS）可用运行时计算换取数据效率和对新环境的适应能力。但用于机器人学习时，需对连续动作空间及动力学进行离散化（通常是动作量化）。已有的基于重构的量化方法只保证单独表示连续动作空间的能力，而策略学习还要求量化后的动作能保持其对未来状态和价值的影响；这种需求并不能由动作重构误差体现。因此作者希望找到更合适的量化准则。

Method: 1) 从理论上分析动作量化对价值函数的影响，证明由动作量化引起的价值函数误差被"量化动作下状态可达性的重构误差"所紧致约束，说明状态可达性误差而非动作重构误差才是关键；2) 据此提出保值动作量化（VAQ），通过保持状态可达性来量化动作；3) 将VAQ与MCTS结合，构成保值、基于搜索的策略学习框架VAQ-MCTS，用于行为克隆；4) 在多种物体推动任务作为受控基准上，与最先进的行为克隆方法进行比较。

Result: VAQ-MCTS在标准设置下匹配或超越当前最先进的行为克隆方法，同时显著提升数据效率和对异构演示的鲁棒性，并能实现对新环境的零样本适应。硬件实验中，仅用10分钟演示数据即完成全部30次真实世界物体推动试验。

Conclusion: 动作量化的关键准则应是保持状态可达性，这为价值误差提供紧致上界；据此构建的VAQ-MCTS兼顾搜索的数据效率优势与策略学习对价值保持的需求，在仿真与真实机器人任务中均表现优异。

Abstract: Search-based policy learning, such as Monte Carlo tree search (MCTS), offers the flexibility to trade runtime computation for data efficiency and adaptation to novel environments. For robot learning, however, applying search requires discretizing the continuous action space and associated dynamics, typically through action quantization. While reconstruction-based quantization is sufficient for representing the continuous action space alone, policy learning additionally requires the quantized actions to preserve their effects on future states and values. We show that such requirement is not necessarily captured by action reconstruction error. Instead, we prove that the error in the value function resulting from action quantization is tightly bounded by the reconstruction error of state reachability under the quantized actions. Based on these results, we propose value-preserving action quantization (VAQ), which quantizes actions by preserving state reachability. We further combine VAQ with MCTS (VAQ-MCTS) as a value-preserving, search-based policy learning framework for behavioral cloning. Using a wide range of object pushing tasks as a controlled benchmark, we evaluate VAQ-MCTS against state-of-the-art behavioral cloning methods. VAQ-MCTS matches or exceeds their performance under standard settings, while substantially improving data efficiency and robustness to heterogeneous demonstrations, while enabling zero-shot adaptation to novel environments. Hardware experiments further demonstrate the effectiveness of VAQ-MCTS, completing all 30 real-world object pushing trials with only 10 minutes of demonstration data.

</details>


### [130] [Physical SLAM: Physics-aware Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17260)
*Guangming Wang,Zhishuo Liu,Haibing Wu,Wanli Ma,Yixiong Jing,Olaf Wysocki,Brian Sheil*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Physical SLAM，一种物理感知方法，从单目RGB-D视频联合重建动态高斯辐射场并推断物理动力学，实现变形物体的4D表示与动作条件预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从移动相机构建物理世界的4D预测性表示需要同时重建动态高斯辐射场并推断其物理动力学，现有方法难以将视觉观测与物理模拟有效融合。

Method: 将场景分解为静态背景高斯场（用于相机定位）和动态物体高斯场（由弹簧-质量物理模型驱动，含弹性形变、惯性和阻尼）；引入虚拟观测力将模拟表面节点逐渐拉向观测深度点；基于全重建窗口聚合的模拟误差更新物理参数；设计物理感知高斯变换与表面对齐策略，使动态高斯沿形变物理表面变换并在渲染优化中保持对齐。

Result: 在PhysTwin基准上，重建和预测PSNR分别比PhysTwin提升10.4%和11.0%；在TUM RGB-D基准上，相比4DGS-SLAM相机跟踪平均误差降低20.6%，SSIM提升3.6%，LPIPS降低8.3%。

Conclusion: 所得表示支持观测帧渲染和动作条件模拟，将SLAM地图扩展为描述变形物体如何响应操作，验证了物理感知4D高斯场在重建与预测上的有效性。

Abstract: To reconstruct a predictive 4D representation of the physical world from monocular video captured by a moving camera, we jointly reconstruct dynamic Gaussian radiance fields and infer their underlying physical dynamics. This paper proposes \emph{Physical SLAM}, a physics-aware method for constructing 4D Gaussian fields of deformable objects and predicting their future state when subjected to external actions, given a sequence of RGB-D images. Our method separates the scene into a static background Gaussian field for camera localization and a dynamic object Gaussian field driven by a spring--mass physical model of elastic deformation, inertia, and damping. To integrate visual observations with physical dynamics, we introduce virtual observation forces that gradually pull simulated surface nodes toward observed depth points, avoiding abrupt replacement of simulated node positions. Meanwhile, the physical parameters of the spring--mass model are updated from simulation errors aggregated over the full reconstruction observation window, enabling the reconstructed dynamics to align with observations. To couple appearance, geometry, and physics, we further design a physics-aware Gaussian transformation and surface alignment strategy, where dynamic Gaussians are transformed along the deforming physical surface of the spring--mass model and constrained to remain aligned with the surface during rendering-based optimization. The resulting representation supports observed-frame rendering and action-conditioned simulation, extending SLAM maps to describe how deformable objects respond to manipulation. On the PhysTwin benchmark, our method improves reconstruction and prediction PSNR over PhysTwin by 10.4\% and 11.0\%, respectively. On the TUM RGB-D benchmark, it reduces average camera tracking error by 20.6\%, improves SSIM by 3.6\%, and reduces LPIPS by 8.3\% compared with 4DGS-SLAM.

</details>


### [131] [ReForesight: Occlusion-Robust Re-Identification and Future Occupancy Forecasting for Dynamic Semantic Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17279)
*Didar Seyidov,Faith Ogunwoye,Hajira Amjad,Danil Tolkachev,Yuri Sarkisov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReForesight 是一个开源 ROS 2 工具包，在基于粒子的语义建图流程上增加实例级未来占据预测与遮挡后重识别，用小型 MLP 预测行人/车辆未来 3 秒位置并生成“幽灵”点云。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人共享人行道时需要知道人会到哪里，但语义占据地图只显示当前占据状态，缺乏对未来的预测能力。

Method: 采用 36.6k 参数 MLP 对每个跟踪目标预测未来至多 3 秒位姿，辅以卡尔曼回退与静止门控；将目标自身点云移动到预测位姿，在地图坐标系中形成“幽灵”点云，无需占据解码器；可选跟踪器利用已有 keypoint 描述子和度量 3D 门控在遮挡后重新关联目标。

Result: 在四个留出的 CODa 序列上，MLP 将 3 秒最终位移误差均值从 0.728 m（卡尔曼）降至 0.662 m，并在每个序列上优于同规模 GRU（0.692 m），单 CPU 核每实例耗时 0.04 ms。已发布代码、全部模型和无 ROS 演示；现场演示使用户外清洁机器人。

Conclusion: 该方法以极低计算成本实现了实例级未来占据预测和遮挡重识别，可作为开源 ROS 2 组件集成到语义建图流程中。

Abstract: Robots sharing walkways need to know where people will be, yet semantic occupancy maps show only where they are. We present ReForesight, an open-source ROS 2 toolkit that adds instance-level future occupancy and re-identification after occlusion to a particle-based semantic mapping pipeline. A 36.6k-parameter MLP predicts each tracked person or vehicle up to 3 s ahead, guarded by a Kalman fallback and a stationary gate, and the object's own points are moved to the forecast pose, forming a "ghost" point cloud in the map frame without an occupancy decoder. An optional tracker re-associates objects after occlusion using keypoint descriptors the pipeline already computes and a metric 3D gate. On four held-out CODa sequences, the MLP lowers the mean 3 s final displacement error from 0.728 m (Kalman) to 0.662 m and beats a similar-size GRU (0.692 m) on every sequence, at 0.04 ms per instance on one CPU core. Code, all models, and a ROS-free demo are released; field demonstrations use an outdoor cleaning robot.

</details>


### [132] [Phase-Conditioned Affordance Fields for SE(3)-Equivariant Visuomotor Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17281)
*Xinzhe Chen,Liqi Huang,Sihua Ren,Haowen Sun,Mingyang Li,Zeyang Liu,Xingyu Chen,Xuguang Lan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出相位条件可供性场（PAF），通过在观测点云上学习逐点分数，为SE(3)等变视觉运动策略引入局部几何归纳偏置，在保留全局等变性的同时提升操作任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SE(3)等变视觉运动策略基于“整个观测服从单一刚体变换”的全局等变假设，该假设在真实操作任务中不成立，因为任务相关区域会随不同物体而变化。因此需要一种能适应任务相关区域变化的局部几何归纳偏置方法。

Method: 在观测点云上学习相位条件可供性场（PAF），即逐点分数。该分数以两种互补方式被SE(3)等变策略使用：一是用分数加权质心作为学习到的、相位条件的锚点，将观测和动作围绕活动区域规范化，替代固定的夹爪坐标系参考；二是将分数作为逐点特征附加到输入中。这样引入了局部几何归纳偏置，同时保留策略的全局SE(3)等变性。

Result: 在多个MimicGen任务上，在不同空间随机化条件下验证，PAF一致地提升底层策略的成功率。在多物体真实机器人任务中，PAF优于基线，并在光照变化下保持领先。

Conclusion: PAF通过相位条件可供性场为SE(3)等变视觉运动策略引入局部几何归纳偏置，克服了全局等变假设的局限，在仿真和真实机器人任务中均有效提升性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: SE(3)-equivariance provides a useful geometric inductive bias for visuomotor policies, encoding that actions covary with rigid transformations of the observation. However, existing SE(3)-equivariant visuomotor policies build this bias on a global equivariance assumption that treats the entire observation as governed by a single rigid transformation, which breaks down in real-world manipulation tasks where the task-relevant region changes across different objects. We address this by learning a phase-conditioned affordance field (PAF) over the observed point cloud, a per-point score that the SE(3)-equivariant policy uses in two complementary ways. Its score-weighted centroid serves as a learned, phase-conditioned anchor that canonicalizes the observation and the action around the active region, replacing the fixed gripper-frame reference, and the score itself is appended as a per-point feature. This introduces a local geometric inductive bias, while preserving the policy's global SE(3) equivariance. We validate on multiple MimicGen tasks under varying spatial randomization, and PAF consistently improves the success rate of the underlying policy. On multi-object real-robot tasks, PAF outperforms baselines and keeps its lead under lighting variations.

</details>


### [133] [Improving Policy Learning from Delayed Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17298)
*Xiru Zhu,David Meger,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究人类监督机器人模仿学习中的专家干预延迟问题，提出LfDI方法，通过学习代价函数推断额外监督信号，优于未考虑延迟的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往模仿学习研究多关注次优专家（专家策略会犯错），但都假设专家干预在错误发生后立即发生。本文探讨专家干预存在延迟的情形：延迟干预虽可视为一种噪声可能提升策略鲁棒性，但实验表明延迟干预整体上不如非延迟干预有效，尤其是在演示"避免错误"行为方面效果较差。

Method: 提出 Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI)：基于专家在何处、何时进行干预来学习一个代价函数，并结合专家提供的关于干预延迟程度的有限信息；利用该代价函数为缺乏专家数据的状态推断额外监督信号，引导策略避开可能触发干预的状态。

Result: 在仿真环境中评估LfDI，结果显示其性能优于其他未考虑干预延迟的方法。

Conclusion: 专家干预延迟会削弱模仿学习效果，尤其是错误避免行为的演示；显式建模干预的时空信息与延迟程度并据此生成额外监督，可有效缓解该问题。

Abstract: In this work, we explore the idea of expert intervention delays in human-supervised robotic systems for imitation learning system. Previous works in imitation learning have primarily examined the impact of suboptimal experts, where the expert policy makes mistakes, but assumed expert interventions occur immediately after errors are made. Although delayed expert intervention can be viewed as a form of noise that might improve policy robustness, our results show that learning from delayed expert interventions is overall less effective than learning from non-delayed expert interventions; delayed expert interventions are less effective at demonstrating error avoidance behaviors.
  To address this, we propose Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI), which learns a cost function based on where and when the expert intervenes, combined with limited information about the extent of intervention delays provided by the expert. This cost function is then used to infer additional supervision for states without expert data, guiding the policy to avoid states that would likely trigger interventions. We evaluate our method, Learning from Delayed Intervention (LfDI), on simulated environments and show that it outperforms other methods that do not account for intervention delays.

</details>


### [134] [Evaluating Long-Horizon Robot Planning under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17326)
*Harsh Muriki,Gayathri Aravindan,Yuchen Zheng,Sonia Chernova*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个包含七项长时程家务任务的Habitat基准，考察不同初始记忆与环境配置下家庭机器人的导航与规划能力。通过统一执行框架评估ReAct、VLM-TAMP与Tru-POMDP三种规划的变体，共90种任务-环境-记忆组合、1350个回合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人需在不完美的内部模型与外部世界中成功导航，但现有工作缺乏在不同初始记忆与环境配置下对长时程任务规划器的系统比较。

Method: 构建七项长时程家务任务的Habitat基准，变化初始机器人记忆与环境配置；在共享执行框架中适配并评估ReAct、VLM-TAMP和Tru-POMDP，覆盖90种任务-环境-记忆组合共1350个回合；测量任务成功、部分完成与动作效率，并基于规则分析执行轨迹。

Result: ReAct取得最高总体任务成功率（45.3%）与完成度（71.7%）；准确的初始记忆并不总是带来最佳性能；轨迹分析发现反复出现的放置错误、前置条件错误与冗余动作。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了不同规划器在使用初始知识与应对不确定性方面的差异，为家庭机器人长时程规划的鲁棒性评估与改进提供依据。

Abstract: Household robots must successfully navigate im-perfect circumstances, both in their internal models and the external world. We introduce a benchmark of seven long-horizon household tasks in Habitat that vary initial robot memory and environment configurations. We evaluate adaptations of ReAct, VLM-TAMP, and Tru-POMDP within a shared execution framework across 90 tasks-environment-memory combinations, totaling 1,350 episodes. Our evaluation measures task success, partial completion, and action efficiency, along with rule-based analysis of execution traces. ReAct achieves the highest overall task success (45.3%) and completion (71.7%), while having accurate initial memory does not consistently yield the highest performance for every planner. We identify recurring placement errors, precondition errors, and redundant actions through trace analysis. These findings highlight differences in how the evaluated planners use initial knowledge and respond to uncertainty.

</details>


### [135] [Can a Frontier VLM Serve as a Robot Policy for Memory-Dependent Manipulation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17350)
*Jack B. Jedlicki,Amish Sethi,Heng Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将冻结的前沿视觉语言模型GPT-6-Astra直接用作机器人策略，在RoboMME基准上取得92.1%成功率，超过人类90.5%的分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索单一通用模型能在多大程度上接管具身系统中的长程规划、记忆和末端执行器控制，模糊这些环节间的传统分工。

Method: 直接使用冻结的GPT-6-Astra作为策略，将整个回合保存在上下文中，输出每个末端执行器航点和夹爪命令，不进行任何RoboMME特定训练。

Result: 在800个测试回合中成功率达92.1%，高于人类90.5%的分数、领先最佳排行榜成绩60.8%和分层系统79.1%；在16个任务中13个达到或超过人类水平，63次失败集中于两个任务。

Conclusion: 结果模糊了长程规划、记忆与末端执行器控制之间的通常划分，提出单一通用模型在具身系统中可承担多少任务的问题。

Abstract: We use a frozen frontier vision-language model, GPT-6-Astra, directly as the robot policy on RoboMME, a benchmark of 16 memory-dependent manipulation tasks spanning temporal, spatial, object, and procedural memory. The model keeps the whole episode in context and outputs every end-effector waypoint and gripper command, with no RoboMME-specific training. On the 800 test episodes it succeeds in 92.1%, above the previously reported human score of 90.5%, which was obtained with an oracle skill interface, and far ahead of the best leaderboard entry (60.8%) and of a hierarchical system that pairs the same model with a RoboMME-trained policy (79.1%). It matches or exceeds the human score on 13 of the 16 tasks. Its 63 failures concentrate in two tasks and are mostly premature completions and mistimed or unintended button presses. These results blur the usual division between long-horizon planning, memory and end-effector control, and raise the question of how much of this stack a single general-purpose model can take over in embodied systems. Project website: https://embodied-astra.github.io. Code, prompts and all episode logs: https://github.com/jackbjed/embodied-astra-robomme.

</details>


### [136] [ClearNav: Perception-Aligned Quadrotor Flight Using Differential-Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17419)
*Aditya Azad,Arthur Huang,Guanrui Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ClearNav通过在仿真训练中引入前视朝向目标、侧向速度惩罚与近障碍减速三项奖励，将无人机运动约束在相机可视方向，显著降低杂乱环境中的碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主四旋翼在杂乱环境中飞行是巡检、搜索等任务的关键能力。仿真训练后迁移到硬件的导航策略虽能在开阔空间高速飞行，但当障碍间距缩小时仍会碰撞。作者将多数碰撞归因于一个核心机制：运动方向超出机载相机视野，例如朝向与行进方向不一致、避障时侧向偏移，都会把飞行器带向视野外的障碍物。

Method: 提出ClearNav导航策略，训练时采用两项奖励约束运动方向处于观测方向：前视朝向目标和对侧向速度的惩罚；第三项奖励在接近障碍物时惩罚速度，使进入视野的障碍物仍可规避。

Result: 在18个未见场景、每个策略2304次配对飞行中，ClearNav相比最先进方法成功率提升6.8个百分点，碰撞率降低65%，最高飞行速度5m/s。在匹配指令速度下，仅使用近障碍惩罚项也能保持70%的碰撞减少。该效果在静态场景中显著，在移动障碍物上方向一致但在整体测试上不显著。并在真实杂乱环境中验证了策略可安全成功穿越。

Conclusion: 将运动约束在相机可视方向是降低碰撞的关键机制；即使在小型无人机的有限传感器与算力下，也能实现更安全的杂乱环境飞行，并具备真实世界可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous quadrotor flight in cluttered environment is an key capability for tasks like inspection and search. Navigation policies trained in simulation and transferred to hardware can fly at high speed in open space, but they can still collide as obstacle clearance shrinks. We attribute most of these collisions to a single mechanism, namely motion in directions the onboard camera does not observe. Heading that is not aligned with the direction of travel and lateral excursion during obstacle avoidance both carry the vehicle toward obstacles outside its field of view. We introduce ClearNav, a navigation policy trained with two reward terms that constrain motion to the observed direction, a forward-looking heading target and a penalty on lateral velocity. A third term penalizes speed near obstacles so that an obstacle entering the field of view remains avoidable. In simulation, across 18 held-out scenarios with 2304 paired flights per policy unseen in training, ClearNav improves success by 6.8 points, cuts the collision rate by 65% relative to the state of the arts, and flies at maximum 5m/s. For the proximity term alone under matched commanded speeds, the collision reduction still holds at 70%. The effect is significant on static scenes and consistent in direction on moving obstacles, though not significant there at the suite level, showing that safer flight in clutter is possible even on the limited sensors and compute of small drones. We also validate our navigation policy in a real-world cluttered environment, showing that it enables the quadrotor to navigate through the cluttered environment successfully and safely.

</details>


### [137] [MIRROR: Reflecting Hidden Symmetries from Offline Robot Interaction Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17422)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihang Yao,Peide Huang,Mengdi Xu,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MIRROR框架，从离线交互数据中学习已知对称群在观测与动作上的联合变换，提升机器人策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素观测下，简单物理对称性也会产生复杂且依赖视角的图像变换，难以手工指定；现有对称感知方法依赖预定义变换表示，需针对传感配置和任务定制预处理。

Method: MIRROR从POMDP的离线交互数据中学习对称群对观测和动作的联合作用，通过一步转移元组的对抗式元组匹配耦合这些变换，并用显式群一致性正则化惩罚违反群运算公理的行为。

Result: 在四个Robosuite桌面操作任务上，MIRROR在四项对称质量指标中的三项取得最低总体误差，兼容性误差较LaLiGAN基线降低61%；结合帧平均BC将平均成功率从80.48%提升至85.12%，单任务增益最高9.66个百分点；并在高度图和分割掩码观测下重复实验，零样本部署于真实Kinova GEN3机械臂。

Conclusion: MIRROR能自动学习观测-动作联合对称变换，提升策略性能，并具备跨观测类型和真实机器人部署的泛化能力。

Abstract: Symmetry-aware robot policy learning requires coupled observation-action transformations. For pixel observations, even simple physical symmetries can induce complex, viewpoint-dependent image transformations that are difficult to specify by hand. Existing symmetry-aware methods often rely on representations with predefined transformations, requiring preprocessing tailored to the sensing setup and task. We introduce MIRROR, a framework that learns how a known symmetry group operates jointly on observations and actions in partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) from offline interaction data. MIRROR couples these transformations through adversarial tuple matching over one-step transition tuples, while explicit group-consistency regularization penalizes violations of the group-operation axioms. On four Robosuite tabletop manipulation tasks, MIRROR achieves the lowest aggregate error on three of four symmetry-quality metrics, including a 61% reduction in compatibility error (which measures consistency under transformation composition) relative to an adapted LaLiGAN baseline. Using the learned transformations in frame-averaged behavior cloning (BC) yields higher mean success rates than vanilla BC on all four tasks, increasing average success from 80.48% to 85.12%, with per-task gains up to 9.66 percentage points. We also repeat symmetry discovery and policy training in simulation with heightmap and segmentation-mask observations, then deploy the resulting policy zero-shot on a real Kinova GEN3 arm. Project page: https://pomdp-mirror.github.io.

</details>


### [138] [Smoothness Ranks Skill, Not Success: An Audit of Published Trajectory-Smoothness Curation Metrics Under Episode-Length Controls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17427)
*Jaiveer Bassi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文检验了rinse提出的轨迹平滑度指标（SAL与TED）在真实robomimic数据上能否经受回合长度控制，发现这些指标的效果主要被回合长度混淆，TED甚至与任务成功负相关，且指标优劣在不同策略配置下不可迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轨迹平滑度被视为一种廉价、免训练的信号，用于筛选模仿学习演示数据。然而，现有指标（如SAL和TED）是否真正捕捉了操作质量，还是仅仅被回合长度等混杂因素驱动，尚缺乏严格检验。作者希望验证这些已发表指标在真实数据上的稳健性。

Method: 在真实的robomimic数据上进行受控实验：对比SAL、TED与基线（回合长度、平均jerk）在can/mh、square/mh、can/mg任务上的AUROC；在时长匹配的区间内重新评估各指标；并用每个指标筛选的子集训练GMM行为克隆策略（10个随机种子）和扩散策略，比较下游成功率。

Result: 在can/mh上SAL的AUROC为0.952，但回合长度单独达到0.957；在时长匹配区间内SAL降至0.628，而jerk升至0.896，该混淆在square/mh上复现。在每回合固定150步的can/mg上，SAL接近随机（0.454），TED与成功反相关（0.376），jerk达0.706。用TED筛选数据训练GMM策略会损失约三分之一成功率（0.240 vs 0.360，p=0.0001，Bonferroni校正后仍显著），SAL与jerk与不筛选无差别。扩散策略下TED仍最差（但5种子下不显著），jerk显著优于两个基线，说明筛选决策不可跨策略配置迁移。

Conclusion: 所检验的轨迹平滑度指标无法稳健反映演示质量，其表现主要由回合长度混淆驱动；TED在接触丰富任务中与成功反相关，因为成功操作需要持续的高偏差接触运动，而失败表现为低幅度悬停。这些指标的有效性依赖于具体策略配置，不能直接迁移。

Abstract: Trajectory smoothness has been proposed as a cheap, training-free signal for curating imitation-learning demonstrations, most recently by rinse, which introduces Spectral Arc Length (SAL) and a contact-aware Trajectory-Envelope Distance (TED). We test whether these published metrics survive episode-length controls on real robomimic data. On can/mh, SAL separates better from worse operators with AUROC 0.952, but episode length alone achieves 0.957, and inside a duration-matched band SAL falls to 0.628 while raw mean jerk, the baseline rinse argues against, rises to 0.896. The confound replicates on square/mh. On can/mg, where every episode is exactly 150 steps, SAL is at chance (0.454) and TED is inverted (0.376) against task success, while jerk reaches 0.706. Training GMM behavior-cloning policies on each metric's curated subset (10 seeds), curating by TED costs roughly a third of downstream success relative to random selection of the same size (0.240 vs. 0.360, p=0.0001, surviving Bonferroni correction), while SAL and jerk are indistinguishable from not curating. Under a diffusion policy TED still ranks worst, though its deficit is not resolvable at five seeds, and jerk becomes significantly better than both baselines, so the curation decision does not transfer unchanged across the two policy configurations we tested. Inspecting trajectories shows why TED inverts: successful manipulation requires sustained high-deviation motion through contact, while failure is low-amplitude hovering that a smoothed envelope follows closely.

</details>


### [139] [Act4R: Action-conditioned 4D reconstruction for kinematics-free control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17462)
*Zhaoliang Zhang,Chenhao Yu,Zhimin Shao,Xijun Liu,Cheng Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Act4R 提出一个基于3D基础模型的框架，用动作信号预测机器人3D场景流，并支持逆控制，在预测与逆控制任务上超越此前SoTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确控制不同具身机器人的运动是长期难题。对复杂机器人结构建模需要大量时间、专用传感器和人工标注。4D表征学习提供了新思路：能否利用从2D观测中提取的4D特征，依据动作信号稠密地映射机器人的空间运动。

Method: Act4R 构建于3D基础模型之上，预测给定动作下的机器人3D场景流。其核心是Spatial-Action Encoder，将当前2D机器人状态与动作信号编码为表征未来3D状态的嵌入；随后将当前与未来空间嵌入解码为点图以估计动作条件下的场景流。为有效学习动作到未来状态的映射，引入transformer编码器中的空间-动作融合机制，以及引导编码器进行未来流预测的空间对齐损失。训练后可通过优化信号实现期望场景流的逆控制。

Result: 在公开真实世界数据集与仿真器合成数据上评估，多种机器人平台实验显示Act4R在场景流预测上优于此前SoTA；逆控制在动作精度指标与仿真闭环控制上同样表现更优。

Conclusion: Act4R 表明借助3D基础模型与4D表征，可在少量样本训练下实现动作条件化的3D场景流预测与逆控制，为跨具身机器人运动控制提供有效方案。

Abstract: Precise control of movement across various robotic embodiments is a longstanding challenge. Modeling complex robotic structures requires significant time, dedicated sensors, and manual labeling. Recent advancements in 4D representation learning offer an interesting alternative: can we leverage 4D features lifted from 2D observations to densely map a robot's spatial movement based on the action signal? To this end, we propose Act4R, a framework that builds on 3D foundation model to predict a robot's 3D scene flow with any given action. Act4R introduces a Spatial-Action Encoder, which encodes the current 2D robot state and its action signals into embeddings that represent future robot state in 3D. Then, both the current and future spatial embeddings are decoded into point maps to estimate action-conditioned scene flow. To effectively learn the mapping between action and future state, Act4R introduces spatial-action fusion in its transformer-based encoder, and a spatial alignment loss that guides the encoder for future flow prediction. After training on some samples of a given embodiment, Act4R can perform inverse control by optimizing the signal to achieve the desired scene flow. We evaluate Act4R on a public real-world dataset and synthetic data collected in simulator. Experiments on various robotic platforms show that Act4R outperforms the previous SoTA in scene flow prediction. Our method also performs better in inverse control, both on the action accuracy metric and in closed-loop control in simulation.

</details>


### [140] [From Orbit Matching to Docking: A Hierarchical Reinforcement-Learning Approach to Multiphase Rendezvous Trajectory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17468)
*Mariko Storey-Matsutani,Anatoly Zlotnik,Lee Holguin,Zigfried Hampel-Arias,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个分层强化学习框架，用于自主三自由度交会与接近操作（RPO），覆盖从轨道匹配到对接的完整轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间任务复杂度提升，RPO跨越多个动力学、目标与约束不同的任务阶段。现有强化学习方法通常只处理部分序列，或依赖人工设定的控制器切换条件，难以覆盖完整交会流程。

Method: 训练五个专用策略，分别对应轨道匹配、远距离接近、中距离接近、近距离接近和对接；再学习一个高层管理器策略，根据航天器状态在各阶段控制器间进行选择。

Result: 在完整端到端交会序列上对该分层方法进行评估，并与固定顺序调用启发式方法进行比较，以考察轨迹性能和阶段切换行为；同时分析各阶段策略及完整分层系统的训练与推理指标。

Conclusion: 分层强化学习能够在无需人工定义切换条件的情况下，实现覆盖完整RPO序列的自主制导，为多阶段空间任务的控制器组织提供可行框架。

Abstract: As the complexity of space missions increases, autonomous spacecraft guidance must accommodate transitions between mission phases with distinct dynamics, objectives, and constraints. Rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) are particularly challenging because they span multiple operating regimes, from orbital-scale matching and far-range approach to close-range maneuvering and terminal docking. Existing reinforcement-learning approaches generally address only a subset of this sequence or rely on manually defined switching conditions between controllers. This study presents a hierarchical reinforcement-learning framework for autonomous, three-degree-of-freedom RPO that spans the complete trajectory from orbit matching to docking. Five specialized policies are trained for orbit matching, far-range approach, mid-range approach, close-range approach, and docking. A learned high-level manager policy then selects among these phase-specific controllers based on the evolving spacecraft state. The hierarchical approach is evaluated over the full end-to-end rendezvous sequence and compared with a fixed sequential-invocation heuristic to assess trajectory performance and transition behavior. Training and inference metrics are also analyzed for the individual phase policies and the complete hierarchical system.

</details>


### [141] [An Optimal Algorithm for Changing from Longitudinal to Latitudinal Formation of Autonomous Aircraft Squadrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17511)
*Paulo André Sperandio Giacomin,Elder Moreira Hemerly*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将自主飞行器编队从纵向队形转换为横向队形的算法，基于预定义三维基本机动动作动态生成机动过程，具有最优时间复杂度、无碰撞且经正确性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主飞行器编队在执行着陆、起飞、避障和地理定位等任务时，需要同时具备纵向与横向两种队形，因此需要在两种队形之间进行转换。

Method: 利用一组预定义的三维基本机动动作，动态定义队形转换过程中的各类机动，并设计从纵向到横向编队的转换算法；同时开展时间复杂度分析并给出正确性证明。

Result: 时间复杂度分析表明该转换算法效率达到最优；仿真实验显示队形转换过程中无碰撞发生；正确性证明保证了转换后横向队形的特性。

Conclusion: 该算法可高效、安全地实现自主飞行器编队由纵向到横向的转换，满足多种任务对两种队形的需求。

Abstract: This work presents an algorithm for changing from longitudinal to latitudinal formation of autonomous aircraft squadrons. The maneuvers are defined dynamically by using a predefined set of 3D basic maneuvers. This formation change is necessary when the squadron has to perform tasks demanding both formations, such as landing, lift-off, obstacle avoidance and georeferencing. The time complexity analysis of the transformation algorithm reveals that its efficiency is optimal. Simulations show that the formation change is done without collision, and the proof of correctness ensures its latitudinal formation features.

</details>


### [142] [YUBI-Real2Sim: 3DGS Scene Verification for Reliable Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17521)
*Hiroki Nishizawa,Takehiko Ohkawa,Nicola Scianca,Tatsuya Matsushima,Shigeo Morishima,Kei Ota*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出YUBI-Real2Sim框架，用3DGS实现真实到仿真重建并在双鱼眼腕部视角下评估VLA策略，发现重建质量对策略评估可靠性的影响随任务而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人上评估VLA策略成本高、耗时且难以复现，real-to-sim仿真成为可扩展评估的关键。3DGS虽能快速生成场景，但面向策略评估所需的重建质量尚未被量化，尤其腕部鱼眼相机策略在广角视角下易受孔洞、漂浮物等视觉伪影影响，直接降低任务表现。

Method: 构建统一框架YUBI-Real2Sim：整合200度鱼眼相机采集、3DGS训练、3D基础模型物体建模与MuJoCo物理仿真，构建覆盖两个双臂机器人平台、三个场景、三个任务及双pi0.5策略的基准，系统测量real-to-sim排名一致性，并通过反事实3DGS伪影（天花板孔洞、漂浮物、扭曲）与采集扰动评估策略敏感性。

Result: 在九组场景-任务对比中，复现了八组真实世界策略排序。结果显示策略表现具有任务依赖性，基于新视角质量分数的可靠性预测存在局限，表明使用Real2Sim进行策略评估时需评估重建质量，但尚不能建立通用的场景接受准则。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了3DGS重建质量与real-to-sim策略评估可靠性之间的关系，指出质量分数无法普遍预测策略排序可靠性，仍需进一步研究以形成通用的场景接受规则。

Abstract: Evaluating VLA policies on real robots is costly, time-consuming, and difficult to reproduce, making real-to-sim simulation essential for scalable evaluation. While 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) enables rapid real-to-sim scene generation, the reconstruction quality required for reliable policy evaluation remains unquantified. In particular, policies relying on wrist-mounted fisheye cameras (e.g., YUBI handheld gripper data) are heavily exposed to visual artifacts (holes, floaters) in wide-angle views, directly degrading task performance. To address this, we present YUBI-Real2Sim, a unified framework for 3DGS-based scene reconstruction and real-to-sim policy evaluation under dual fisheye wrist views. We develop an integrated pipeline combining 200-degree fisheye camera capture, 3DGS training, 3D foundation models for object modeling, and MuJoCo-based physical simulation, reproducing the observed real-world policy ordering in eight of nine scene-task comparisons. Using this framework, we construct a benchmark across two bimanual robot platforms, three scenes, three tasks, and dual pi0.5-based policies. This systematically measures real-to-sim rank agreement and evaluates policy sensitivity against counterfactual 3DGS artifacts (ceiling holes, floaters, warping) and capture disturbances. We further analyze whether novel-view quality scores can indicate policy-ranking reliability under a scene-held-out evaluation protocol. The results show task-dependent performance and limitations of quality-based reliability prediction, highlighting the need to assess reconstruction quality when using Real2Sim for policy evaluation. These results do not yet establish a generally reliable scene-acceptance rule. Project Page: https://yubi-real2sim.airoa.io.

</details>


### [143] [Box-WAM: Box Trajectories as Compact State Representations for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17526)
*Qinxi Yu,Wenzhou Ding,Gloria X. Wang,Albert J. Zhai,Shenlong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BoxWAM，用任务相关物体的有向包围盒轨迹替代稠密未来帧预测，作为世界动作模型的轻量级紧凑状态表示，在降低计算成本的同时提升动作预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAMs）通过显式预测动作和未来世界状态，在机器人操作任务上优于仅动作模型。但用全帧视频预测表示未来在训练和推理阶段计算代价高昂，因此核心问题是：什么样的紧凑状态表示能保留未来预测带来的规划与监督收益。

Method: 提出BoxWAM，将稠密未来帧替换为任务相关物体的有向包围盒（oriented bounding box）轨迹。通过显式"想象"以物体为中心的未来运动，提供一个轻量级的预测目标，捕捉机器人动作引起的关键物理变化。

Result: 包围盒轨迹预测大幅降低计算成本，同时提升动作预测性能，表明以物体为中心的紧凑状态可作为视频式世界动作建模的高效替代方案。结果视频见 https://box-wam.github.io。

Conclusion: 以物体为中心的紧凑状态表示（有向包围盒轨迹）能有效替代基于视频的世界动作建模，兼顾效率与性能，适用于机器人操作任务。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are a category of policy models that ex- plicitly predict both actions and future world states. They have been shown to outperform action-only models across various robotic manipulation tasks. How- ever, representing the future with full-frame video prediction can be computation- ally expensive during both training and inference. This raises a central question: what compact state representation preserves the planning and supervision bene- fits of future prediction? In this paper, we propose BoxWAM, which replaces dense future frames with oriented bounding box trajectories of task-relevant ob- jects. By explicitly imagining object-centric future motion, our method provides a lightweight prediction target that captures the key physical changes induced by robot actions.
