We show that box trajectory prediction substantially reduces com- putational cost while improving action prediction performance, suggesting that object-centric compact states can serve as an efficient alternative to video-based world action modeling for robotic manipulation. Result videos can be found on https://box-wam.github.io

</details>


### [144] [Sim4RAD: A MATLAB Toolkit for Design-Phase Sizing, Actuator Selection and URDF-Model Verification of Serial Robotic Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17534)
*Jingchen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Sim4RAD 是一个 MATLAB 工具包，用于机械臂执行器选型早期阶段的规格计算与后续验证，支持从参数化刚体模型或 URDF 模型进行静力学、动力学、随机运动与工作空间灵巧度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 执行器选型（关节力矩与速度规格）是早期且影响深远的决策，它决定了电机、减速器和制动器的选择，甚至早于 CAD 模型的存在，因此需要在这一阶段提供可复现、面向运动类别的规格分析工具。

Method: Sim4RAD 通过少量参数构建刚体模型，并提供四类分析：结合力臂采样与姿态搜索的静力学分析、在四种约束（含执行器力矩-速度包络）下重塑轨迹的动力学流程、面向大量随机运动的随机分析，以及区分可达性与可达方式的 workspace 与灵巧度分析；将合成模型替换为 URDF 文件后，相同分析可用于验证。文中以一个 7-DOF 拟人臂在三种布局下（肩部轴序不同、末端腕关节是否与负载同轴）进行对比。

Result: 半臂长处的静力学需求在三种布局中几乎相同，且由关节而非采样运动决定规格；重新排列肩部轴序使最坏力矩保持在 55 N·m 不变，可达体积差异在 8% 以内；末端腕关节与负载同轴可消除其重力力矩，使相邻腕关节降低近三分之一，并在 5 m/s 扫描下将最后关节需求降低十倍，但灵巧度代价明显（均值从 0.570 降至 0.348）。同一机械臂在 5 m/s 目标下所需力矩是 2 s 运动的 20 至 37 倍。

Conclusion: 执行器规格应首先固定应用场景而非单条轨迹；验证应使用详细设计的 URDF 模型；工具包已开源。

Abstract: Actuator sizing is an early and consequential decision: joint torque and velocity specifications fix the motors, gearboxes and brakes before any CAD model exists. Sim4RAD is a MATLAB toolkit for that phase and for the verification that follows it. It builds a rigid-body model from a handful of parameters, and four analyses supply the specification: a static analysis combining lever-arm sampling with a posture search, giving a rated torque at a chosen payload envelope and a reproducible peak torque; a dynamics pipeline that reshapes a trajectory under one of four constraints, including the actuator torque-speed envelope; a stochastic analysis over many random motions, so a specification rests on a class of motions, not one trajectory; and a workspace and dexterity analysis that separates where the arm can reach from how it can reach there. Replacing the synthesized model by a URDF file turns the same analyses to verification. A 7-DOF anthropomorphic arm is compared in three layouts differing in the shoulder axis order and in whether the last wrist joint is coaxial with the payload. The static demand at half arm length is nearly the same in all three, and it sizes the joints rather than the sampled motion; reordering the shoulder axes leaves the worst-case torque unchanged at 55 N m and the reachable volume within 8 percent. A last wrist joint coaxial with the payload removes its gravity torque, reduces the adjacent wrist joint by almost a third and cuts the last joint demand tenfold in a 5 m/s scan, at the price of dexterity (mean 0.570 to 0.348). The same arm needs 20 to 37 times more torque for a 5 m/s target than for a 2 s motion: the scenario, not the trajectory, must be fixed first. Verification should use the URDF model of the detailed design; the toolkit is available at https://github.com/LasterLi/Sim4RAD.

</details>


### [145] [Beyond the End-Effector: Learning Whole-Arm Contact Manipulation from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17802)
*Bo-Ruei Huang,Daniel Seita,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出WACL分层框架，用三种机器人无关表示描述整臂接触任务，交由准静态表面接触优化器落地到机械臂，仅需10次无遥操作人类演示即可学习，并在末端约束下保持接触性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器人操作依赖末端执行器，而人类常用整臂接触提升效率、鲁棒性与适应性。让机器人具备该能力面临挑战：需决定手臂接触位置、生成有效的非抓取交互，同时满足末端执行器的任务位姿或安全约束。

Method: WACL为分层框架，将整臂接触任务用三种机器人无关表示描述：概括场景的语义关键点、指定手臂按压方式与位置的参考接触轨迹、以及由视觉语言模型从任务描述生成的安全路径约束。这些表示输入准静态表面接触优化器，在机械臂运动链上联合搜索接触连杆、连杆上的接触点及实现该接触的关节构型。分解后，接触轨迹可从每任务10次裸手人类演示中学习（无需遥操作），新末端约束只改变优化器输入而非已训练组件。

Result: 在7自由度机械臂上针对四个整臂任务的真实实验显示，在末端约束下WACL保持接触的成功率为78%，而强连杆位姿基线仅33%；且无需重训练即可泛化到新约束。

Conclusion: WACL通过机器人无关表示与接触优化的分解，实现了高效、可泛化的整臂接触操作，在末端约束场景下显著优于基线，并支持从少量非遥操作演示中学习。

Abstract: Robot manipulation has traditionally relied on end-effector-centric interactions. In contrast, humans routinely exploit whole-arm contacts to improve efficiency, robustness, and adaptability, especially when the hands are occupied or when precise grasping is unnecessary. Enabling similar capabilities in robots remains challenging: the robot must select where on its arm to make contact, generate effective non-prehensile interactions, and satisfy task-specific pose or safety constraints on the end-effector. We propose Whole-Arm Contact Learning (WACL), a hierarchical framework that describes each whole-arm contact task with three robot-agnostic representations and hands them to a quasi-static surface-contact optimizer: semantic keypoints that summarize the scene, a reference contact trajectory that specifies where and how the arm presses on the object, and safety-aware path constraints that a vision-language model generates from the task description to protect the end-effector's task. The robot's kinematic chain enters only in this optimizer, which grounds these representations onto the arm by jointly searching over which link makes contact, where on that link, and the joint configuration that realizes it. This decomposition lets WACL learn the contact trajectory from 10 bare-hand human demonstrations per task collected without teleoperation, while new end-effector constraints change only the optimizer's input rather than any trained component. Real-world experiments on a 7-DoF arm across four whole-arm tasks show that WACL sustains contact under end-effector constraints where a strong link-pose baseline fails (78% vs. 33% success) and generalizes to new constraints without retraining. Website: https://wacl-robot.github.io

</details>


### [146] [Which Trajectory Is Safer? Combining Rule and Learning for Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17806)
*Xuewen Luo,Linlian Jiang,Bo Yu,Fan Ding,Xinxin Zuo,Yang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出连续安全指标FutureRisk，通过量化轨迹交互接近不安全状态的程度与持续性，细粒度评估自动驾驶轨迹安全，优于现有代理安全指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞率虽为主流安全评价指标，但其二值特性掩盖了无碰撞轨迹之间的安全差异；现有代理安全指标在物理可解释性与交互情境自适应性之间难以兼顾。

Method: 核心为情境自适应风险模型（CARM）：将情境相关的安全时间分布与独立监督的关键事件时间分布相结合；安全判据围绕闭式物理先验有界，附加有界残差实现自适应而不脱离物理锚点。

Result: 在DeepAccident与Bench2Drive上，FutureRisk与原生碰撞标签的一致性高于现有代理安全指标，可区分无碰撞轨迹间的风险，并支持零样本跨数据集迁移；在四种规划器对比中揭示了二值碰撞结果所掩盖的安全差异。

Conclusion: FutureRisk是一种细粒度、可解释且情境自适应的轨迹安全评价指标，代码已开源。

Abstract: Collision rate remains a dominant metric for evaluating autonomous driving trajectory safety, but its binary nature obscures substantial safety differences among collision-free trajectories. Existing surrogate-safety metrics provide finer-grained assessments, yet often trade off physical interpretability against adaptivity to interaction context. We introduce FutureRisk, a continuous metric that quantifies the latent future risk of a trajectory by how closely and persistently its realized interactions approach unsafe states. At its core, our Context-Adaptive Risk Model (CARM) combines a context-dependent safety-time distribution with an independently supervised critical-event-time distribution. Its safety criterion is bounded around a closed-form physical prior, while a bounded residual provides additional adaptation without abandoning this physical anchor. Experiments on DeepAccident and Bench2Drive show that FutureRisk achieves stronger agreement with native collision labels than existing surrogate-safety measures, discriminates risk among collision-free trajectories, and transfers zero-shot across datasets. Comparisons across four planners further show that FutureRisk reveals safety differences hidden by binary collision outcomes. Together, these results establish FutureRisk as a fine-grained, interpretable, and context-adaptive metric for trajectory safety evaluation. Code is available at https://github.com/Xuewen2025/safety-evaluation.

</details>


### [147] [Physics-Aware Object-Centric SE(3) Forecasting for Real-Time Proactive Dynamic Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17843)
*Yirui Zhou,Hongli Xu,Ying Zheng,Yi Wang,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出以SE(3)刚体状态作为接触相关的紧凑任务充分抽象，构建“预测-选择”框架实现主动动态抓取，在仿真与真实任务中显著提升成功率，且推理仅需15毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态抓取需要机器人预测未来接触时刻的状态，而非仅对当前观测到的物体位姿做出反应。核心问题是：机器人应预测什么样的未来、以何种抽象层次预测。作者认为无需预测视觉上完整的未来场景，只需预测决定接触的紧凑物理状态。

Method: 提出以SE(3)接触相关刚体状态作为紧凑且任务充分的抽象，直接决定机器人在何时何地建立接触。基于此构建轻量级以物体为中心的“先预测后选择”框架：由短位姿历史预测未来刚体轨迹，并用可达性感知的拦截选择器在运动学与时间约束下选择可行的接触状态。通过随机化物理仿真在基元形状上训练，当恢复真实物体的度量几何与SE(3)状态历史后即可迁移。

Result: 在仿真中平均成功率从24.5%提升至62.0%；在真实世界的接球、传送带与转台拦截任务中从15.3%提升至67.3%。SE(3)预测器运行时间为15毫秒，比稠密视觉未来预测快数个数量级，支持实时接触决策。

Conclusion: 有效的主动抓取不需要想象整个未来场景，预测决定接触的紧凑物理未来，为实时动态抓取提供了有效且实用的抽象。

Abstract: Proactive dynamic grasping requires robots to predict the future state at which physical contact will occur, rather than reacting to the currently observed object pose. But what future must the robot predict, and at what level of abstraction? We argue that contact-relevant rigid-body state in SE(3) is a compact task-sufficient abstraction for this problem: it directly determines where and when the robot should establish contact, without requiring visually complete future scenes. This insight leads to a lightweight object-centric forecast-then-select framework that predicts future rigid-body trajectories from short pose histories and uses a reachability-aware interception selector to choose feasible contact states under kinematic and timing constraints. Trained with randomized physics simulation over primitive shapes, the forecaster transfers to real objects once their metric geometry and SE(3) state histories are recovered. Experiments across simulation and real-world dynamic grasping tasks show that our framework improves the average success rate from \(24.5\%\) to \(62.0\%\) in simulation, and from \(15.3\%\) to \(67.3\%\) in real-world catching, conveyor, and turntable interception. The SE(3) forecaster runs in \(15\,\mathrm{ms}\), orders of magnitude faster than dense visual future prediction, enabling real-time contact decision making. Our results suggest that effective proactive grasping does not require imagining the entire future scene. Instead, predicting the compact physical future that determines contact provides an effective and practical abstraction for real-time dynamic grasping.

</details>


### [148] [LAG-IO: Multi-IMU State Estimation for Legged Robots Using Factor Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17870)
*Yicheng Zhou,Longwen Chen,Qinghua Yu,Junhao Xiao,Huimin Lu,Xieyuanli Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LAG-IO：基于因子图的多IMU状态估计框架，结合连续概率接触检测，提升四足机器人本体感知状态估计鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在外部感知传感器不可靠的环境中，本体感知状态估计至关重要。传统腿部里程计依赖单个机身IMU与腿部运动学经EKF融合，存在线性化误差和硬接触假设问题，在打滑或动态运动时会产生严重漂移。

Method: 提出LAG-IO框架，在滑动窗口优化中紧耦合机身主IMU、多个腿部安装IMU与关节编码器测量。引入基于核密度估计（KDE）的连续概率接触检测模块，自适应缩放零速更新（ZUPT）因子的协方差；并构建运动学相对位置因子，将各独立腿部惯性状态结构性地锚定到机器人主体。

Result: 在四足机器人于多种非结构化地形上的实验表明，相比现有基线，LAG-IO实现了更高的平面轨迹精度，减小了不可观的垂直方向发散，在不稳定接触时提供更平滑的轨迹过渡，同时保持有竞争力的姿态可观测性。

Conclusion: LAG-IO通过多IMU因子图优化与概率接触检测，有效克服传统EKF腿部里程计的漂移问题，提升了复杂地形下足式机器人本体感知状态估计的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robust proprioceptive state estimation is crucial for legged robots, particularly in challenging environments where exteroceptive sensors are unreliable. While traditional leg odometry typically relies on a single body-mounted inertial measurement unit (IMU) fused with leg kinematics via an extended Kalman filter (EKF), such methods are vulnerable to linearization errors and hard-contact assumptions, leading to severe drift during slippage or dynamic locomotion.In this paper, we propose LAG-IO, a factor graph-based multi-IMU state estimation framework that tightly couples measurements from a main body IMU, multiple leg-mounted IMUs, and joint encoders within a sliding-window optimization. To handle non-stationary foot-ground interactions, we introduce a continuous probabilistic contact detection module based on kernel density estimation (KDE), which adaptively scales the covariance of zero-velocity update (ZUPT) factors. Furthermore, kinematic relative position factors are formulated to structurally anchor the independent leg inertial states to the robot's main body. Experimental results on a quadruped robot across various unstructured terrains demonstrate that, compared to existing baselines, LAG-IO achieves higher planar trajectory accuracy, reduces unobservable vertical divergence, and provides smoother trajectory transitions during unstable contacts, while maintaining competitive attitude observability.

</details>


### [149] [Information-based Unified Design Framework for NMR Magnetic Tracking System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17873)
*Seçkin Eren Yetim,Mehmet Efe Tiryaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息论的统一框架，用局部协方差分析评估核磁共振（NMR）磁追踪中接收器几何、标记分离和外部源配置对定位精度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMR磁追踪有望替代MRI定位用于医疗机器人导航，但标量场测量和场对齐标记矩限制了位置与姿态追踪，需要谨慎的构型设计。

Method: 采用基于局部协方差的信息论统一框架，分析接收器几何、标记分离以及板上/板外NMR追踪的外部源配置，并进行数值评估。

Result: 平面阵列精度取决于接收器数量与几何构型，三角形晶格阵列在9–20个接收器中取得最低最佳情况位置不确定度；两个空间分离的场对齐标记可实现器件轴向追踪，在工作区中心预测角不确定度低于2°；并建立点偶极子源–接收器对偶性。

Conclusion: 该统一框架可用于指导NMR磁追踪的构型设计，并通过源–接收器对偶性统一板上与板外NMR追踪的分析。

Abstract: Nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) magnetic tracking offers a promising alternative to magnetic resonance imaging (MRI)-based localization for medical robotic guidance. However, scalar field measurements and field-aligned marker moments constrain position and orientation tracking, requiring careful configuration design. We present a unified information-based framework that uses local covariance analysis to evaluate receiver geometry, marker separation, and external source configuration in on/off-board NMR tracking. Numerical evaluation shows that planar-array precision depends jointly on receiver count and geometry, with triangular lattice arrays achieving the lowest best-case position uncertainty among the evaluated layouts for 9--20 receivers. Two spatially separated, field-aligned markers enable device-axis tracking, with predicted angular uncertainty below $2^\circ$ at the evaluated workspace center for suitable marker separations. We further establish point-dipole source--receiver duality, unifying the analysis of on/off-board NMR tracking. These

</details>


### [150] [Agentic Robot Creation through Generation, Verification, and Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17881)
*Xuebing Li,Yibo He,Sitong Liu,Ran Ji,Jiayuan Gu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究AI智能体能否将高层用户意图转化为可运行的机器人并教会其行为，从"具身创建"与"行为创建"两方面展开，并实现了从生成设计到物理部署的完整闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI智能体能否将高层用户意图转化为功能可用的机器人并赋予其行为，这是具身智能自动化设计的关键问题。作者从具身（本体）创建与行为创建两个互补方面切入，分别考察多模态模型对机械需求的捕捉能力，以及如何将任务规范转化为可执行策略，从而推动端到端的机器人生成与学习流程。

Method: 1) 具身创建：评测现有多模态大模型（MLLMs）捕捉机械需求的能力，提出在制造前评估生成设计的计算指标；2) 行为创建：提出Roboforge智能体框架，将任务规范转为可执行策略，流程包括运动生成、策略学习与基于执行的修订。给定机器人本体与任务，智能体生成参考运动、配置学习目标并训练策略；关键在于"运动始终在环"——rollout反馈可反过来修订参考运动、奖励或两者。在四个不同机器人上进行行为创建实验，并与奖励精炼和一次性运动（one-shot motion）基线对比。

Result: 计算指标揭示了生成设计中反复出现的机械失效问题；行为实验表明，基于执行的修订能提升任务级行为表现与执行质量；此外，作者制造了由智能体生成的Gorilla机器人，并将学到的行为直接部署到物理机器人上。

Conclusion: AI智能体能够将高层用户意图转化为可运行且具备可学习行为的机器人，从生成设计经计算评估、行为学习到物理部署形成完整闭环；强调运动在环与执行落地修订对提升行为质量的重要性。

Abstract: Can an AI agent turn high-level user intent into a functioning robot and teach it how to behave? We study this question from two complementary aspects: embodiment and behavior creation. For embodiment creation, we investigate how well current multimodal models capture mechanical requirements, and introduce computational metrics for evaluating generated designs before fabrication. For behavior creation, we present Roboforge, an agentic framework that turns task specifications into executable policies through motion generation, policy learning, and execution-grounded revision. Given a robot embodiment and task, the agent generates a reference motion, configures the learning objective, and trains a policy. Crucially, motion remains in the loop: rollout feedback can revise the reference motion, reward, or both. We evaluate a generated embodiment across multiple MLLMs and behavior creation across four diverse robots against reward-refinement and one-shot motion baselines. Our computational metrics expose recurring mechanical failures in generated designs, while behavior experiments show that execution-grounded refinement improves task-level behavior and execution quality. Finally, we fabricate an agent-generated Gorilla and directly deploy learned behaviors on the physical robot.

</details>


### [151] [WoVR+: World Model as Reliable Simulator for Versatile VLA Policy Optimization with RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17922)
*Shangqing Zhou,Zhennan Jiang,Ning Ma,Xin Fu,Yupeng Zheng,Shuai Tian,Huangrui Li,Jiahao Liu,Chao Yu,Zhongpu Xia,Haoran Li,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WoVR+ 通过重新设计幻觉感知的世界模型与想象交互协议，缓解 VLA-RL 中的幻觉与长时程误差累积问题，在仿真和真机上显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件下的世界模型能减少 VLA-RL 中昂贵的真机交互，但幻觉会误导策略优化。将该框架扩展到更丰富的 VLA 接口和长时程任务时，面临时空与跨模态一致性、RL 中探索-可靠性平衡、以及rollout误差累积等挑战。

Method: 在 WoVR 的幻觉感知 WM-RL 框架上重新设计学习环境与想象交互协议：(1) 与 VLA 接口对齐的世界模型提供多视角预测与本体感受更新，同时缓解幻觉；(2) 分块噪声在保持基于流策略探索的同时限制损害预测的动作抖动；(3) 针对长时程任务采用阶段局部想象训练，结合阶段入口重置、阶段特定奖励、局部时域和可控采样，缩短生成前缀并平衡各阶段训练。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 仿真和 6 个真机任务上，WoVR+ 相比 SFT 分别提升 21.09% 和 16.7% 的成功率；长时程手机包装任务成功率从 30% 提升到 75%。

Conclusion: WoVR+ 验证了通过接口对齐的世界模型、分块噪声探索与阶段局部想象训练，可有效抑制幻觉并缓解长时程误差累积，从而大幅提升 VLA-RL 的成功率，为长时程 VLA 策略学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models can reduce costly real-robot interaction in reinforcement learning for Vision-Language-Action models (VLA-RL), but hallucinations can mislead policy optimization. Extending them to richer VLA interfaces and long-horizon tasks raises challenges in spatiotemporal and cross-modal consistency, exploration-reliability balance in RL, and compounding rollout errors. We present WoVR+, extending WoVR's hallucination-aware WM-RL framework by redesigning the learned environment and imagined interaction protocol: A VLA-interface-aligned world model provides multi-view prediction and proprioceptive updates while mitigating hallucinations. Chunk-wise noise preserves flow-based policy exploration while limiting prediction-degrading action jitter. For long-horizon tasks, stage-local imagined training combines stage-entry resets, stage-specific rewards, local horizons, and controllable sampling to shorten generated prefixes and balance training across stages. WoVR+ improves success over SFT by 21.09% and 16.7% on RoboTwin and 6 real-robot tasks. Long-horizon phone-packaging success rises from 30% to 75%. Videos are available at https://youmo445.github.io/WoVR_Plus.github.io/.

</details>


### [152] [GLST: Global-Local Spatio-Temporal Memory Decomposition Framework for Online Chained-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17927)
*Yousef Liang,Ryan Ardalan,Yarden Goraly,Chenhao Zhu,Yue Yin,Jonas Frey,Claire Tomlin,Marco Pavone,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究在线链接目标导航（chained-goal navigation）问题，提出GLST记忆分解框架，将潜在信念分为全局-局部空间与长-短时间表示，并引入目标条件奖励平衡探索与利用，在仿真与Spot四足机器人上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无地图导航方法大多假设整个episode中目标固定，无法应对实际场景中目标在线变化（现有目标重定向或新目标出现且无显式转换信号）的情况。在此设定下，导航可能既需要持续探索又需要策略性重访，产生冲突的记忆需求。目标是解决部分可观测、目标演化下的长时序导航问题。

Method: 提出Global-Local Spatio-Temporal Belief Learning (GLST)记忆分解框架，将潜在信念分离为全局-局部空间表示和长-短时间表示，用于长时序推理；并引入目标条件奖励（goal-conditioned rewards）以在演化目标下平衡探索与利用。

Result: 在多种地形的仿真实验中，链接目标导航性能优于基线方法；使用Spot四足机器人进行的真实机器人实验进一步验证了记忆分解框架的有效性。

Conclusion: GLST通过记忆分解与目标条件奖励，有效应对在线链接目标导航中探索与重访的冲突需求，仿真与真实机器人实验均支持其有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in real-world environments often requires long-horizon operation under partial observability and evolving goals. Existing mapless navigation approaches predominantly assume fixed goals throughout an episode, limiting applicability to practical scenarios where goals change online during execution. We study online chained-goal navigation, where goals evolve through existing-goal redirection or new-goal emergence without explicit transition signals. Under this setting, we find navigation may require either continued exploration or strategic revisitation, creating conflicting memory requirements. We propose Global-Local Spatio-Temporal Belief Learning (GLST), a memory-decomposition framework that separates latent beliefs into global-local spatial and long-short temporal representations for long-horizon reasoning. We further introduce goal-conditioned rewards to balance exploration and exploitation under evolving goals. Extensive experiments in simulation across diverse terrains demonstrate improved chained-goal navigation performance over baselines. Real-robot experiments using the Spot quadruped further validate our memory decomposition framework.

</details>


### [153] [Locomotion with Online Awareness of Dynamics for Precision Footholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17930)
*Kunzhao Ren,Zhishuo Zhu,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LOAD是一个感知式运动框架，结合显式惯性估计与隐式动力学特征，实现机器人负载下的窄梁行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知负载会改变机器人动力学，在立足点余量很小时难以精确执行接触任务。

Method: 使用独立循环模块从本体感知推断躯干惯性变化和任务相关历史；将高度图教师蒸馏为基于深度的学生模型。

Result: 在仿真和硬件上跨多种梁宽与负载配置评估：隐式动力学特征加速训练，显式惯性估计提升成功率，两者结合使LOAD接近使用真值惯性参数的privileged策略性能。硬件实现单侧6kg负载通过10cm梁、单侧9kg通过15cm梁、均匀分布11kg通过15cm梁。

Conclusion: 结合物理估计与历史特征有助于未知负载下的精确运动控制。

Abstract: Unknown payloads alter robot dynamics and make precise contact execution difficult when foothold margins are small. We present LOAD, a perceptive locomotion framework that combines explicit inertial estimates with implicit dynamics features for narrow-beam traversal. Separate recurrent modules infer torso inertial changes and task-relevant history from proprioception, while a height-map teacher is distilled into a depth-based student. We extensively evaluate LOAD and its variants in simulation and on hardware across beam widths and payload configurations. The results show that implicit dynamics features accelerate training, while explicit inertial estimation further improves traversal success; combining both enables LOAD to approach the performance of a privileged policy supplied with ground-truth inertial parameters. Hardware experiments demonstrate traversal with unilateral payloads of 6 kg on a 10 cm beam and 9 kg on a 15 cm beam, and an evenly distributed 11 kg payload on a 15 cm beam. These results support combining physical estimates and history features for precision locomotion under unknown loads.

</details>


### [154] [ReEnvision: Future-Guided Selective Action Repair for Long-Horizon Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17977)
*Xiyin Zeng,Xuewei Huang,Yi Lu,Weihao Yuan,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ReEnvision框架，通过未来引导的选择性动作修复应对长时程机器人操作中的执行偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作需要在相互依赖的动作序列上稳健执行。现有方法要么重规划整个动作块（在分布偏移下会从不熟悉状态重新生成整段动作，可能给原本有效的动作引入错误），要么逐步纠正（反应过晚，未显式预判偏差对后续动作的影响）。

Method: ReEnvision利用执行反馈更新联合的未来-动作信念，并学习该信念修正如何影响每个未执行动作，从而实现对缓存动作块中受影响动作的选择性修复，保留有效动作，仅在严重偏差时才进行完整重规划。

Result: 在CALVIN上达到86.6%的5步成功率；L-CALVIN上10步成功率从56%提升至63%；RLBench上平均成功率88.8%；五个真实世界操作任务上平均成功率82.8%。受控修复实验进一步验证了定位受影响动作并保留有效动作的优势。

Conclusion: 通过未来引导的选择性修复，ReEnvision在多项基准和真实任务中优于传统重规划与逐步纠正方法，证明了局部化修复加保留有效动作策略的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires robust execution over sequences of interdependent actions. Existing methods typically handle execution deviations by replanning the remaining action chunk or correcting actions step by step as new observations arrive. However, a local deviation may affect only a few actions under distribution shift, yet replanning regenerates the entire chunk from an unfamiliar state, potentially introducing errors into otherwise valid actions. Step-wise correction may react too late, as it does not explicitly anticipate how the deviation affects subsequent actions. We propose ReEnvision, a future-guided selective action repair framework that updates a joint future-action belief using execution feedback. It then learns how the resulting belief revision affects each unexecuted action. The resulting belief revision enables selective repair of only the affected actions within a cached action chunk, preserving valid actions and reserving full replanning for severe deviations. On CALVIN, L-CALVIN, and RLBench, ReEnvision achieves 86.6% 5-step success, improves 10-step success from 56% to 63%, and reaches an average success rate of 88.8%, respectively. On five real-world manipulation tasks, it achieves an average success rate of 82.8%. Controlled repair experiments further demonstrate the benefits of localizing affected actions and preserving valid ones during execution.

</details>


### [155] [Breaking the Average: Overcoming Convergent Defaults in Robotic Manipulation via Diagnostic Chain-of-Thought and Structured Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17983)
*Mohammed Daba,Waleed Abdulrahman Saleh AlAkwa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免参数更新的三阶段框架，识别并纠正VLA模型的"收敛默认"行为，用Lens Prompts重塑采样分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型（如π_0.5）通过大规模数据的行为克隆获得泛化能力，但因训练数据中的众数平均（mode averaging）出现分布收敛，退化为"安全"但次优的平均行为，形成通用抓取策略即"收敛默认"，在受限或长尾场景中失效。

Method: 三阶段方法：(1) Probing for Defaults，探测任务特定的模式坍缩；(2) Diagnostic Root Cause Analysis，利用VLA的潜在推理能力，将次优动作背后的逻辑言语化；(3) Structured Guidance Injection，将具体且差异化的替代方案编码为可复用的"Lens Prompts"，以偏移采样分布。全程无需参数更新。

Result: 在ManiSkill3仿真环境中使用π_0.5进行验证；论文假设（hypothesize）该方法在复杂操作任务中优于原始π_0.5与标准Chain-of-Thought提示，能有效打破收敛默认。

Conclusion: 该工作提出一种免训练、可解释的VLA行为纠偏范式，通过诊断+提示注入打破模式坍缩，缓解长尾与受限场景下的次优默认行为；实证结论尚待完整实验确认。

Abstract: Recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, such as $π_{0.5}$, have demonstrated impressive generalization capabilities by scaling behavior cloning across massive datasets. However, these models frequently suffer from distributional convergence, a phenomenon where the policy regresses to "safe" but suboptimal mean behaviors due to mode averaging in the training data. This often results in generic grasp strategies, Convergent Defaults, that fail in constrained or long-tail scenarios. In this work, we propose a novel framework to identify and correct these behaviors without parameter updates. We introduce a three-stage methodology: (1) Probing for Defaults, identifying task-specific mode collapse; (2) Diagnostic Root Cause Analysis, utilizing the VLA's latent reasoning capabilities to verbalize the logic behind suboptimal actions; and (3) Structured Guidance Injection, where concrete, distinct alternatives are encoded into reusable "Lens Prompts" that shift the sampling distribution. We validate this approach using $π_{0.5}$ in ManiSkill3 simulation environments. We hypothesize that our method outperforms vanilla $π_{0.5}$ and standard Chain-of-Thought prompting by effectively breaking convergent defaults in complex manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [156] [TRACE: Efficient Transfer Learning for Neural Whole-Body Control Across Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.17999)
*Qingyu Lan,Zhihao Tian,Zifan Wang,Junwei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE是一个跨本体适配框架，在保留预训练运动表征的同时将反馈控制专门化到目标机器人，以极低算力将人形控制器从Unitree G1迁移至H2，实现96.08%的运动跟踪成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形行为基础模型（BFM）能实现多样化的全身控制，但将预训练控制器适配到新的机器人本体时，因运动学与动力学不匹配而面临困难，需要一种高效且能保持预训练运动表征的跨本体适配方法。

Method: TRACE首先在输入输出投影层注入运动学先验，并对关节标称动力学进行对齐以减小本体差异、支持稳定微调；随后结合编码器蒸馏与低秩强化学习，在经质量筛选的紧凑运动集上训练，并采用两阶段课程：先建立稳定跟踪，再精炼困难动作。

Result: 将SONIC从Unitree G1迁移到Unitree H2，使用1000万运动帧、单张RTX PRO 6000 GPU上约8小时训练，GPU小时成本不足从零训练SONIC的0.1%；在67,953个评估运动、高度缩放跟踪准则下取得96.08%成功率，并在真机上完成动态运动跟踪与全身遥操作演示，所采集数据可支持下游视觉-语言-动作策略的后训练。

Conclusion: 结果表明，预训练人形控制器可在不同体型尺度与动力学的机器人之间实现实用化复用，为跨本体人形控制提供高效适配路径。

Abstract: Humanoid behavior foundation models (BFMs) enable diverse whole-body control, but adapting a pretrained controller to a new embodiment remains challenging because of kinematic and dynamic mismatch. We present TRACE, a framework for efficient cross-embodiment adaptation that preserves pretrained motion representations while specializing feedback control to the target robot.TRACE first injects kinematic priors into input and output projection layers and aligns nominal joint dynamics to reduce embodiment mismatch and support stable fine-tuning. It then combines encoder distillation with low-rank reinforcement learning on a compact, quality-screened motion set, using a two-phase curriculum to establish stable tracking before refining difficult motions. We apply TRACE to transfer SONIC from Unitree G1 to Unitree H2 using 10 million motion frames and approximately eight hours of training on a single RTX PRO 6000 GPU, at less than 0.1% of the GPU-hour cost of training SONIC from scratch. The adapted controller achieves 96.08\% success on 67,953 evaluation motions under a height-scaled tracking criterion. Real-robot demonstrations span dynamic motion tracking and whole-body teleoperation, with collected demonstrations supporting downstream vision-language-action policy post-training. These results demonstrate practical reuse of a pretrained humanoid controller across robots with different body scales and dynamics.

</details>


### [157] [Data-Free Continual Learning for World Action Model with Rectified Generative Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18038)
*Kaipeng Fang,Hangxu Liu,Chaojian Shi,Yunsong Zhou,Hongzi Zhu,Minyi Guo,Jingkuan Song,Lianli Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出R²-WAM，让世界动作模型通过"再生自己的过去"实现无数据持续学习，在LIBERO及真实任务上减少90%以上遗忘，接近使用真实数据的经验回放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于预训练视频生成模型的世界动作模型（WAM）能快速适应新机器人任务，但在顺序微调时会遗忘旧任务。经验回放可缓解遗忘，但机器人场景中策略仅以权重形式部署，历史训练数据不可得，因此需要无数据的持续学习方案。

Method: 关键观察是WAM能自回归地重新生成旧任务的完整rollout并回放以缓解遗忘。针对原始rollout远不如真实数据的两大问题——无法识别任务完成导致任务结束后继续生成无意义动作、开环生成下动作与观测逐渐漂移——提出R²-WAM：用智能评估器在任务完成处截断生成的rollout，并过滤掉动作与观测物理不一致的rollout。

Result: 在四个LIBERO套件和五个真实世界任务上，R²-WAM相对顺序微调减少90%以上遗忘，且在不访问任何旧任务数据的情况下几乎匹配经验回放。分析进一步揭示WAM的遗忘集中在逆向动力学建模：被遗忘的WAM仍能生成视觉上合理的旧任务观测，但生成的动作失效。

Conclusion: WAM具备通过再生自身过去实现无数据持续学习的潜力；经截断与物理一致性过滤的生成式回放可有效缓解遗忘，且遗忘的主要瓶颈在于逆向动力学建模而非观测生成。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) built on pretrained video generation models adapt quickly to new robotic tasks, but they forget previously learned tasks under sequential fine-tuning. Experience replay (ER) mitigates such forgetting by rehearsing data of previous tasks, yet this data is often unavailable in robotics, where a policy is deployed as weights only and its training data is not shared along with it. In this work, we ask whether a WAM can achieve data-free continual learning by regenerating its own past. Our key observation is that a WAM can indeed autoregressively regenerate complete rollouts of previously learned tasks and replay them to mitigate forgetting. However, replaying such raw rollouts still lags far behind replaying real data, which we attribute to two issues. First, the WAM is unable to recognize task completion and keeps generating purposeless motion after the task is done. Second, under open-loop generation, the generated actions gradually drift away from the generated observations. To address both issues, we present rectified generative replay (R$^2$-WAM), which truncates generated rollouts at task completion with an agentic evaluator and filters out rollouts whose actions are physically inconsistent with the observations. On the four LIBERO suites and five real-world tasks, R$^2$-WAM reduces forgetting by over 90% relative to sequential fine-tuning and nearly matches ER without accessing any data from previous tasks. Our analysis further reveals that forgetting in WAMs concentrates in inverse dynamics modeling: a forgotten WAM still produces visually plausible observations of previous tasks while its generated actions fail.

</details>


### [158] [Contractor Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18040)
*Simon Rohou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Contractor Filters（CFs），一种面向非线性连续时间状态估计的集合隶属架构，用于处理有界不确定性和稀疏异常值，并在实验中优于鲁棒粒子滤波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非线性连续时间状态估计中存在有界不确定性与稀疏异常值的挑战，现有集合论滤波方法每次更新后需重新物化几何包围盒，缺乏灵活性；同时鲁棒粒子滤波在持续偏差和空间混叠下可靠性下降。

Method: CFs以contractor的组合符号化表示可行状态，而非每次更新后重建几何包围。该架构在集合论滤波方程与数值实现之间提供抽象层，使预测、校正、模糊性与异常值处理可在同一代数中表达而不预设状态几何。作者推导了精确标称递推与保证鲁棒包围，并通过验证流包含保持可靠性；可选的子铺砌压缩提供有限外表示。

Result: 在仅测距与地形定位任务中，面对异常值、概率模型失配、关联模糊性和多模态，CFs在所有测试配置下均保持模拟真值，而粒子滤波在持续偏差和空间混叠下可靠性下降。Contractor强度与压缩预算控制铺砌紧密度与计算成本之间的权衡，同时保持包围保证。

Conclusion: CFs为非线性连续时间状态估计提供了一种统一的集合隶属架构，在不预设状态几何的前提下保持可靠性保证，并通过contractor强度与压缩预算实现精度与成本的灵活权衡。

Abstract: We introduce Contractor Filters (CFs), a set-membership architecture for nonlinear continuous-time state estimation with bounded uncertainties and sporadic outliers. CFs represent feasible states symbolically by compositions of contractors rather than rematerializing a geometric enclosure after each update. This provides an abstraction layer between the set-theoretic filtering equations and their numerical realization, so prediction, correction, ambiguity, and outlier handling can be expressed within one algebra without prescribing a state geometry. We derive an exact nominal recursion and a guaranteed robust enclosure, with validated flow inclusions preserving soundness. Optional sub-paving compression then provides finite outer representations. CFs are evaluated against a robust particle filter on range-only and terrain-based localization under outliers, probabilistic model mismatch, association ambiguity, and multimodality. CFs retain the simulated truth in all tested configurations, while particle-filter reliability degrades under persistent bias and spatial aliasing. Contractor strength and compression budget control the trade-off between paving tightness and computational cost, while preserving the enclosure guarantee.

</details>


### [159] [LADDER: A Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18053)
*Petko Petkov,Giuliano Albanese,Nikolay Nikolov,Luc Van Gool,Jonathan Tremblay,Danda Pani Paudel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LADDER长时程机器人操作基准，跨越1至10个子目标，揭示低层策略在长时程任务上的崩溃现象，并评估两类编排范式的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人操作策略能有效学习短时程技能，但在长时程任务上迅速退化。现有基准无法隔离失败原因，因为任务时程过短或与场景、物体变化纠缠在一起。

Method: 构建LADDER基准，在同一环境内将任务从1个子目标扩展到10个子目标，包含三个套件（SimplerEnv-Long、RobotArena-∞-Long、RoboLab-Long）、145个任务、八个类别、两种具身形式，每个系统2024个仿真回合。评估两类编排范式：面向学习型低层策略的文本子目标（VoLo）与工具选择编排（CaP-X、GPT-Policy、PSP-Agent、TiPToP）。

Result: 直接给定完整指令时，低层策略在单子目标任务上达85.4%，但在扩展任务上降至接近零。编排可缓解该问题，但受限于低层策略的物理执行误差而非高层规划，仅达约25%。基于GPT-6 Astra的GPT-Policy工具选择编排显著优于学习型策略方法，但耗时与成本预算高，成功率仅约62%。跨两种具身形式的真实世界实验证实低层策略的崩溃及编排带来的增益。

Conclusion: LADDER基准有效隔离了长时程操作中的失败因素，表明当前系统的主要瓶颈在于低层执行的物理误差而非高层规划，编排虽能带来提升但成本与成功率仍待改善。

Abstract: General-purpose robot manipulation policies learn short-horizon skills effectively, but degrade rapidly on long-horizon tasks. Existing benchmarks cannot isolate failures because task horizon is either too short or entangled with scene and object variations. We propose LADDER, a long-horizon robot manipulation benchmark scaling from 1 to 10 subgoals within the same environment over three suites (SimplerEnv-Long, RobotArena-$\infty$-Long, and RoboLab-Long), 145 tasks, eight categories, two embodiments, and 2,024 simulated episodes per system. We evaluate two orchestration paradigms: text subgoals for learned low-level policies (VoLo) and tool selection orchestration (CaP-X, GPT-Policy, PSP-Agent, TiPToP). Given the full instruction directly, low-level policies achieve 85.4% on single-subgoal tasks, but drop to near zero on extended tasks. While orchestration remedies this, it remains bounded by physical execution errors of the low-level policy rather than high-level planning and achieves only ~25%. Tool selection orchestration with GPT-Policy on GPT-6 Astra significantly outperforms learned-policy methods, but incurs significant time and cost budget and succeeds only ~62%. Real-world experiments across two embodiments confirm the collapse of the low-level policy and the gain from orchestration. Project website: https://ladder.insait.ai

</details>


### [160] [LynxScenes: A Large-Scale Real-World Benchmark for Outdoor Wheel-Legged Robot Perception, Navigation, Imagination and Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18093)
*Jun Tian,Hao Wu,Ruo Chen,Yili Liao,Hongyi Lin,Jincheng Yu,Chao Li,Yingjie Niu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LynxScenes——面向户外轮腿机器人导航的大规模真实世界数据集与基准，含约10万段30秒导航片段、833.6小时、3294.7公里、300万标注帧，覆盖轨迹预测、BEV感知、未来场景预测与多视图重建四类任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 户外共享空间中的轮腿机器人需要精准感知、社会合规导航、未来场景推理与场景重建等综合能力。但现有数据集通常只支持其中单一能力，且很少在真实轮腿平台上提供密集的空间与行为级监督。

Method: 使用LYNX M20轮腿机器人采集同步数据：4个鱼眼相机、双LiDAR、IMU、里程计与机器人状态。标注包括27类2082万个有向3D框、1389万条道路结构标注，以及分三层、与连续未来轨迹对齐的20条导航指令。支持四个互补任务，并提出基于日志回放的伪闭环评估器，从安全、进度与指令遵从三方面评价轨迹预测。

Result: 构建了约10万段30秒导航片段、833.6小时、3294.7公里、300万标注帧的大规模数据集；对代表性导航、感知、世界模型与重建方法进行广泛评估，结果显示近距离户外机器人导航仍存在显著挑战。

Conclusion: LynxScenes为户外轮腿机器人提供统一的大规模真实世界数据与评测基准，数据集和评测工具包计划公开发布，以推动该领域研究。

Abstract: Wheel-legged robots operating in outdoor spaces shared with humans require accurate perception, socially compliant navigation, future-scene reasoning, and scene reconstruction. However, existing datasets typically support these capabilities in isolation and rarely provide dense spatial and behavior-level supervision on real wheel-legged platforms. We introduce LynxScenes, a large-scale real-world dataset and benchmark collected with a LYNX M20 wheel-legged robot. It contains approximately 100K 30-s navigation clips spanning 833.6 h and 3,294.7 km, with 3.0M annotated frames. The synchronized recordings include four fisheye cameras, dual LiDARs, IMU, odometry, and robot states. Annotations comprise 20.82M oriented 3D boxes across 27 categories, 13.89M road-structure annotations, and 20 navigation instructions organized into three layers and aligned with continuous future trajectories. LynxScenes supports four complementary tasks: instruction-conditioned trajectory prediction, BEV-based perception of 3D objects and road structures, future scene prediction, and multi-view scene reconstruction. For trajectory prediction, we further introduce a log-replay-based pseudo-closed-loop evaluator that measures safety, progress, and instruction compliance beyond trajectory imitation. Extensive evaluations across representative navigation, perception, world-model, and reconstruction methods reveal substantial remaining challenges in close-range outdoor robot navigation. The dataset and evaluation toolkit are planned for public release.

</details>


### [161] [ForeTac: Predictive Contact Guidance for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18118)
*Zhiyuan Chen,Shuai Chen,Haoran Zhou,Yizhe Zhao,Kaiyuan Yang,Zhixiang Duan,Shaoqing Xu,Ran Cao,Diyun Xiang,Yangwei You*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ForeTac预测式接触引导框架，以"表示-预测-评估-引导"闭环让生成策略在执行前预判触觉后果，真实机器人任务成功率从36.3%提升至71.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉相似的动作可能导致接触丢失、卡阻、滑移或不可逆损伤，而触觉反馈往往在关键转变之后才到达，因此接触丰富的操作需要生成式策略在执行前预见并评估候选动作的接触后果。

Method: ForeTac构成"表示—预测—评估—引导"循环：编码当前触觉状态，预测候选动作序列的触觉后果，并用从成功与失败物理交互中学到的接触质量目标评估预测轨迹；部署时梯度经触觉预测器回传以引导动作生成，而预训练模型参数保持冻结。

Result: 在平面与曲面擦拭、卡片刷扫、易碎物体抓取四项真实机器人任务上，配合扩散与流匹配策略，平均任务成功率由36.3%提升至71.3%，平均接触成功率由20.0%提升至66.3%（对比仅视觉的Diffusion Policy）；组件分析支持触觉表示与动作条件前瞻的有效性，并表明预测触觉可保持接触质量排序。

Conclusion: 以触觉预测为先验、以接触质量为评价信号来引导生成策略，可在执行前规避不良接触结果，显著提升接触丰富操作的任务与接触成功率。

Abstract: Visually similar actions can produce contact loss, jamming, slippage, or irreversible damage, while tactile feedback may arrive after a critical transition. Contact-rich manipulation therefore requires generative policies to anticipate and evaluate whether candidate actions will produce desirable contact outcomes, then adjust action generation before execution. We present ForeTac, a predictive contact-guidance framework organized as a represent-predict-evaluate-guide loop. ForeTac encodes the current tactile state, predicts the tactile consequences of a candidate action sequence, and evaluates the predicted trajectory using a contact-quality objective learned from successful and failed physical interactions. At deployment, its gradient propagates through the tactile predictor to guide action generation while the pretrained model parameters remain frozen. We evaluate ForeTac on four real-robot tasks (planar and curved-surface wiping, card swiping, and fragile-object grasping) with diffusion and flow-matching policies. Across these tasks, ForeTac raises the average task success rate from 36.3% to 71.3% and the average contact success rate (CSR) from 20.0% to 66.3% compared with vision-only Diffusion Policy. Component analyses support the tactile representation and action-conditioned foresight, and show that predicted touch preserves contact-quality ordering. Project page: https://foretac.github.io/.

</details>


### [162] [MARTS: A Modular Agricultural Robot for Targeted Spraying of Diseased Tomato Plants in Greenhouses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18123)
*Rajmeet Singh,Manveen Kaur,Yusuf Adamu,Lochan Kshetrimayum,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MARTS——面向商业温室番茄的低成本模块化靶向喷洒机器人，打通"植株级病害检测→叶片级精准施药"闭环，在真实温室实现92.4%靶向成功率并减少71%农药用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室番茄病害防治仍以广谱喷施为主，健康与病患组织被同等施药，既造成农药浪费，又使工人暴露于有害气溶胶中，且效果依赖操作者经验。因此需要一种能对病害叶片进行局部精准施药的低成本机器人系统。

Method: MARTS集成Clearpath Husky A200移动底盘、UR5机械臂、基于单个12 V微型隔膜泵的新型模块化喷洒末端执行器，以及腕部安装的Intel RealSense D435 RGB-D相机。病害检测复用已发表的番茄叶片病害视觉语言模型TLDVLM（Grounding DINO、SAM-2与LoRA微调BLIP-2，10类准确率97.27%）；本文侧重驱动、控制与系统集成。ROS流程将检测结果提升为3D目标，利用拟合叶片法线在120 mm间距处生成避碰喷嘴位姿，并按病斑面积调节泵脉冲时长。

Result: 水敏纸标定显示脉冲—体积呈线性关系（R²=0.994）。MuJoCo仿真中，自适应底盘重定位策略对18个航点实现100%覆盖，最大姿态误差1.1°。在阿联酋Al Ain的SILAL商业温室对自然感染的樱桃番茄开展部署，MARTS在12次自主试验中处理312片病叶，靶向成功率92.4%，平均喷雾中心误差18.7 mm，相邻叶片过喷率6.1%，农药用量较广播喷施减少71%。单叶平均周期4.8 s，喷洒子系统成本412美元，比同类系统低6–10倍。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在商用现货硬件上即可实现精准的叶片级靶向施药，MARTS以低成本验证了从检测到驱动的完整闭环方案的有效性。

Abstract: Broadcast spraying remains the dominant method of disease control in greenhouse tomato production, yet it treats healthy and diseased tissue alike, exposes workers to hazardous aerosols, and depends on operator skill. This paper presents MARTS (Modular Agricultural Robot for Targeted Spraying), a low-cost robotic platform that closes the loop from plant-level disease detection to leaf-localised spray delivery in commercial greenhouses. MARTS integrates a Clearpath Husky A200 mobile base, a UR5 manipulator, a novel modular spraying end-effector built around a single 12 V micro-diaphragm pump, and a wrist-mounted Intel RealSense D435 RGB-D camera. Disease detection reuses our previously published Tomato Leaf Disease Vision Language Model (TLDVLM; Grounding DINO, SAM-2 and LoRA-tuned BLIP-2, 97.27% accuracy over 10 classes); this work focuses on actuation, control and system integration. A ROS pipeline lifts detections into 3D targets, generates collision-aware nozzle poses at a 120 mm stand-off using fitted leaf normals, and scales pump pulse duration to lesion area. Water-sensitive paper calibration showed a linear pulse-volume relationship (R^2 = 0.994). In MuJoCo simulation, an adaptive base-repositioning strategy achieved 100% coverage of 18 waypoints with a maximum orientation error of 1.1 deg. Deployed at the SILAL commercial greenhouse (Al Ain, UAE) on cherry tomatoes under natural infection, MARTS treated 312 diseased leaves in 12 autonomous trials with 92.4% targeting success, 18.7 mm mean spray-centre error, 6.1% adjacent-leaf overspray, and a 71% reduction in pesticide volume versus broadcast spraying. Mean cycle time was 4.8 s per leaf, and the spraying subsystem costs USD 412, 6-10x lower than comparable systems, showing that accurate leaf-level targeting is achievable on commodity hardware.

</details>


### [163] [SkipWorld: Latent Skip Planning with Distilled World Models for Efficient Visual Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18135)
*Yifei Yang,Zehua Fan,Zike Yan,Zhongyu Li,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SkipWorld潜在跳步规划框架，通过三项组件提升视觉导航长程规划的效率与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型视觉导航需在候选轨迹下预测未来状态进行动作规划，但长时域展开面临两大难题：模型用真实视觉历史训练却在闭环中预测，存在训练-推理不匹配；逐步展开评估大量候选轨迹计算代价高昂。

Method: SkipWorld包含三部分：(i) Latent AutoRoll，在潜在空间递归展开未来状态以缩小训练与闭环推理的差距；(ii) SkipDistill，将潜在自回归模型蒸馏为可直接预测长步幅未来潜在状态的跳步世界模型；(iii) Skip-then-Refine Planning，双模型粗到细规划器，先用跳步模型筛选候选轨迹，再用更强的自回归模型对入选候选重排序。

Result: 在RECON、HuRoN、SCAND和TartanDrive上，相比NWM，平均ATE降低4.2%、RPE降低2.7%，规划时间减少62.4%，实现2.7倍加速。

Conclusion: 该设计在保持精确轨迹评估的同时减少冗余展开计算，兼顾精度与效率，适用于长时域视觉导航规划。

Abstract: World-model-based visual navigation enables action planning by predicting future states under candidate trajectories. However, long-horizon rollout remains challenging: models are often trained with ground-truth visual histories but deployed in closed-loop prediction, and evaluating many candidates with step-by-step rollout is computationally expensive. We propose SkipWorld, a latent skip planning framework for efficient visual navigation. SkipWorld introduces three components: (i) Latent AutoRoll, which trains a world model to recursively roll out future states in latent space, reducing the mismatch between training and closed-loop inference; (ii) SkipDistill, which distills the latent autoregressive model into a skip world model that directly predicts long-stride future latent states; and (iii) Skip-then-Refine Planning, a dual-model coarse-to-fine planner that first filters candidate trajectories with the skip model and then reranks selected candidates with the stronger autoregressive model. This design preserves accurate trajectory evaluation while reducing redundant rollout computation. Experiments on RECON, HuRoN, SCAND, and TartanDrive show that, compared with NWM, SkipWorld reduces the average ATE by 4.2% and RPE by 2.7%, while decreasing planning time by 62.4% with a 2.7-fold speedup.

</details>


### [164] [Walk Like This: Stylized Locomotion for Physics-Based Characters through Example-Driven Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18147)
*Joshua Aurand,Agon Serifi,Sammy Christen,Ruben Grandia,Espen Knoop,Marco Hutter,Moritz Bächer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个实时富有表现力的腿足运动框架，实现速度与风格独立控制，无需显式风格标签，可泛化至未见风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动控制器训练时通常需要显式风格标签，难以利用大规模无结构运动数据，也无法泛化到未见风格。

Method: 通过定制采样策略解耦风格与功能性运动，训练风格编码器，其输出条件化自回归扩散模型；推理时用户提供风格参考片段与摇杆速度指令，扩散模型生成运动学参考，由强化学习控制器跟踪；并提出注意力偏置机制与速度指令校正策略。

Result: 在仿真中的物理人形角色与实体机器人上验证，展示了鲁棒的实时控制与连续风格调制能力。

Conclusion: 该方法无需风格标签即可利用大规模无结构运动数据，实现速度与风格的独立、连续控制，并成功迁移到真实机器人。

Abstract: We present a real-time framework for expressive legged locomotion that enables independent control over velocity and style. Unlike existing data-driven controllers, which typically require explicit style labels for training, our method efficiently leverages large-scale unstructured motion data and generalizes to unseen styles. We disentangle style from functional locomotion through a tailored sampling strategy used to train a style encoder whose output conditions an autoregressive diffusion model. During inference, the user provides a style reference clip and a joystick velocity command. The diffusion model then generates a kinematic reference tracked by a reinforcement learning controller. To improve user controllability and stylization strength, we propose an attention biasing mechanism and a velocity command correction strategy. We demonstrate our method on a physics-based humanoid character in simulation and on a physical robot, showcasing robust real-time control and continuous style modulation. The results can be best seen in our supplemental video (https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=roFnS6N7nJ4).

</details>


### [165] [Force Sensor-Based Picking Pattern Analysis and Two-Jaw Gripper Design for Cherry Tomato Robotic Harvesting in Greenhouse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18160)
*Rajmeet Singh,Manveen Kaur,Yusuf Adamu,Irfan Hussain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于力数据驱动的自主圣女果采收框架，集成六轴力/力矩传感、仿生柔性夹爪与移动机器人平台，在商业温室中实现86.0%采摘成功率、2.3%损伤率和8.4秒单果周期，优于多种现有末端执行器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 温室圣女果采收面临果实成簇密集、表皮脆弱、需轻柔且有效脱果等挑战，现有末端执行器效率低、易损伤果实，缺乏对手工采摘力学特征的量化指导。

Method: 通过可穿戴ATI六轴力/力矩传感器采集150次手工采摘（抓取、扭转、横向拉拽各50次）的力学特征；据此设计指尖局部化Finray夹爪（TPU 95A柔性几何+PLA刚性本体，模拟人手双刚度指尖），由Dynamixel电机以电流力矩控制施加夹持力与45°腕部扭转；经有限元分析验证应力，集成于UR5臂与Husky A200底盘，并在Isaac Sim及阿布扎比SILAL商业温室进行50次自主试验。

Result: 抓取需峰值正压力3.92和0.45 N，扭转产生19.20±2.10 Nmm主导力矩且对果串扰动小，纯横向拉拽不足以可靠脱果；有限元显示最大von Mises应力0.24 MPa，低于表皮损伤阈值；混合夹-扭-拉策略采摘成功率86.0%、损伤率2.3%、周期8.4秒，优于真空(66.3%)、旋转(70.1%)、夹持(76.0%)和吸切(52.4%)末端执行器。

Conclusion: 该框架在测试设施与品种条件下验证了自主圣女果采收的初步可行性，力数据驱动的仿生设计可有效提升采摘成功率并降低果实损伤。

Abstract: Robotic harvesting of cherry tomatoes in greenhouses remains challenging due to dense fruit clusters, delicate skin, and the need for gentle yet effective detachment. This study presents a force-data-driven framework for autonomous cherry tomato harvesting that integrates six-axis force/torque sensing, a bio-inspired compliant gripper, and deployment on a mobile robotic platform. A wearable ATI six-axis force/torque sensor mounted on the index finger captured kinetic signatures of three elemental picking patterns (gripping, twisting, and lateral pulling) across 150 manual trials (50 per pattern) in laboratory and greenhouse settings. Gripping required a peak normal force of 3.92 and 0.45 N, twisting produced a dominant torque of 19.20 2.10 Nmm about the pedicel axis with minimal truss disturbance, and pure lateral pulling alone was insufficient for reliable detachment. These measurements informed a novel fingertip-localised Finray gripper, with compliant TPU 95A Finray geometry restricted to the fruit-contact region and a rigid PLA body, emulating the dual-stiffness human fingertip. A Dynamixel XM430-W350 servomotor under current-based torque control applies the measured gripping force and a 45° wrist twist. Finite element analysis showed a maximum von Mises stress of 0.24 MPa under the 3.92 N load, below the bruising threshold of cherry tomato skin. Integrated on a UR5 arm and Clearpath Husky A200 base, the system was validated in NVIDIA Isaac Sim and in 50 autonomous trials at the SILAL commercial greenhouse, Abu Dhabi, UAE. The hybrid grip-twist-pull strategy achieved an 86.0% picking success rate, 2.3% fruit damage, and an 8.4 s cycle time per fruit, outperforming vacuum (66.3%), rotating (70.1%), clamping (76.0%), and suction-cutting (52.4%) end-effectors. These results demonstrate preliminary feasibility under the tested facility and cultivar conditions

</details>


### [166] [RESTA: Time-Scaling Augmentation for Data-Efficient Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18163)
*Yunpeng Jiang,Jianshu Hu,Han Fang,Zhiyuan Guan,Paul Weng,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RESTA通过轨迹反转与时间重采样增强数据，提升机器人模仿学习的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习依赖昂贵的演示数据，通常只覆盖单一任务方向与固定时间分辨率，数据效率低。

Method: 提出RESTA：轨迹反转增强，反向并重标记成对相反目标任务，复用演示；因接触操作非严格可逆，视为近似成对任务先验；时间步长增强，变化观测与动作采样步幅并以此条件化策略，支持固定或随机时间调度。

Result: 在16个成对双臂操作任务上，轨迹反转在低数据下尤为有效，时间步长增强在随机时间调度下提升成功率；每任务25和50条演示时平均成功率为78.4%和85.2%，匹配或超过用双倍演示训练的基线（77.3%和85.4%）。

Conclusion: RESTA能有效提升机器人模仿学习的数据效率。

Abstract: Robot manipulation policies trained through imitation learning commonly rely on costly demonstrations recorded in one task direction and at one temporal resolution. We propose Reversal and Stride-based Trajectory Augmentation (RESTA), which exploits time-scaling data augmentation to improve data efficiency through trajectory reversal and temporal resampling. Trajectory-reversal augmentation reverses and relabels demonstrations between paired tasks with opposite objectives, reusing recorded rollouts without additional environment interaction. Because contact-rich manipulation is not exactly reversible, we treat reversal as an approximate paired-task prior rather than a physical symmetry. Temporal-stride augmentation varies observation and action sampling strides and conditions the policy on the resulting offsets, enabling fixed or randomized temporal schedules. Experiments across 16 paired bimanual manipulation tasks show that trajectory reversal is particularly effective in the low-data regime and that temporal-stride augmentation improves success under randomized temporal schedules. With 25 and 50 independently collected demonstrations per task, RESTA achieves 78.4% and 85.2% average success, respectively, matching or exceeding baselines trained with twice as many demonstrations (77.3% and 85.4%). These results show that RESTA can improve data efficiency in robot imitation learning.

</details>


### [167] [Beyond Planning: Differentiable Control-in-the-loop End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18167)
*Zhidong Wang,Zirui Li,Zitong Shan,Zhiqi Mao,Qihang Huang,Zhan Chen,Jingsong Liang,Han Yu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DIVA 将可辨识的执行器级车辆模型嵌入端到端驾驶网络训练回路，构建从感知到车辆状态的可微链路，并用学习式控制解码器替代外部跟踪控制器，在 Bench2Drive 上取得更高驾驶分数与更平滑控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端自动驾驶虽能直接从原始传感器数据规划轨迹，但从规划到控制仍依赖人工调参的跟踪控制器，切断了从车辆运动回传到网络的梯度路径。该鸿沟源于车辆动力学不可微、专家标签采样粗糙导致监督混叠、高频率控制与低频率重规划之间的速率不匹配，以及训练与部署时的车辆模型差异。

Method: 提出 DIVA，将辨识得到的执行器级车辆模型嵌入端到端驾驶网络的训练回路，形成从感知到车辆状态的可微链；用学习式控制解码器替换外部控制器，并在滚动展开的车辆状态上监督，绕过混叠的控制输入标签；采用速率一致的控制输入参数化，协调异构采样率并支持异步执行，控制周期仅 3.8 ms；通过在线策略重辨识和在线扰动观测器分别处理训练期分布偏移与部署期模型失配。

Result: 在闭环 Bench2Drive 基准上，DIVA 取得 86.7 的驾驶分数，超过广泛采用的最先进方法 3.3 分，同时平均 jerk 近乎减半、刹车切换减少 64%，并在部署时存在显著车辆模型失配的情况下仍保持更优的路径跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 通过把可微、可辨识的车辆动力学纳入训练回路，并以学习式控制解码器与速率一致参数化打通规划到控制的可微链路，DIVA 有效提升了端到端自动驾驶的闭环性能、控制平滑性与部署鲁棒性。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has achieved remarkable progress by planning trajectories directly from raw sensor data. Yet the step from planning to control still relies on hand-tuned tracking controllers that block the gradient path from vehicle motion back to the network. This gap stems from non-differentiable vehicle dynamics, aliased supervision from coarsely sampled expert labels, rate mismatch between high-rate control and low-rate re-planning, and vehicle model discrepancies between training and deployment. This paper proposes DIVA (Differentiable Identified Vehicle dynamics for end-to-end Autonomous driving) to close this gap. An identified actuator-level vehicle model is embedded in the training loop of the end-to-end driving network, forming a differentiable chain from perception to vehicle states. A learning-based control decoder replaces the external controller and is supervised on the rolled-out vehicle states, bypassing the aliased control-input labels. A rate-consistent control-input parameterization reconciles the heterogeneous sampling rates and enables asynchronous execution with a 3.8 ms control-only cycle. On-policy re-identification and an online disturbance observer address training-time distribution shift and deployment-time model mismatch respectively. On the closed-loop Bench2Drive benchmark, DIVA achieves a driving score of 86.7, surpassing the widely adopted state-of-the-art approach by 3.3 points while nearly halving mean jerk and reducing brake switching by 64%, and maintains superior path tracking accuracy under significant vehicle model mismatch at deployment.

</details>


### [168] [OneDemo: Unified Policy Learning and Data Generation from a Single Demonstration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18188)
*Ying Qian,Jianshu Hu,Han Fang,Shuguang Li,Yutong Ban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OneDemo 提出用强化学习编辑单条成功演示，从而同时实现策略学习与可扩展演示生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从演示学习机器人策略受限于采集多样高质量专家数据的成本。将单条成功演示扩展为多样成功轨迹是理想替代方案，但现有增强方法依赖人工设计变换规则或复杂轨迹规划，需要大量任务特定工程与人力。

Method: 将演示编辑建模为结构化强化学习问题：编辑器从稀疏的任务成功奖励中学习两件事——何时在物体中心的轨迹片段之间切换，以及如何对每个片段进行几何适配。学习到的编辑器既可作为任务策略，也可作为可复用的演示生成器。

Result: 在15个Meta-World任务上平均53.7k次环境交互内达到92.7%成功率；在9个DexMimicGen任务上生成的成功演示平均成功率98.4%，用生成数据训练的BC-RNN策略在匹配数据规模下平均成功率达99.6%。

Conclusion: 学习编辑单条成功演示为策略学习与可扩展演示生成提供了结构化且有效的基座。

Abstract: Learning robot policies from demonstrations is often limited by the cost of collecting diverse, high-quality expert data. While an appealing alternative is to expand a single successful demonstration into a diverse set of successful trajectories, existing augmentation methods typically rely on manually designed transformation rules or complex trajectory planning, requiring substantial task-specific engineering and human effort. We introduce OneDemo, a reinforcement learning framework that learns to edit a single successful demonstration for new task configurations. We formulate demonstration editing as a structured reinforcement learning problem, in which an editor learns, from sparse task-success rewards, both when to transition between object-centric trajectory segments and how to geometrically adapt each segment. The learned editor serves both as a task policy and as a reusable demonstration generator. Across 15 Meta-World tasks, OneDemo reaches a 92.7% success rate within 53.7k environment interactions on average. Across 9 DexMimicGen tasks, it generates successful demonstrations with a 98.4% average success rate, and BC-RNN policies trained on the generated data achieve 99.6% average success at matched dataset scale. These results show that learning to edit a single successful demonstration provides a structured and effective basis for both policy learning and scalable demonstration generation.

</details>


### [169] [Cross-Embodiment Affordance Transfer for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18194)
*Sawera Yaseen,Serge Thill,Rekha Raja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出将人类标注的可操作区域转化为机器人可执行的可操作预测，条件化于机器人物理属性，实验证明人类标注不足，需以机器人条件化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作系统的可操作性数据多来自人类标注，反映的是人手而非特定机器人的物理约束，可能存在几何不可达或机械不可行的情况，这种不匹配很少被显式处理。

Method: 提出一个框架，将人类可操作性标注转化为以机器人物理属性为条件的机器人可执行预测。网络输入对象3D点云及编码机器人几何与约束的描述符，输出哪些表面区域对机器人物理可执行。训练中结合人类标注与仿真中多次采样接近试验的执行结果来确定可执行区域。

Result: 在家用物品数据集上，使用UR5e机械臂的两种平行夹爪配置进行评估，该框架优于未接收机器人信息的模型。

Conclusion: 可操作性预测必须条件化于机器人，仅靠人类标注不足以学习机器人可执行的可操作性表示。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation systems commonly learn object affordances from human-annotated datasets, which reflect interaction possibilities for a human hand rather than the physical constraints of a specific robot. For example, a surface region of an object identified as graspable by a human annotator may be geometrically unreachable or mechanically infeasible for a robot, yet this mismatch is rarely addressed explicitly. To tackle this challenge, we proposed a framework that translates human affordance annotations into robot-executable affordance predictions conditioned on the physical properties of the robot. The network predicts which surface regions of an object are physically executable by the robot, given a 3D point cloud of an object and a descriptor encoding the robot's geometry and constraints. The model was trained using human-annotated affordances together with robotic execution outcomes from multiple sampled approach trials in simulation, where execution outcomes determined which regions are executable for the robot. The evaluation was performed on a household objects dataset using two parallel-jaw gripper configurations on a UR5e robot arm. The framework outperformed a model that does not receive information about the robot. The experimental results demonstrated that affordance prediction must be conditioned on the robot and that human annotations alone are insufficient for learning robot-executable affordance representations.

</details>


### [170] [I2O2I-Nav: A Benchmark with a Modular Agent for Long-Horizon Indoor-to-Outdoor-to-Indoor Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18307)
*Zhengjie Shu,Wanyu Li,Di Xiao,Lingxin Jiang,Lai Jiang,Kaidi Hu,Xincheng Tang,Wenkang Hu,Haoran Wang,Jinchuan Zhang,Chang Chen,Wei Li,Jia Pan,Ruigang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出I2O2I-Nav基准与MODULAR-Agent，评估室内外连续导航组合能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务与配送机器人需跨室内外完成长时程导航，但现有具身导航基准多评估孤立阶段或部分跨域行程，难以衡量异构导航能力在连续执行中的组合表现。

Method: 构建物理启用的I2O2I-Nav基准，包含20个真实复合场景、超11.5万平方米、多楼栋多楼层，配对齐的3DGS渲染与碰撞几何；设三级评估（独立原语、两阶段组合、完整四阶段I2O2I任务），每阶段从前一阶段实际终点开始，并用阶段条件指标追踪完成与失败定位。提出MODULAR-Agent，通过完成验证、阶段交接与有界恢复协调冻结导航策略。

Result: 1,374回合评估显示连续组合困难。MODULAR-Agent在单个原语上成功率40.5%-76%，但完整I2O2I成功率仍低；相比使用相同底层策略的固定策略链，I2O与O2I成功率翻倍以上，全任务成功率从1.15%提升至4.02%。阶段分析表明其使更多任务到达最终物体导航阶段，而该阶段条件成功率仍低。

Conclusion: 长时程阶段执行与完成决策仍存在挑战，I2O2I连续组合导航是重要开放问题。

Abstract: Service and delivery robots often need to complete long-horizon navigation missions spanning indoor and outdoor environments. Existing embodied navigation benchmarks primarily evaluate isolated stages or partial cross-domain journeys, providing limited support for assessing how heterogeneous navigation capabilities compose under continuous execution. We introduce I2O2I-Nav, a physics-enabled benchmark of 20 real-world composite scenes spanning over 115,000 square meters across multiple buildings and floors, with aligned 3DGS rendering and collision geometry. Its three-level evaluation progresses from independent primitives to two-stage compositions and complete four-stage I2O2I missions. Each stage starts from its predecessor's actual endpoint, while stage-conditional metrics track completion and localize failures along the mission. We further propose the MODULAR-Agent that coordinates frozen navigation policies through completion verification, stage handoffs, and bounded recovery. Evaluation across 1,374 episodes highlights the difficulty of continuous composition. The MODULAR-Agent achieves 40.5-76% success on individual primitives, but full I2O2I success remains low. Compared with a fixed policy chain using the same underlying policies, it more than doubles success on both Indoor-to-Outdoor (I2O) and Outdoor-to-Indoor (O2I), while improving full-mission success from 1.15% to 4.02%. Stage-wise analysis shows that the MODULAR-Agent enables more missions to reach the final object-navigation stage, where conditional success remains low, revealing challenges in both long-horizon stage execution and completion decisions.

</details>


### [171] [CoSMO: Conformal Semantic Mapping for Open-World Safe Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18321)
*Shuqi Wang,Yiwei Zheng,Pingrui Zhang,Yue Gao,Xiang Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 CoSMO，将视觉语言模型嵌入转化为经过校准的覆盖集合与安全感知的障碍膨胀，在动态扩展的风险词表上实现开放世界语义安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全自主导航不仅需要几何避障，还需要语义感知。不同物体类别需要不同的安全间距，且相关类别集合会随机器人探索新环境而扩展。VLM 虽能提供丰富语义表示，但其置信度校准差，原始预测无法直接用于安全关键规划。现有共形预测方法假设固定标签集与平稳数据，难以适用于开放世界场景。

Method: 提出 CoSMO 语义安全建图框架，将 VLM 嵌入转化为经过校准的覆盖集合以及安全感知的障碍膨胀，并支持动态扩展的风险词表，即使存在分布偏移也能适用。

Result: 在 Matterport3D（MP3D）数据集上，CoSMO 相较纯几何基线将语义碰撞减少 61.6%，同时在延迟专家反馈下仍保持形式化覆盖保证。在四足机器人真实实验中，长时经验覆盖率达到 90.73%，目标为 90%。

Conclusion: CoSMO 通过共形语义建图在开放世界与分布偏移条件下实现了可验证覆盖保证与显著提升的语义安全导航性能。

Abstract: Safe autonomous navigation requires not only geometric obstacle avoidance but also semantic awareness. Different object categories demand different safety clearances, and the set of relevant categories may expand as the robot explores novel environments. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer rich semantic representations for building maps of unknown scenes, yet their confidence scores are poorly calibrated, making raw predictions unreliable for safety-critical planning. Conformal prediction (CP) can provide coverage guarantees, but existing methods assume a fixed label set and stationary data, limiting their use in open-world settings. We propose Conformal Semantic Mapping for Open-World Safe Robot Navigation (CoSMO), a semantic safety mapping framework that transforms VLM embeddings into calibrated coverage sets and safety-aware obstacle dilations over a dynamically expanding hazard vocabulary, even under distribution shift. Experiments on the Matterport3D (MP3D) dataset show that CoSMO reduces semantic collisions by 61.6% relative to a geometry-only baseline, while maintaining formal coverage guarantees even under delayed expert feedback. Real-world experiments on a quadruped robot further demonstrate generalization by achieving long-run empirical coverage of 90.73% against the 90% target. Project website: https://yangming911.github.io/cosmo-page/.

</details>


### [172] [Let the Mind Conceive, Let the Hand Achieve: Following Multimodal Instructions for Long-Horizon Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18352)
*Youpeng Wen,Gang Wang,Tianyu Huang,Weiwei Zhang,Zhijian Huang,Wei Chen,Xieyuanli Chen,Yunhui Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Orchestra 是一种将任务规划与执行管理从动作生成中分离的智能体框架，帮助机器人在动态场景中按正确顺序操作正确物体，在 RMBench-Compose 与真实任务上显著提升多步任务执行成功率与序列长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人执行多步指令时，需要在场景变化中按正确顺序操作正确物体，并保持对任务进度的感知、判断何时推进到下一步。现有仅用原子指令和短轨迹训练的机器人难以胜任长序列子任务。

Method: Orchestra 将完整多模态指令分解为有序子任务，跟踪已完成步骤，并在执行中更新相关物体与目的地位置；它维护包含当前子任务、空间 grounding 和近期观测的共享上下文。动作策略基于该上下文生成动作，另设单独训练的进度评估器判断何时推进到下一子任务。

Result: 在 RMBench 九个任务上平均成功率 68.22%，比 Mem-0 高 26.22 个百分点；在 RMBench-Compose 上平均成功长度（ASL）为 7.35 个子任务，是 HarnessVLA 的 2.46 倍；在四个真实多步任务上，相比使用基础 π_{0.5} 策略的 Orchestra，平均 ASL 提升 25.0%。

Conclusion: Orchestra 能帮助机器人执行更长的子任务序列，同时保持所要求的子任务顺序并作用于指定物体。代码与项目页面见 https://youpengwen.github.io/Orchestra。

Abstract: A robot following a multi-step instruction needs to act on the right objects in the right order, even as the scene changes. It must also maintain awareness of task progress and determine when to advance. To address these challenges, we introduce Orchestra, an agent harness that separates task planning and execution management from action generation. Orchestra decomposes a complete multimodal instruction into ordered subtasks, tracks completed steps, and updates the locations of relevant objects and destinations during execution. It maintains a shared context containing the active subtask, spatial grounding, and recent observations. An action policy uses this context to generate actions, while a separately trained progress evaluator estimates when to advance to the next subtask. To evaluate whether robots trained on atomic instructions and short trajectories can execute longer subtask sequences, we introduce a benchmark named RMBench-Compose. Orchestra achieves a mean success rate of $68.22\%$ across nine RMBench tasks, outperforming Mem-0 by $26.22$ percentage points. On RMBench-Compose, it achieves an Average Success Length (ASL) of $7.35$ subtasks, $2.46$ times that of HarnessVLA. Across four real-world multi-step tasks, it improves mean ASL by $25.0\%$ over Orchestra using the base $π_{0.5}$ policy. These results show that Orchestra helps robots carry out longer sequences of subtasks while preserving the requested subtask order and acting on the specified objects. Our code and project page are available at https://youpengwen.github.io/Orchestra.

</details>


### [173] [Visual Sevoing from Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18363)
*Nicolas Andreff,Bernard Espiau,Radu Horaud*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于直线的视觉伺服新方法，引入“双归一化Plücker坐标”表示，实现直线对齐及具有多种优良性质的控制律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于直线的视觉伺服中，如何恰当地表示直线并定义图像直线对齐概念存在困难，需要一种更适合视觉伺服的直线表示方法。

Method: 对主要直线表示进行理论与几何研究，定义新的“双归一化Plücker坐标”表示，并据此构造实现图像直线对齐的控制律。

Result: 该控制律具有旋转与平移部分解耦、运动方程可解析求逆、全局渐近稳定条件等性质；并在正交三面体的具体情形下通过仿真与实验验证。

Conclusion: 双归一化Plücker坐标为视觉伺服提供了合适的直线表示，所提控制律性质优良且经仿真与实验验证有效。

Abstract: In this work, we present a fundamentally new approach to visual servoing using lines. It is based on a theoretical and geometrical study of the main line representations which allowed us to define a new representation, the so-called binormalized Plucker coordinates. They are particularly well suited to visual servoing. Indeed, they allow the definition of a proper image line alignment concept. The control law which realizes such an alignment has moreover several properties: partial decoupling between rotation and translation, analytical inversion of the motion equations and global asymptotic stability conditions. This control law was validated both in simulation and experimentally in the specific case of an orthogonal trihedron.

</details>


### [174] [DR-VLA: Learning Human-Like Quadruped Navigation from Egocentric Web Videos via Domain-Invariant Scene Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18373)
*Jun Tian,Yingjie Niu,Ruo Chen,Hongyi Lin,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DR-VLA 通过域不变场景表示（DISR）和 Mixture-of-Transformers 框架，把人类第一视角网络视频中的导航经验迁移到四足机器人，并提出 EgoLynx 数据集与 QNS 评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人户外配送需理解复杂场景并以符合社会规范的方式导航，但机器人示范数据采集成本高、难以规模化；第一视角网络视频蕴含丰富人类导航经验，却因视角与运动差异难以迁移到机器人。

Method: 提出 DR-VLA，一个 Mixture-of-Transformers 框架，通过域不变场景表示（DISR）迁移人类导航经验，融合视觉语言理解、潜在世界建模与流匹配动作专家；构建 EgoLynx（含 10,000 小时 EgoLynx-Web 网络视频与 2,000 段 EgoLynx-Robot 遥操作机器人导航片段）；先在无动作标签的 EgoLynx-Web 上预训练理解与世界专家，再适配机器人观测并在 EgoLynx-Robot 上训练动作专家；提出 QNS 伪闭环评估指标。

Result: 在 EgoLynx-Robot 留出测试集上，QNS 相对最佳对比结果提升约 5%，开环最终朝向误差（FHE）降低 14.6%；消融显示网络视频预训练提升泛化能力且各关键组件均有贡献；作为未来预测目标，DISR 优于五种通用视觉表示，性能接近任务专用 3D 感知，且无需专门几何标签或点云；在 NAVSIM v1 上单独训练达到 92.1 PDMS。

Conclusion: 网络视频预训练与域不变场景表示能有效迁移人类导航经验，DR-VLA 在四足机器人导航上优于现有方法，并可能推广至自动驾驶等更广泛领域。

Abstract: Quadruped robots performing outdoor deliveries must interpret complex scenes, anticipate changes, and navigate in socially compliant ways. Robot demonstrations are costly to collect at the scale needed for generalization. Egocentric web videos offer abundant human navigation experience, but differences in viewpoint and motion make this experience difficult to transfer to robots. We introduce DR-VLA, a Mixture-of-Transformers framework that transfers human navigation experience through a domain-invariant scene representation (DISR). The framework combines vision-language understanding and latent world modeling with a flow-matching action expert. We introduce EgoLynx, comprising 10,000 hours of curated egocentric web video (EgoLynx-Web) and 2,000 clips of teleoperated robot navigation collected during real-world deliveries (EgoLynx-Robot). We pretrain the understanding and world experts on EgoLynx-Web without action labels, then adapt the model to robot observations and train the action expert on EgoLynx-Robot to predict executable trajectories. We also propose the Quadruped Navigation Score (QNS), a pseudo-closed-loop metric combining safety, instruction compliance, comfort, and progress. On the held-out EgoLynx-Robot test split, DR-VLA improves QNS by approximately 5% and reduces open-loop final heading error (FHE) by 14.6%, each relative to the best competing result for that metric. Ablations show that web-video pretraining improves generalization and that the model's key components contribute to performance. As a future-prediction target, DISR outperforms five generic visual representations and yields performance close to task-specific 3D perception without dedicated geometric labels or point clouds for target construction. Trained separately on NAVSIM v1 without web pretraining, DR-VLA achieves 92.1 PDMS, suggesting that the framework also applies to autonomous driving.

</details>


### [175] [Benchmarking Interaction-Oriented Aerial Mobility across Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18381)
*Zhibo Duan,Hanlin Kong,Tianran Ma,Yuze Wu,Yuxuan Xu,Kenghou Hoi,Changheng Xie,Xin Zhou,Jianzhe Gao,Fei Gao,Wenguan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出 CrossFly 基准，用于评估无人机在室内外异构环境中的长时程交互导向移动能力，并引入交互就绪率（IRR）指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空中操作基准多局限于单一环境，且交互任务中智能体常被初始化在目标附近，导致从导航到交互的过渡过程缺乏评估，无法反映异构环境下的连续移动与操作衔接能力。

Method: 构建 CrossFly 基准，包含超过 20 个重建场景下的 15K 条轨迹，统一仿真与评估框架，覆盖室内、室外及室内↔室外轨迹；提出交互就绪率（IRR）衡量终端导航状态是否适合下游交互，无需假设特定操作策略；并测试五种代表性空中 VLA 智能体及提出的 CrossFly-Agent。

Result: 室内↔室外设置比单一环境更具挑战性，在未见场景上最高成功率为 9.7%；下游操作案例研究进一步验证了交互导向到达对后续物理交互的相关性。

Conclusion: CrossFly 有望推动空中具身智能在异构环境中实现统一移动与物理交互。

Abstract: Aerial mobile manipulation requires unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to traverse heterogeneous environments such as indoor and outdoor domains, and connect navigation with subsequent physical interaction within the same trajectories. Existing aerial benchmarks are typically confined to single-environment settings, limiting evaluation of agents across continuous environmental transitions. When interaction is evaluated, agents are often initialized near the target, leaving the transition from navigation to interaction largely unassessed. This work introduces CrossFly, a benchmark for long-horizon interaction-oriented aerial mobility across heterogeneous environments. CrossFly contains 15K trajectories across more than 20 reconstructed scenes under a unified simulation and evaluation framework, covering Indoor, Outdoor, and Indoor$\leftrightarrow$Outdoor trajectories. It further introduces Interaction Readiness Rate (IRR) to assess whether terminal navigation states provide suitable conditions for downstream interaction without assuming a specific manipulation policy. Experiments with five representative aerial vision-language-action (VLA) agents and the proposed CrossFly-Agent show that Indoor$\leftrightarrow$Outdoor settings are more challenging than single-environment settings, with the highest success rate reaching 9.7% on unseen scenes. A downstream manipulation case study further validates the relevance of interaction-oriented arrival to subsequent physical interaction. We hope CrossFly helps advance aerial embodied intelligence toward unified mobility and physical interaction in heterogeneous environments.

</details>


### [176] [Intent-guided Adaptive Stiffening for Visuomotor Policies under Unknown Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18386)
*Francesco Cufino,Carmela Mariniello,Mario Selvaggio,Andreas Sochopoulos,Fabio Ruggiero*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种置于策略之下的残余刚度层，通过指令运动与实运动之差识别阻力并沿单一方向提升刚度，在两项真实任务上将成功率从52%提升到90%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拧瓶盖、沿表面推动零件等操作任务需沿高摩擦方向施力以克服阻力，而生成式视觉运动策略只预测运动、不预测所需力度，其能力上限受底层阻抗控制器约束。阻力超过柔性控制器在有限跟踪误差下可施加的力时任务不可达；且所需力度取决于观测无法揭示的接触状态，无法预先估计。

Method: 在未修改的策略下方加入残余刚度层。无需力传感，通过策略指令运动与机器人实际运动之间的偏差识别阻力；一个四模态监督器沿从策略自身动作块读出的单一方向提高刚度，在任务推进期间保持，任务完成后释放。该方法无需柔顺标签、无需重训练、无需重新采集示范数据。

Result: 在两项真实任务（拧开瓶盖；在非均匀摩擦下沿T型槽推动T型螺母）上，于六个阻力水平评估，其中两个水平的示范数据被刻意保留不用于训练。该层将成功率从52%提升至90%，同时相比同等阻力下一致刚度控制器，峰值偏轴力减少61%。

Conclusion: 通过在策略之下引入方向性残余刚度调节，可在不依赖力传感与重训练的前提下显著提升高摩擦操作任务的成功率，并降低偏轴力，且对所保留的未示范阻力水平具备一定泛化能力。

Abstract: Manipulation tasks such as unscrewing a cap or sliding a part in contact with a surface require imposing force along a high-friction direction: the robot must overcome a resistance rather than yield to it. Generative visuomotor policies predict the motion for such tasks but not the authority needed to realize it, and that authority is bounded by the impedance controller beneath them: when the resistance exceeds what a compliant controller can exert under a bounded tracking error, the task is unattainable however accurate the prediction is. The required amount cannot be anticipated either, since it depends on a contact state the observation does not reveal. In this paper, we propose a residual stiffening layer placed underneath an unmodified policy. Resistance is recognized, without force sensing, from the mismatch between the motion the policy commands and the motion the robot realizes, and a four-mode supervisor raises the stiffness along a single direction read off the policy's own action chunk, retains it while the task progresses and releases it afterwards. The layer needs no compliance labels, no retraining and no re-collected demonstrations. On two real tasks, unscrewing a bottle cap and pushing a T-nut along a T-slot groove against non-homogeneous friction, evaluated at six resistance levels of which two were withheld from the demonstrations, the layer raises the success rate from 52% to 90% while producing 61% less peak off-axis force than a uniformly stiff controller at the same resistance.

</details>


### [177] [CALM: Decentralized Control of Cooperative Aerial Manipulation with Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18425)
*Jiaxi Huo,Petre Ricioppo,Fausto Francesco Lizzio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CALM：面向多无人机协同运送篮筐并跟踪移动地面机器人的去中心化控制器，结合MAPPO参数共享MARL、目标估计、ESO、篮筐质量估计与预测式编队偏航前馈，实验误差低于所有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机通过共享载荷耦合，在风扰、载荷运动和带噪目标广播下协同空中操作困难，需提升跟踪精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出CALM去中心化控制器：参数共享MARL策略（MAPPO训练）+目标估计+每机扩展状态观测器ESO+篮筐质量估计+预测式编队偏航前馈（利用估计目标运动提前旋转编队，减小篮筐偏航滞后且不增加净指令力）+扰动条件化critic支持集中训练、去中心化执行。

Result: 在留出轨迹上，CALM平均位置与姿态误差低于所有基线，包括匹配机械臂基线（7.68 vs 10.95 cm，3.3 vs 4.7°）、缆索悬挂实现及两种模型控制器；在未见全信息路径退化下，与机械臂基线统计相当或更优。

Conclusion: CALM能在去中心化执行下有效提升协同空中操作在干扰与噪声条件下的跟踪性能，并具备对信息退化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cooperative aerial manipulation is difficult under wind, payload motion, and noisy goal broadcasts because multiple Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) are coupled through a shared payload. We present CALM (Cooperative Aerial Learned Manipulation), a decentralized controller for aerial manipulators carrying a basket while tracking a moving ground robot. CALM combines a parameter-shared Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) policy, trained using Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO), with goal estimation, per-UAV Extended State Observers (ESOs), basket-mass estimation, and anticipatory formation-yaw feedforward. The anticipatory formation-yaw feedforward uses estimated goal motion to rotate the formation before position error develops, reducing basket-yaw lag without adding net commanded force. A disturbance-conditioned critic supports centralized training while execution remains decentralized. Across held-out trajectories, CALM achieves lower mean position and orientation errors than all evaluated baselines, including the matched arm baseline (7.68 versus 10.95 cm and 3.3 versus 4.7$^\circ$), the cable-suspended realization, and two model-based controllers. Under unseen full-information-path degradation, CALM remains statistically comparable or superior to the arm baseline. Videos are available at https://petreric.github.io/CALM/.

</details>


### [178] [PACT: Posture-Aligned Co-Design Technique for Hand Configuration and Retargeting in Multi-Finger Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.18443)
*Koyo Sasazaki,Satoshi Funabashi,Pranav Ponnivalavan,Alexander Schmitz,Tetsuya Ogata,Shigeki Sugano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PACT通过联合优化17自由度机械手结构与姿态重定向器，提升拟人抓取与遥操作模仿学习性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作演示的保真度取决于机械手复现操作者姿态的能力，但姿态重定向规则通常在硬件定型后才确定，且常规手部设计常忽视人类抓取姿态。

Method: 基于人类抓取姿态的优化方法，联合优化17舵机机械手的机械参数（连杆长度、关节偏移、安装角、基座位置）与非线性姿态重定向器（线性人-机关节映射、逐关节姿态IK权重、阻尼）；以DexYCB数据集人类姿态约束全指对应（MCP、PIP、DIP、指尖），并受舵机干涉、装配间隙、平手突出约束。

Result: 制造PACT Hand并验证：运动学RMSE为3.9 mm（基线LEAP Hand为12.8 mm）；所有指尖在每种抓取姿态下均在10 mm内，基线仅21.6%姿态达标；相同逐关节力矩限制下举升2 kg（基线1 kg），纸杯变形为基线七分之一，接近人手水平；YCB日常物体抓取成功率86%（基线64%）；模仿学习策略在罐体取放任务新位置达75%成功率。

Conclusion: 结构-重定向协同优化可显著改善机械手的拟人抓取、负载与变形控制及模仿学习表现。

Abstract: The fidelity of teleoperated demonstrations used for imitation learning is determined by how faithfully a robot hand can replicate the operator's posture. The posture retargeting law that the robot will execute is typically selected only once the hardware has been finalized, and conventional hand designs often neglect human-like grasping postures. We introduce PACT, an optimization based on human grasping postures that jointly optimizes the mechanical parameters of a 17-servo robot hand (link lengths, joint offsets, mounting angles, and base placements) and its nonlinear posture retargeter (a linear human-to-robot joint map, per-joint posture-IK weights, and damping). The optimization enforces full-finger correspondence (MCP, PIP, DIP, and tip of each finger) to human poses from the DexYCB dataset, and is constrained by servo interference, assembly clearances, and flat-hand protrusion, so the resulting design can be physically assembled. We fabricate the PACT Hand and validate it through teleoperation experiments and an imitation-learning demonstration. The hand reproduces all human hand keypoints with a kinematic RMSE of 3.9 mm, compared to 12.8 mm for the LEAP Hand baseline, and keeps all fingertips within 10 mm on every grasping posture, while the baseline achieves this for only 21.6% of the poses.
